madhumatī tato ya *? māyāṃdīṃ carma bījakaṃ |
phaṭkāraṃ praṇavādyantaṃ śivaśakti *?vīkṛtaṃ ||

eṣā * * * *? vidyā ajavā prāṇadāyinī(?) |
pañcamī paramā vidyā siddhidā sā tadā bhavet ||

asyārthaḥ ||

* *? ai māyā hrīṃ kamalā śrīṃ vā * *? kūṭasya 
* *? jayet |
praṇavaṃ oṃ lakṣmī śrī vāk aiṃ manmathaṃ klīṃ bhuvaneśvarīṃ hrīṃ 
etenāyaṃ mantraḥ | aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ ka e? ī la hrīṃ |

17a)

oṃ śrīṁ aiṁ klīṁ hrīṁ hasakala hrīṃ || oṃ aiṁ śrīṃ klīṃ hrīṃ hakahala hrīṃ 
klīṃ eṃ klīṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ ka hasala hrīṃ hasa oṃ hrīṃ drīṃ hūṃ phaṭ oṃ 
hasahakalasa hrīṃ evaṃ kṛte pañca siddhā bhavatītyarthaḥ || 

aparañca pravakṣyāmi mantriṇāṃ durlabhaṃ paraṃ |
māyā śrī madanaścaiva śrīmāyā madanairapi ||

madanairmāyayā śrīśca śrīrmadanamāyayā |
māyayā madanaiḥ śrīśca kathitā ca tripañcikā ||

nāstyasyāḥ sadṛśī vidyā vidyate bhuvana traye |
bhairavyāśca mahātantre vidyā parama durlabhā ||

siddhāsunirmalā śāntā trailokya vaśakāriṇī |
pañcakūṭādi datvā japed vai pañca pañcamī ||

tena hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ | śrīṃ hrīṃ klīṃ | klīṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ | śrīṃ klīṃ hrīṃ | hrīṃ 
klīṃ śrīṃ evaṃ trīṇi trīṇi vījāni pratyekaṃ pañcakūṭādike datvā japet tadā 
pañcamī siddhā bhavati || 

anyāsāṃ sarvavidyānāṃ prāṇaṃ vakṣyāmi sāmprataṃ | 
ramā māyā haṃsabījaṃ vāgbhavādye sudurlabhaṃ ||

śaktyantehaṃ sa vījantu mahāmāyā ramā tathā |
etairyuktena niyataṃ vidyā japanamā caret ||

asyārthaḥ ||

ramā śrīṃ māyā hrīṃ haṃsabījaṃ haṃsaḥ vāgbhava kūṭasyādau dadyāt | śakti 
kūṭānte tu haṃsabījaṃ haṃsaḥ mahāmāyā hrīṃ ramā śrīṃ etairvījaiḥ 
pūrvāparasthaiḥ sarvavidyā prabodho bhavati || 

ramābījena yuktena dharmamokṣa sukhapradā |
hrīṃkāreṇa putradā nityā nānārtha saṃcaya pradā ||

17b)

haṃsabīje naktena nānākāma pradāyinī |
mokṣantu praṇavodyante niścitaṃ durlabhaṃ paraṃ ||

etacca kathitaṃ sāraṃ parañca kathayāmyatha |
etena śrīṃ hrīṃ haṃsa ityādau vīja trayaṃ kṛtvā(?) 
sarvavidyāyāḥ śakti kūṭānte haṃsaḥ krīṃ śrīṃ iyaṃ sarvaśrīvidyāsudīpinī || 

tāra māyā ramā kāma vāgbhavairvāgbhavaṃ japet |
tadeva viparītantu kāmabījaṃ nabhonvitaṃ ||

sa tāraṃ vāgbhavaṃ kāma namā māyā samanvitaṃ |
caitanyaṃ kathitaṃ sāraṃ parañca kathayāmyatha ||

asyārthaḥ ||

toya oṃ māyā hrīṃ ramā śrīṃ kāmaḥ klīṃ vāgbhavaḥ aiṃ tadeva viparītaṃ 
vāgbhavādyaṃ kṛtvā nabhonvitaṃ hakāra yuktaṃ kāmarājamityārthaḥ | tāra oṃ 
vāgbhavaṃ aiṁ kāmaḥ klīṃ ramā śrīṃ māyā hrīṃ || tena oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ 
eṃ vāgbhava kūṭa aiṃ klīṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ oṃ kāmakūṭa oṃ aiṃ klīṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ 
śaktikūṭa iyañca śrīvidyā dīpinī || aparañca ||

vāṅmāyā kamalā bījaṃ vāgbhavādya niyojayet |
vedādyaṃ lakṣmi vāgvīje manmathaṃ tadanantaraṃ ||

māyā bījaṃ tato datvā vāgbhavākhyaṃ samuccaret | 
ādyante tāra māyāntu madhye śrībījasaṃyutaṃ ||

tārasyānte vāgbhavantu kāmabījaṃ tathā sthitaṃ |
evametat samuccārya kāmarājaṃ samuccaret ||

etasyā eva vidyāyā madhye kāmaṃ niyojayet |
manmathādisthamadharaṃ śrībījaṃ manmathāntaraṃ ||

evaṃ krameṇa saṃyojya śaktikūṭaṃ tatoccaret(?) |
iyañca dīpanī proktā sarvatantreṣu gopitā ||

18a)

tena aiṁ hrīṃ śrīṃ eṃ oṃ śrīṃ eṃ klīṃ hrīṃ vāgbhave | oṃ eṃ śrīṃ klīṃ hrīṃ 
kāmakūṭe | oṃ aiṃ klīṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ śaktikūṭe iyañca dīpinī || aparañca || 

tāramāyāhaṃ sabījaṃ vāgbhavādye niyojayet |
kāmabījantataḥ paścād brahmabījaṃ tadantike ||

śaktyante haṃsabījantu prajapet sādhakottamaḥ |
iyañca dīpinī vidyā parañca kathayāmyatha ||

tena oṃ hrīṃ haṃsaḥ vāgbhava kāmakūṭa oṃ śaktikūṭa haṃsaḥ iyaṃ ca 
śrīvidyā dīpinī | athavā praṇavaṃ lakṣmīṃ manmathaṃ vāgbhavaṃ parāṃ | 
etajjaptvā vāgbhavākhyamuccaret sādhakottamaḥ || praṇavañcaiva vāgbījaṃ 
ramāmanmatha māyayā | 

evameva tadā japtvā kāmabījaṃ samuccaret |
praṇavañcādharaṃ kāmaṃ ramāñca bhuvaneśvarīṃ ||

evaṃ krameṇa saṃyojyaṃ śaktikūṭaṃ samuccaret |
etacca kathitaṃ sāraṃ sarvasiddhi pradāyakaṃ ||

etāguptatamā vidyāḥ sarvāścaitanya kārikāḥ |
āsāmekatamāṃ datvā japed vidyāṃ samāhitaḥ ||

japādau tu japasyānte guruvākyamanukramāt |

tena oṃ śrīṃ klīṃ aiṃ hrīṃ vāgbhave oṃ aiṃ klīṃ hrīṃ kāmakūṭe | oṃ aiṃ klīṃ 
śrīṃ hrīṃ śaktikūṭe iyaṃ ca śrīvidyā dīpinī evañca āsāmanyatamaṃ yathokta 
prakāreṇa datvā japārambha sakalavāraṃ japānte sakalavāraṃ 
(sakalaprakāremityarthaḥ) japediti saṃkṣepaḥ | aparañca ||

vāmakeśyāśca vidyāyā viśeṣaḥ punarucyate |
vāgbhave kāmarāje ca śaktikūṭe punaḥ kramāt ||

kāmabīja krameṇaiva dīpanyādi samīritaṃ (bījatrayeṇaiveti kecit pāṭhaḥ) |

18b)

evañca vāgbhavādi kūṭe | yadi kāmabījārope vāmakeśvaryā dīpanaṃ 
bhavatītyarthaḥ ||

śrīramāramaṇadevaśarmaṇānirmite nigamasāranirṇaye | cchinna *? lita 
suṣuptivācakaḥ pūrvamādyapaṭalaḥ tṛtīyakaḥ ||


iti yadyapi nānāvidhāni sādhano pāyāni likhitāni tathāpi bhāvena vinā mantro na 
siddhantīti bhāvacūḍāmaṇirlikhyate ||

tatra cūḍāmaṇau || 

bhairava uvāca ||

mātardevi mahāmāye vandhamokṣapravartini |
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi gurukramamanuttamaṃ ||

devyuvāca ||

gurukrama *? vahudhā mantravistara gauravāt |
kālā?nāmapyanādityāt kathaṃ tat kathayāmite ||

naiva jñātvā gurukulaṃ naṣṭa(?) mārgo bhaviṣyati |
naṣṭa? mārgānmahāvidye na tādṛk phalagocare ||

gurūṇāṃ śiṣya bhūtānāṃ nāsti cet saṃtati kramaḥ |
tantramantryaśca vidyāśca nisphalā nātra saṃśayaḥ ||

viṃśatiṃ puruṣān vāpi navasupta trayopi vā |
jñānato guruvaṃśānāṃ śiṣyaḥ syānnaṣṭasaṃtati ||

svavaṃśā dadhikaṃ jñeyaṃ guruvaṃśa śubhāśubhaṃ |
janakādadhikaṃ jñeyaṃ mantradasya maheśvara ||

tasmāt sarvatra deveśa * *? yā cchṛṇutān gurun |
ādau sarvatra deveśa mantradaḥ paramo guruḥ ||

parāpara gurusvaṃ hi parameṣṭhirahaṃ tataḥ |
sarvatantreṣu vidyāsu svayaṃ prācyā?ta rūpiṇī ||

tataḥ puruṣa(?) rūpaśca(?) tasmāt svaguru santatiṃ |

19a)

te naivāhaṃ madaṃśāśca madbhaktāśca viśeṣataḥ |
sarva tantreṣu prabhavaḥ sarvatraśudāyakaḥ ||

śivaḥścaivāśca ye viprāstepi sarvatra deśikāḥ |
gaṇeśogāṇapatyāśca gaṇadīkṣā prabhurmataḥ ||

śūraḥ śaurāstathā vatsa viṣṇurviṣṇu parāyaṇāḥ |
vidyaugha guravo deva siddhaugha guravastathā ||

mānavaughāḥ samāsena kathayāmi tavāgrataḥ |
sarvatantreṣu mantreṣu proktāmantra catuṣṭayaṃ ||

tatrādau kathitā devī tasyāḥ śṛṇu gurukramaṃ |
mahādevī mahākāla tripuraścaiva bhairavaḥ ||

divyaugha guravaḥ proktā niddhaughān kathayāmite |
brahmānandaḥ pūrṇadevaścalacittaścalācalaḥ ||

kumaraḥ krodhanaścaiva varadaḥ smadīyanaḥ |
māyā māyāvatī caiva mānavaughān śṛṇu priye ||

vimalaḥ kakaraścaiva bhīmasenaḥ sudhākaraḥ |
mīno gorakṣakaścaiva bhojadevaḥ prajāpatiḥ ||

mūladevoranti devo vighneśvara hutāśanau |
samayānandaḥ sahajaḥ vijñeyā guravaḥ smṛtāḥ ||

atha tārā gurū navakṣye dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalapradān |
ūrddhvakeśo vyomakeśo nīlakaṇṭho vṛdhvajaḥ ||

divyaughāḥ siddhidā vatsa śṛṇuṣvā vahitāsmanā |
vaśiṣṭhaḥ kūrmanāthaśca nīlanātho maheśvaraḥ ||

harinātho mānavaughā nathavakṣyāmi sadgurūn |
tārāvatī bhānumatī jayāvidyā mahodarī ||

sukhānandaḥ parānandaḥ pārijātaḥ kuleśvaraḥ |

19b)

virūpākṣo pheravī ca kathitaṃ tāriṇī kulaṃ ||

siddhaughe mānavaughe ca divyaughe parameśvaraḥ |
akṣyobhya sarvamūrddhanyastri mūrtināmarūpadhṛk ||

atha svapnāvatī devān kathayāmi tava priye |
parānandaśca pūrṇeśī devadevī janārdanaḥ ||

pūrṇeśī sundarī viṣamāhutīrati(?) kāmike |
purastyo bhāvasampanno vīro karṣaṇakarṣiṇaḥ ||

siddhaughe ca satī devī bharavaścandraśekharaḥ || mā ||

mānavaughe satāṃ devī kṣamāpūrṇā kalāvatī |
maheśo viṣṇunāmā ca gaṃgeśaḥ smaranāyakaḥ ||

kalānandaḥ kumārī ca sarvāśca saṃskṛtāḥ striyaḥ |
vidagdhāḥ sarvajātīnāṃ sarvāvasthā gatāśca vā ||

vālā vā yauvanonmattā niṣpatirvāsa putrikā |
gurubhāvaṃ parityajya yoṣitsu yadi sādhakaḥ ||

svapnāvatī yajed vācā manasā vapuṣāpi ca |
sarvadoṣasamāyuktastyājyasau kulayāṃśalaḥ ||

tasmāt sarva prayatnena vipriyaṃ parayoṣiti |
nijāsvapi cano kārya madaṃśā yoṣito yataḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmite vatsa ṣoḍaśīnāṃ guru kramaṃ |
ānandanātha? devaśca paraprakāśakastathā ||

tataḥ paraśivo devaḥ paraśaktistataḥ paraṃ |
kauleśvaraḥ śakti devā'kuleśānaśca * *? kaḥ ||

siddhaughā sa kathayiṣyāmi bhogaḥ klīṅgaśca bhairavaḥ |
samayo deva sahayau mānavaughān śṛṇu priye ||

gagaṇo viṣṇu vimalau sahajo bhavanastathā |
nīlaḥ khādu priyaḥ paścāt kathitā gurusattamāḥ ||

20a)

bhairava uvāca ||

vidyā catuṣṭayī proktā durgayā sadṛśādhara |
tāṃ vihāyakaṃ tārā proktā paramadurlabhā ||

bhairavyuvāca ||

sādhupṛṣṭhaṃ tvayā vatsa sarvaprāṇaiḥ sukhāvaha |
yathā tārā tathā durgā viṣmayo nāsti yadyapi ||

tathāpi tatprakāśāya bhayaṃ jātaṃ pade pade |
śṛṇuvatsa mahādeva gūrun durgāmanud bhavān ||

paramātmā parānandaḥ parameṣṭhī śubhodayaḥ |
kṛṣṇaḥ kolaḥ kalānātho divyaughā bhairavādikāḥ ||

nāradaḥ kaśyapaḥ śambhu bhārgavaḥ kulakaulikaḥ |
ete pañca mahādeva siddhaughāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||

rudrācāryaḥ kṣamācāryaḥ pavanāśana saṃjñakaḥ |
kumārīśaḥ śaktidharo ghanānanda prakāśakaḥ ||

hariśarmāviṣṇuśarmā dattātrayaḥ priyamvadaḥ |
vakulāśākinī devī caryānāthaḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||

lopāmudrā gurūn vakṣye praśaṅgācchṛṇu bhairava |
ādau pareśvaro devaḥ parameśī tataḥ paraṃ ||

mitrīśaścaiva ṣaṣṭhīśa uḍḍīśo divya sādhakāḥ |
caryānātho lupo mudrā agastāḥ kāladīpanaḥ ||

mānavaughāṃstato vakṣye dharmācāryastataḥ paraṃ |
muktakeśīśvaro deva dīpakotha eva ca ||

viṣṇuḥ prabhākaraścaiva manojastadanantaraṃ |
kalyāṇa devaratna devau śrīrāmānanda devakaḥ ||

ānandanātha śabdāntā guravaḥ sarvasiddhidāḥ |
athavā sarvaśākteṣu guravaḥ pūrvasūcitāḥ ||

20b)

kulacūḍāmaṇau deva śṛṇu deva maheśvaraḥ |
yeṣu yeṣu ca tantreṣu ye ye ṛṣi gaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ ||

te te pūjyāḥ saparyādau saṃkṣepād gaditaṃ mayā |
ajñānād gurunāmnāṃ vai guru tritaya sarvayet ||

catuṣṭayaṃ vā? saṅkoco na kāryaśca tataḥ paraṃ |
gurūn paragurūṃścaiva parāpara gurūṃstataḥ ||

parameṣṭhi gurūṃścaiva kathitā guravastataḥ |
gurūpūjāṃ vinā vatsa yadi pūjāṃ samācaret ||

tadoṣa samane vatsa kūlapūjāṃ samācaret |
viguṇaṃ yacca? yad vā syāt saguṇaṃ kula pūjanāt ||

kulā * * karaṃ cet syāt kutaḥ prokṣaṇa mārjanaṃ |
kva snānaṃ kva ca vāk śuddhiḥ kva ca nyāsa viśodhanaṃ ||

dīkṣā yantraḥ kulīnaścet kulamvā vaṭukeśvaraḥ | 
tadgeha gurumānīya tvat pāda smaraṇaṃ caret ||

na jānāmi gurornāma vaṃśaśuddhiṃ maheśvari | 
svasvādāmbujareṇunākanayo de?hi dāsatāṃ ||

iti stavena mantratyu(?) nijaṅkala(?) guruṃ yadi | 
tannāmnā pūjayed vatsa tena pūjā gurorbhavet ||

svapnāvatyāstu pūjāyāṃ kularūpaṃ guruṃ smaret |
kulaṃ(?) ya?dhā gurustatra na tathā kulasādhakaḥ ||

guru kra?mañca kathitaṃ na jñātvā ku * *? laḥ |
na deyaṃ yatra kutrāpi yogya ṣaṣṭhe ca cambuke ||

paśoragre nadyaiveṣāṃ nāmagrāhyaṃ kuleśvara |
vaiṣṇave śakti tantre vā gāṇapatya'thavā punaḥ || 

nijaṃ guruṃ tadā dhyātvā tato guru catuṣṭayaṃ |

21a)

pūjayitvā japed devi na ca saṅkoca mācaret |

bhairava uvāca ||

samayācāra saṃketo bhāvasaṃketameva ca |
sūcitantu mahādevī kathayasvānukampayā ||

sarvatantreṣu vidyāsu bhāvasaṅketameva hi |
tathāpi śakti tantreṣu viśeṣāt sarvasiddhidaṃ ||

bhāvastu trividho deva divya vīra paśu kramāt |
guravastu tridhā cātra tathaiva mantra devatāḥ ||

ādya bhāvo mahādeva śreyān sarvasamṛddhidaḥ |
dvitīyo madhyamaścaiva tṛtīyaḥ sarvaninditaḥ ||

vahujapāt tathā homāt kāya kleśādi vistaraiḥ |
na bhāve na vinā deva yantra mantrāḥ phalapradāḥ ||

kiṃ vīrasādhanairdeva (* *?) kimvādṛṣṭi (* 
*? dṛṣṭi) kulākulaiḥ |
kiṃ pīṭha pūjane naiva kiṃ kanyā bhojanādibhiḥ ||

svayoṣit prīti dānena kiṃ pareṣāntathaiva ca |
kiṃ jitendriya bhāvena kiṃ kulācāra karmaṇā ||

yadi bhāva viśuddhātmā nasyā bhāva parāyaṇaḥ |
bhāvena labhate muktiṃ bhāvena kulavarddhanaṃ ||

bhāvena kula gotra (kularci?) vṛddhiḥ syād bhāvenakāya śodhanaṃ 
|
kiṃ nyāsa vistareṇaiva kiṃ bhūtaśuddhi vistaraiḥ ||

kim vṛthā pūjane naiva yadibhāvo na jāyate |
kenonā pūjyate vidyā na ca kena ca japyate ||

phalā bhāvastu deveśa bhāvābhāvāt prajāyate |
etad rahasya kathane katake (* * *?) samamānasaṃ ||

trilokī matsarī prāyaṃ sarvatra tatsamāgamaḥ |

21b)

nā vīkṣya nirjanaṃ deva kathaṃ tat kathayāmi te |

bhairava uvāca ||

tvat prasādān mayā devi vidyā svapna vivodhinī |
sarvamāyā prapañcānāṃ dīkṣādakṣā mamāgraṇīḥ ||

vidyate devi sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ svāpakāraṇaṃ |
karomi kathayatvaṃ māṃ yadi putratvamāgataḥ ||

devyuvāca ||

prathamaṃ divya bhāvastu kathyate śṛṇu sādaraṃ |
yadvarṇā devatā yatra tat tejaḥ puñjapuritaṃ ||

tejomayaṃ jagatsarvaṃ vibhāvya mūrti kalpanaṃ |
tat tanmūrti mayairmantraiḥ svenā svenaiva vā punaḥ ||

ātmānaṃ tanmayaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvaṃ bhāvantathaiva ca |
tatsarvaṃ yogibhirddhyātvā cintaye śuddha mānasaḥ ||

aśeṣakula sampanno nānā jāti samudbhavāḥ |
nānādeśodbhavā vāpi guṇalāvanyasaṃyutāḥ ||

dvitīya vatsarādūrddhaṃ yāvat syād daśamāvadhi |
tāvat kumārī vijñeyā yantra mantra phalapradā ||

kumārī pūjanāccaiva kumārī bhojanādadi |
eka dvitryādi bījānāṃ phaladā nātra saṃśayaḥ ||

tābhyaḥ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ vāpi anulepādikaṃ tathā |
vālapriyaṃ ca yad dravyaṃ datvā natvā ca pūjitaḥ ||

dhyātvā tadaṅge svātmānaṃ vālabhāva viceṣṭitaḥ |
tābhiḥ priya kathālāpa krīḍākautūhalādikaṃ ||

yathā tathā tat priya kṛt kṛtvā siddhīśvaro bhavet |
tasmāt ṣoḍaśa paryantaṃ bhavatīti pracakṣyate ||

tatra bhāva prakāśaścet sa bhāvaḥ paramo mataḥ |
caraṇān mūla paryantaṃ pītvāpi ca punaḥ pipet ||

22a)

tasyānakha śaśi jyotsnā khaṇḍitāntastamo bhavet |
kamaṭhī kharparākārā prapadāñcala śobhayā ||

snigdhāntaḥ karaṇo vīro nirvikāraḥ sadā bhavet |
svavṛttajānunā cāru jaṃghoruje ghanāśrayaiḥ ||

nābhiromā valīvakṣaḥ sthalaiḥ pīnapayodharaiḥ |
grīvā nayana paryantaḥ keśāgraiḥ pravarasmitaiḥ ||

yāvan madhu madāpūrṇaṃ nadīnaiḥ bhāva lolitaiḥ |
snigdhendriyaśca saṃtuṣṭaḥ caturvarga samāśriyaḥ (pradā payaḥ?) 
||

nivṛtti kāle punastasyā bhāvapūrṇāmṛtairnijaṃ |
mukhaṃ vindu vadākāraṃ tadadhaḥ kuca yuṣmakaṃ ||

sarvavidyāmṛtāpūrṇaṃ sarvavāgvibhavapradaṃ |
sarvārtha sādhakaṃ deva sarvarañjana kārakaṃ ||

tadadhaḥ saparārddhañca supariskṛtamaṇḍalaṃ |
sarvadevādi bhūtantat sarvadeva namaskṛtaṃ ||

sarvā hlādana sampūrṇaṃ sarvarañjana kārakaṃ |
sarvārthasādhakaṃ deva sarvavaśya pravartakaṃ ||

etat kāmakalā dhyānaṃ sugopyaṃ sādhakottamaiḥ |
dhyātvā spṛṣṭvātha vā dṛṣṭvā dhyātvā kāmakalānvitaḥ ||

kṣaṇaṃ tatva vimarṣeṇa jāyate bhāvapaṃ?cayaḥ |
kuleṣu tatkalārūpamakule paricintya ca ||

kulīno jāyate yasmāt kathaṃ tat kathayāmi te |
kule sākṣād yatastatvaṃ svayaṃ cintati tatvataḥ ||

tena tatkulaśāstrajñaiḥ pūjanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
kuladevī vānma devī niścalā yasya vidyate ||

sa dhanyaḥ puruṣā loke niścayodaya eva saḥ |
yatkule kuladīkṣā tu na syāt pāpī sa eva hi ||

adīkṣita kulāsaṃgāt siddhihāniḥ prajāyate |
tatkathā miśraṇaṃ cet syāt tat talpa gamanaṃ yadi ||

sukulīnaḥ kathaṃ devi pūjayet parameśvarī |
gandharūpānurūpeṇa nāmakuryānnijecchayā ||

mātāpitṛ kṛtaṃ vā?me varjanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
parayo vādhit(?) sajñeyā nijā śrīprīti varddhinī ||

āgate svāgataṃ kuryāllīlā yogāt svalīlayā |
talvādutthāya hastāgre dhṛtvā samveśayet tataḥ ||

*? sabhāge samāsīnāṃ suvarṇāṃ kāmarūpiṇīṃ |
dhyātvā kāmakalāṃ tatra viṃdvādi pūrṇavigrahāṃ ||

keśavinyāsa tilakaṃ bhārakeyūra yāvakaṃ |
sarva kalāmvidhā yādau kuladravyā niyojayet ||

hetu yuktaṃ samāmvu?laṃ datvā bhuktvā tu sādhakaḥ |
pṛcchat kulakathāṃ tasyāṃ laukikālaulikādikīṃ ||

ma pañcodbhava puṣpāni(?) kīrtitāni kulādiṣu |
na karoti kulābhijñaḥ siddhāpi naśyati dhruvaṃ ||

dṛṣṭvā doṣādikaṃ tatra snehādiskṛta(?) śikṣayā |
bhāṣayenneka bhūktya vaikṛte tu nisphalaṃ bhavet ||

kulaśāstra rahasyantu kulamukhaṃ yataḥ prabho |
varaṃkulamanostyā govalaṃ kulagurorapi ||

na tyāga yogyaṃ svakulaṃ dṛṣṭadhāṣādapi(?) priyaṃ |
kulavṛkṣa sthito sohaṃ svakulaṃ poṣayāmyahaṃ ||

tena sarva prayatnena rakṣaṇīyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
svakule kula vāhulyaṃ yadi syād bhāgya yogataḥ ||

23a)

samarūpaṃ vidhātavyaṃ vaiparītyamvarityajet |
pṛthak sthānaṃ pṛthag dhyānaṃ pṛthak pūjā pṛthak stutiḥ ||

na kartvavyā prayatnena kimebhirvahu jalpitaiḥ |
kule tiṣṭhāmi deveśa nātrakāryā vicāraṇā ||

tāsāṃ nisvāsa yogena tadeśaścaiva naśyati |
pramodādamṛta snānaṃ mama syān nātra saṃśayaḥ ||

kulajāhaṃ mahāyuddhe vīrāsphālananādinī |
na doṣamanu gacchāmi pālayāmi yato jagat ||

maukharyaṃ parividveṣaḥ kaṭūkti kalahādikaṃ |
nīcādi gamanāccaiva nāhamīṣannarā priyā ||

brahmāṇḍabhāṇḍa sambhedāt tena jīve sa siddhi yuk |
vinākulena matsiddhirna kutra pratipadyate ||

bhairava uvāca ||

caturthāśramiṇāṃ devi mokṣadātvaṃ sadā kathaṃ |
kulabhāva virodhi tvāt saṃśayaṃ manadūraya ||

devyuvāca ||

mokṣadvārastu vahudhā yadyasti kulabhairava |
kevalaṃ kulayogena prasīdāmi na saṃśayaḥ ||

caturthāśramiṇāṃ deva avadhūta śramo mahān |
tatrāhaṃ kula yogena prasannāmokṣadāyiṇī ||

yadi na syāstathā vatsa śṛṇu tat kathayāmi te |
jñānābhāve samutpanne samprārthya nijakaulikīṃ ||

tasmānnayāmi muktiḥ syāt tāṃ saṃpūjya kulāntare |
kuṇḍalī śakti vivare tadā yogyaṃ samabhyaset ||

nirdvandvo nirahaṃkāraḥ śuddhanāḍī gaṇovratī |
mṛdvāsane samāsīnaḥ khagasaṃyamane rataḥ ||

meludaṇḍa vahiḥ pārśve candrasūryātmike śiva |

23b)

madhye suṣumnā sandiṣṭā tanmadhye citrarūpiṇī |
te naiva grathitaṃ padmaṃ mūlādi padmapaṃcakaṃ ||

kālikākālarūpeṇa ḍākinyādyavalambitaṃ |
mūladvāraṃ samācchādya sthitā sā nāgarūpiṇī ||

vāyunābhidya tadvaktraṃ dṛḍhavidrāvayet tataḥ |
tacchvāso jvarakāmasya śikhāyāti samujvalā ||

praticakraṃ prabuddhāsau jīvātmānaṃ pare kule |
niyojayati tadyogādyātyeya mamṛtaṃ ciraṃ ||

vyomaprodbhidya nāsāntaṃ gatau candradivākarau |
adhovaktraṃ mahāpadmaṃ kroḍīkṛtya suṣumnayā ||

vyāghuṣṭhānāsikā madhyaṃ gatā citrāntasaṅgatā |
ūrddhaṃ bhitvā tu liṅgamvai itaraṃ puṣkarantataḥ ||

vahiratna mano kāṃkhinī * * mokṣadāyinī |
jīvanā dṛṣṭasaṃruddhā sthitā sā purasannidhau ||

etad bhedaṃ vijānāti bhitvā yāti paraṃ padaṃ |
adṛṣṭa dhātu jīvāsau vahiryāti dine dine ||

dinaśāṅguli mānena tadarddhañcoparāsataḥ |
dviguṇaṃ ratikālasyāntriguṇaṃ bhojanād vahi ||

arddha ūrddhaṃ vahed devistridinaṃ yadi bhairavaḥ |
nūnamvā tadadhaḥ prāṇaḥ śarīraṃ parimuñcati ||

śarīrasamatāṃ nītvā nūnamvā vahukeśvara |
kumbhayitvā adho vāyuṃ kuṇḍalīmukhavarnmani ||

yojayitvā tato jīvaṃ nītvānādāvasānakaṃ |
gamāgamaṃ kārayitvā sivo? bhavati mānavaḥ ||

pīyate khādyate yat tat tatsarvaṃ kuṇḍalī mukhe |
hutvā siddhimavāpnoti na sa vandhena vadhyate ||

bhitvākāryā na ca svārthaṃ kuṇḍalīnyāḥ kṛte punaḥ |

24a)

re mātardehi me bhikṣāṃ kuṇḍalīṃ tarpayāmyahaṃ |
avadhūtāśrame sthitvā bhairavānanda tatparaḥ ||

bhairavohaṃ na cānyasmin na devo matparaḥ kvacit |
mantra tantrārjanaṃ sarvaṃ mayijātaṃ na cānyathā ||

śivohaṃ bhairavānando mattohaṃ kulanāyakaḥ |
raktacandana digdhāṅgo raktakaupīnabhūṣitaḥ ||

raktamālyāmvaradharo hetu yuktaḥ sadā bhavet |
evaṃ bhikṣāṃ prajan bhikṣurbhairavācāra tatparaḥ ||

kṣīyate na ca pāpena vadhyate na ca jantunā |
yatra dveṣe dvayaṃ yāti sa deśaḥ kṣiti nāyakaḥ ||

atra nindā parā ye tu tena paśyanti kṣaṇāt prabho |
tasmād yatnāt pūjitavyo na tiraskārya eva ca ||

yatrecchā vidyate tasya tatkāryaṃ priyamapriyaṃ |
tadanye devadeveśa vilopācāra vistaraṃ ||

pūjayanti mahādevīṃ kulāṅga dṛṣṭi mātrataḥ |
puṣpaṃ gandhañca tāmbūlaṃ naivyedyaṃ yatra tad bhavet ||

manasā tatsamutsṛjya vāhyato'kuluvāriṇā |
ātmane tat māyojya devī rūpaḥ kuleśvara ||

na pūjānāpi tanyāso na niṣṭhā na vratādikaṃ |
pūrṇohaṃ bhairavaścāhaṃ nityānandohamavyayaḥ ||

nirañjana svarūpohaṃ nirākārastvahaṃ prabhuḥ |
sarvaśāstrādi yuktohaṃ sarvamantrārtha pāragaḥ ||

nāsmāt parataro devo na jātā na janiṣyati |
ānandarūpavān bhūtvā sarveṣāṃ priyakārakaḥ ||

muktadurgandha matsyādityājyaṃ divya vali priyaiḥ |

24b)

nānāsugandhisaṃyuktaṃ māṃsādi bhojanaṃ caret |
sudhandhāmalatailādyairnānāṅgaṃ rāgavistaraiḥ ||

snānaṃ kāryañcāti śuddhiṃ sūkṣmaṃ vā sobhidhāpayet |
sugandhipuṣpamālābhiḥ svarṇālaṅkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ ||

snigdhaṃ svamadhuraṃ dehaṃ kāmarūpaṃ manoharaṃ |
kṛtvānumaṅgalaṃ dehaṃ mṛdubhāṣaṇa tatparaḥ ||

kulālāyānusaṃdhāryaṃ kularūpaṃ vicintya vai |
vihared divase caiva rātrā vāja *? ṣṭi tatparaḥ ||

ānīya yoṣitaḥ sarvā jātyaṣṭakasamudbhavāḥ |
gandhānukramato deva nāmarūpaṃ vicintya vai ||

kuryān maheśvara | 

yāsyāt prathama śaktistu śuddhāntaḥ karaṇā dvijā |
śuddhā vimalasadgātrī divyagandhā manoramā ||

sā hi svapnāvatī devī tanmantrantatra yojayet |
śubhrapuṣpaiśca naivyadyairlavanairmadhusaṃyutaiḥ ||

pūjayitvā yathāpūrvaṃ sādhayen nijasādhanaṃ |
yā hṛṣṇa? nīlakṛṣṇā sāmṛdumaṅgala locana ||

sādhakā kāṃkṣa dvadayā sugandhā nindyagandhikāṃ ||

tasyāṃ nārī samāyojyā sā devī parikīrtitā |
tatpuṣpe naivatāṃ devīṃ tannaivedyaiśca pūjayet ||

sā syā * *? ttṛtīyā gaurāṅgī madhugandhānulocana |
tripurā nāma sā devī raktapuṣpeṇa pūjayet ||

madagandhānu yā nārī madaskhalana mantharā |
yuktā(?) āmiṣa gandhena mahiṣāsuramardinī ||

madya gandhā ca yā nārī rakta netrā viniskure |
sogratāreti saṃproktā mattā pāpa vināsinī ||

25a)

aśvagandhā tu yā nārī dīrghajaṃghā manoharā |
sāsyārcākatalolākṣī aśvārūḍhā varapradā ||

āmagandhā ca vadane pūtigandhā ca sandhiṣu |
nīlacarcala keśāyā sāsyād durgā mahonmukhī ||

sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktā nānāgandhā samākulā |
sudatī cāru keśī ca dīrghanetrā manoharā ||

nānāvilāsakuśalā kulaśāstra vicāriṇī |
guruvakṣasthalākrānta madhyāvivaladarśinī ||

sudatī sā bhaved devī sarvakāryā pravaddhinī |
etad gandhaiḥ samāyuktā nānā vā eka jātayaḥ ||

rūpagandhasamāyuktā nāmataḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
rakṣitavyāḥ prayatnena akṣatāstā yathā bhavet ||

svayaṃ kāmeśvaro nāmnā bhūtvā kāma parāyaṇaḥ |
bhāvavṛddhiṃ vidhāyaiva mūlayogaṃ samā * ret ||

mūlayoge kṛte tatra saptāhān maraṇaṃ bhavet |
na viṣṇurna hariḥ śambhurna devī na ca ṣoḍaśī ||

na ca mṛtyuñjayo vāpi na nyāso nāpi bhāvanā |
tasya mṛtyukṣaye devi prabhavanti na devatāḥ ||

śāstreṇa mūlayogena nāgadaṣṭavidhena vā |
vajrenābhi jvareṇaiva mahāvyādhi vaśādapi ||

mriyate tena pāpena satyaṃ deva suniścitaṃ |
devakṛtyā tu vahudhā dṛṣṭādṛṣṭavibhedataḥ ||

viśeṣatotra deveśa kimanyad vahujalpitaiḥ |
māyayā ccchādyacātmānaṃ nijastrīrūpadhāriṇī ||

āgatya ramate nityaṃ te naiva naśyati dhruvaṃ |
tasmāt svadeśe naivai tatkartavyaṃ siddhikāṃkṣayā ||

25b)

kiṃ vā kāmavilopārthaṃ patitavyaṃ sureśvara |
karmadaṇḍaṃ samādāya śivasāmyaṃ vidhāya ca ||

tadgātraṃ cakra sūtreṇa veṣṭanaṃ saptadhā kṛtaṃ |
pāśatvaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ rakṣaṇāya svayaṃbhuvā ||

nirmitosi mahābhāga ugraṃ bandhaṃ vidhīyatāṃ |
evamāmantrya tatsūtraṃ śivāṅge saptaveṣṭitaṃ ||

jāgrad daśāṃsa māsādya svāpamantraṃ tato japet |
saṃhāra pra?vodhinyā puṭitaṃ praṇava trayaṃ ||

svāpa mantraḥ samākhyātaḥ svāpadaḥ sarvajantuṣu |
evaṃ kṛtvā svayaṃ devo maheśādhikya eva ca ||

kulaṃ yadi syād yogañca cañcaraṃ smara lolupaṃ |
tadā pakṣā vidhātavyā na kāryā karhicid budhaiḥ ||

varāṅge śṛṇumadvākyamāvayoḥ śubha hetukaṃ |
etat kāryaṃ samāsādya kartavyaṃ tava veṣṭitaṃ ||

evamuktvā? nijaṃ kāryaṃ vidhāya sādhakottamaiḥ |
vaktavyaṃ na ca kartavyaṃ kulayogaṃ maheśvara ||

vīrapatnī vīrakanyā vīrāṅgajavarāṅganā |
nākṛṣyā sādhakaiḥ pūrvaṃ yāvat(?) syān masasaḥ(##?

manaḥkṣobha tatra jāte mūlayogena yatphalaṃ |
tatphalaṃ jāyate deva satyaṃ satyaṃ na cānyathā ||

vīrapatnī tu paramā svayameva maheśvarī |
tatsutā kulavidyānāmādyā vai tripurātanī ||

tadvadhūrbhuveneśānī sarvamantra prabodhinī |
nyāyato nyāyato vāpi vīrapatnī gururyadi ||

vīkṣaṇādeva sarvārtha sādhakaḥ sādhako bhavet |

26a)

yadi bhāgyavaśādeva vīrapatnī tu labhyate |
phalapākaṃ tatra kṛtvā kiṃ na siddhyati bhūtale ||

phalapākavidhānantu sarvadeva namaskṛtaṃ |
kathayāmi mahādeva na prakāśyaṃ kathañcana ||

dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇa kulaṃ deva deva gandharva kinnarāḥ |
āyānti phalapākārthaṃ tasmād yatnād vicintayet ||

visarjanaṃ vidhātavyaṃ gopanaṃ bhāva śāntikaṃ |
tat tat kalyā vidhānena kulaṃ kuryāt svaśikhitaṃ ||

mātaḥ pativratāddharmā dharmā na hi jagat traye |
patiṣprāṇaprado nityaṃ patireva parāgatiḥ ||

patireva parabrahma patiṣprāṇādhikaṃ prabhuḥ |
sarvatīrtha mayasvāmī sarvadaiva mayaḥ patiḥ ||

sarvadeva mayaścaiva patireva mahāguruḥ |
āgatyo paviśet pārśve mukhavīkṣya sthitā bhavet ||

pati bhāveṅgite dakṣā hṛṣṭā syāt pati darśane |
uttareṇottaraṃ dadyād vyāhared bhūta karmaṇi ||

sarvadevamayaṃ hitvā yā nārī paragocarā |
śūkarī garddhabhī kākī śṛgālī jāyate dhruvaṃ ||

ahantu putravad devi nātrabhītirna ca trayā |
tavāṅge yo hi jāti yatirūpadharaḥ kvacit ||

mūlamantraṃ japaṃ toyaṃ tad gātre deyamīśvari |
tadā yadi vahirna syāt tadā svapatireva ca ||

itodapi kṣayākāryāḥ śikṣitā kulayocitaḥ |
rakṣākāryā vidhānena pūrvokte naiva vatmanā ||

yoginīnāṃ mahāpūjā vaṭukānāñca bhairave |
kāryāvighnapināśārthaṃ kulavārarkṣa yogataḥ ||

26b)

yāsāṃ mūlatarormūla mūlavidyā tu kāmikā |
likhitvāneka kāleṣu tato vighnairna lupyate ||

vīrapatyā muladeśe na kāryaṃ mantra yojanaṃ |
visarjana vidhau deva yadīcchetphala pātanaṃ ||

ye * * * * *? tasya kulasya kulavittataṃ |
tadrūpaṃ(?) vigrahaṃ kṛtvā bhairavā vaṭukādayaḥ ||

tatra vījārpaṇaṃ kṛtvā kurvanti phalarakṣaṇaṃ |
liṅgai * * * * *? vajñātvā * *? 
samudbhavaṃ ||

* * * * * *? rjātaṃ yathārthajñāna śāstrataḥ |
jñātvā phalavibhāga *? pañcamāt parataḥ prabho ||

saptamāṣṭamamā sādvā(?) * *? madhye saro *##?
hastaṃ da * * * * * * *? saṃhāra yogataḥ ||

japedaṣṭa sahasraṃ vai svayaṃ vṛkṣānpatiṣpati |
evaṃ kṛte yat patati tatphalaṃ deva ni * *? ||

aśvā * *? ñca patati * * * * * * * 
*? |
* * * *? phalaṃ jñātvā ānīya kṣālayet tataḥ ||

doṣabhāgaṃ parityajya phalavṛkṣaṃ praṇamya ca |
tāmrapātre mahāraṇye phalapākaṃ samānayet ||

vṛkṣādi *? vidhāyaiva vaṭutraya samanvitaṃ |
nānāsugandhisaṃmiśraṃ siddhaṃ jñātvā vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

saṃskṛtā nalamadhye tu aghoraṃ homayet tataḥ |
dhenumudrā *? tī kṛtya homaṃ kuryādalakṣitaḥ ||

rātrā devaṃ vidhāyaiva yajñaśeṣaṃ bhajet tataḥ |
bhuktamātre tu tasmin vaicirajīvi(?)kṣitau bhavet ||

sadye madhumatī siddhiḥ karasthā bhogadāyinī |

27a)

divyabhāvaḥ sādhakendro martyaśakra samo bhavet |
devānāṃ priya kāritvāt mokṣadatvān maheśvara ||

sādhakānāṃ kulajñānāṃ na doṣaḥ kalpapātane |
etat phalanipātārthaṃ phalavaptā pade pade ||

vighnaṃ karoti sarvatra nijasaṣkā ca kāraṇāt |
phalavṛkṣopari tathā vighnamācarati dhruvaṃ ||

phalavṛkṣasya hāni syāt kadācid daivayogataḥ |
sādhakopi tathā deva bhaven nātra vikalpanaṃ ||

vācāvāmanasā vāpi kāyād vā yadi vādhyate |
phalavṛkṣastadā devo naśyati smara śāsanaṃ ||

vādhyate kulavṛkṣaścet sādhakasya kṛte prabho |
tāvan naḥ kulapūjā syād yāvat tanniskṛtirbhavet ||

tāvan nija kulālāpo varjanīyaḥ prayatnataḥ |
etat kārya vidhāne tu yogyo nāsti mahītale ||

ati dūrāntare deśe nāmavarṇa vivarjitaḥ |
karoti yadi bhadraṃ syād vighnaṃ vā phalasādhanaṃ ||

na doṣaistatra vādhyante sādhakāḥ kula devatāḥ |
vighnapradhāne metat tu kadācit phalasādhanaṃ ||

sarvatrasaṃketa naiva kartavyaṃ phalasādhanaṃ |
svaphalaṃ samaṃnuprāpya kriyate yadi pāmaraḥ ||

phalaṃ bhavati deveśa pāpena vadhyate dhruvaṃ |
nindāmadhumatī tatra jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||

madhumatyāḥ phalandeva kathayāmi śṛṇuṣva me |
sudhādhāra dharāvāṇī vaktrāt tasya prajāyate ||

sarvajña sarvaśāstrāṇāmadhipaḥ kulanāyakaḥ |
candrasūryāvadhi pṛthyāṃ bhunakti vahulaṃ sukhaṃ ||

27b)

devakanyāpsarobhistu divyagandharva cāraṇaiḥ |
nānāvilāsasampannaiḥ kalākuśa? *? ko vidaiḥ ||

nṛtyairgītaiḥ sumadhurairnānopāya rasāyanaiḥ |
amṛtārthe prīti karairdeva bhogyaiḥ sudurlabhaiḥ ||

devakanyā hastagatairathamāruhya sotsuka |
āyāti sādhakā kāṃkṣa hṛdayā parameśvarī ||

yatra kāmeśvaro devaścintā vaśamupāgataḥ |
tatraiva yānti satataṃ devakanyāḥ sahasrasaḥ ||

indra gopya puvī(?) kṛtvā marate sādhakaiḥ samaṃ |
tatro yogata pāpātmā bhuktā tu vahudhā sukhaṃ ||

cire(?) kārai(?) bhuvi sthitvā cānta mukti mavāpnuyāt |
eṣate kathitā deva divyabhāvaḥ sukhāvahaṃ ||

na deyo yasya kasyāpi paśau gopyaḥ prayatnataḥ |
vīrabhāvo mahādeva kathyate śṛṇu bhairava ||

nirdvandva mānasā bhuktvā hṛdi * *? kalā * *? |
nisāpūjā * *? rohe tu yuktaḥ sa deva hi ||

nijakulaṃ samādāya svayaṃ bhairava rūpadhṛk |
kulañca bhairavī rūpaṃ tadgātra nyāsa vistaraṃ ||

vinyasya sakalaṃ nyāsaṃ navayonyātmakantathā |
prasūna tūlikā madhye puṣpaprakarasaṅkule ||

nānāgandhasamākīrṇe kuladravyena yantrakaṃ |
likhitvā pūjayecchaṃkhaṃ ghaṭasthāpana pūrvakaṃ ||

svavāma bhāge ṣaṭkoṇaṃ tanmadhye brahmarandhrakaṃ |
likhitvā tatra kumbhaṃ vai sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ yavā ||

tāmraṃ bhūmi mayaṃ vāpi yadvālohavivarjitaṃ |
sthāpayet kalayā deva kumbhaṃ gandhasuvāsitaṃ ||

28a)

hetu dravyaṃ brāhmaṇādi bhedataḥ paripūjayet |
tatra mantraṃ vilikhyādau yadyat kulasamudbhavaṃ ||

dhyātveṣṭaṃ devatāṃ tatra japedaṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ |
dhenumudrāṃ pradarśyātha amṛtantadvicintayet ||

dṛṣṭvārghasya tu pātraṃ vai nṛtyanti sa gaṇādhipāḥ |
nṛtyanti bhairavāḥ sarve nṛtyanti mātaro gaṇāḥ ||

indrādayaḥ surāḥ sarva nṛtyanti madhulolupāḥ |
brahmaviṣṇu maheśādyā nṛtyanti harṣa tatparāḥ ||

arghe bhāṇḍaṃ dvidhā kṛtvā gurave caika bhāgakaṃ |
evaṃ kulāya datvā vai ekena deva tarpaṇaṃ ||

pītvā kula rasaṃ deve nānālaṃkālabhūṣitaḥ |
sākṣād yadi gururna syāt tadā toye visarjayet ||

ānanda rūpavān bhūtvā pūjayet parameśvarīṃ |
tat tat kalpokta vidhinā tat tad yantre prapūjya ca ||

visarjanaṃ vidhāyārthaṃ svakule yojayet tataḥ |
tad vindu rasa pānena amṛtaṃ bhujyate mayā ||

taccakro rarasā khādaiḥ sampraddrumama jāyate |
tatphala grahaṇā deva sumeru śṛṅgarohaṇaṃ ||

latā liṅganamātreṇa sudhādhauta kalevarā |
mūlayoge kṛte tatra japedaṣṭa sahasrakaṃ ||

japapūtaṃ havirddravyaṃ gṛhītvā tarpayet tataḥ |
vidhāya tarpaṇaṃ deva pradakṣiṇa manu vrajan ||

praṇamya stutvā kalpoktastavena toṣayet tataḥ |
idaṃ vīrakulaṃ deva sundaraṃ sumanoharaṃ ||

yad deśe vidyate vīrastatkulaṃ vāpi bhairava |
na ca mārī bhayaṃ tatra na ca rāja bhayādikaṃ ||

sumaṅgalaṃ sadā tatra dhanaputra vivarddhanaṃ |

28b)

lakṣmīstatra mahādeva susthirā sarvadā bhavet ||

mantraṃ svapna prabodhinyā avaśyaṃ vṛkṣamūlake |
yojanīyaṃ prayatnena na ca vighnaṃ prajāyate ||

nānyavīreṇa tad yogād grahaṇe * * *? pi |
yoginībhistu pūjyante(?) * *? pāpena * *? 
te ||

yatra tatra * *? vāre śmaśāne gamane kṛte |
pūjāphalaṃ bhavet tatra saptavāsara sammitaṃ ||

caturmāsyāṃ(?) gate tatra * * * *? phalaṃ 
* *? |
* * * * *? ta * *? paśureva na saṃśayaḥ ||

nānyaḥ syādadhikyā(?) deva(?) iti cintā parāyaṇaḥ |
sādha * * * * * *? mayikṣobha prajāyate ||

ta * * *? bhāga(?) yuto bhaved vīravaraḥ sudī? |
bhogena mokṣamāpnoti bhogena kula sādhanaṃ ||

vinā hetuṃ samāsādya * * * * maheśva *? |
*? pūjāṃ mama saṃkuryā svādhyānaṃ nava cintanaṃ ||?

* * * * * * *? ca pūjayet parameśvara |
rakta manyeti doṣāsti māyā ca do * * *? ṇaṃ ||?

yadyad * * *? nidrāti yat karoti yadarvati |?
tatsarvaṃ kularūpaṃ tu dhyātvā yadi haret sukhī ||

ekānte nirjane deśe śmaśāne parvate vane |
śūnyāgāre nadītīre * *? saṃgo vihare *? ||

vīrāṇāṃ japa kālastu sarvakālaḥ praśasyate |
sarva deśe sarvapīṭhe kartavyaṃ navasaṃśayaḥ ||

yadi viprā(?) bhaved bhraṣṭaḥ kuladravya parāyaṇaḥ |
tadā na * * * * *? kartavyaṃ kulatoṣaṇaṃ ||

aparo puṣpa garbhe tu kulasthānaṃ manoharaṃ |

29a)

sarvadeva mukhaṃ tat tu mahākāma kalātmakaṃ |
hayāri kusume devaḥ svayamasti sadāśivaḥ ||

tanmadhye laghumādāya puṣpamadhye tu candanaṃ |
raktaṃ kuṃkumarāgaṃ vā datvā tatra śivātmakaṃ ||

yojayecchivaśaktyāstu aikyaṃ sambhāvayan dhiyā |
kṣaṇantatraiva saṃpūjya niścitya parameśvarīṃ ||

japtvā tadeva kuṇḍotthaṃ dravyaṃ paramadurlabhaṃ |
amṛtādadhikaṃ jñeyametad dravyaṃ sudurlabhaṃ ||

yatrā parājitā puṣpaṃ javāpuṣpaṃ ca bhairava |
karavīre raktaśukle droṇamvā yatra tiṣṭhati ||

tatra devī vasen nityaṃ tadyaṃtre pūjanaṃ mama |
dattaikaika javāpuṣpe paṭṭavastre phalaṃ labhet ||

brahmahatyādikaṃ pāpaṃ kṣaṇān naśyati niścitaṃ |
aparāyāntu mahānmyaṃ vaktuṃ na hi maheśvari ||

vīrasādhaka kāryantu kartavyaṃ vīrapūruṣaiḥ |
divyairapi ca kartavyaṃ paśubhirnava pāmaraiḥ ||

varaṃ pāmara kāryantanna paśoriti niścayaḥ |
yad yad yadā purā proktaṃ śakte taṃ mantra sādhanaṃ ||

añjanaṃ guṭikādīṃśca kuryād vīro mahāvalaḥ |
divye vīre nabhe nabhe dosti yo bhedaḥsara kathyate ||

śānto vinīto madhuraḥ kalālāvanya saṃsthitaḥ |
divyastu devavat prāyo vīraścoddhata mānasaḥ ||

vibhūti bhūṣaṇo vāpi candanairvā vilepitaḥ |
ākāra gopanā vāpi tyakto vā kula bhairava ||

raktacandana digdho vā vaiṣṇavo vāpya vaiṣṇavaḥ |
asaṃskṛtaḥ saṃskṛto vā nitya vyaktaḥ kuleśvaraḥ ||

apamāne ca pūjāyāṃ hṛṣṭaḥ puṣṭaḥ sadā bhavet |
devanindāparo vāpi tatpūjādi paropi vā ||

pūjā ratastatra hitaḥ kulāt kula mate sthitaḥ |
nijabhāva samāyukto devavad viharet kṣitau ||

svakulānte puraścaryā kāryā rātrau na cānyathā |
vedahīne dvije devaṃ yathā na śruti satkriyā ||

viṣṇubhaktiṃ vinā devi bhaktirna prabhaved yathā |
śāktaṃ jñānaṃ vināmuktiryathā hāsyāya kalpate ||

guruṃ vinā yathā tantre nādhikāraḥ kathaṃcana |
patihīnā yathā nārī sarvakarma vivarjitā ||

kulaṃ vinā tathā devo vīro vāmamasādhakaḥ |
nādhikārīti kauleya tasmād bhāva paro bhava ||

avinītaṃ kulaṃ yasya sa kathaṃ samasādhakaḥ |
tasmād yatnāt tathā kāryaṃ yathā syād vinayānvitaṃ ||

bhāvābhāva kuleśāstre nādhikāraḥ kathaṃcana |
tena bhāvaviśuddhastu sādhakaḥ kaulikā bhavet ||

devyuvāca ||

paśubhāvaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuvatsa mahāhitaḥ |
yathāvidhi paśordīkṣāṃ gṛhītvā bhāve tatparaḥ ||

prathamaṃ pūrvamevārthaṃ yatnātaḥ śuddhimāpnuyāt |
namassya bhojanaṃ kuryān na striyaṃ manasā smaran ||

na para dravya lobhaḥ syān na bhoge mānasaṃ bhavet |
sindhutīre parvate vā kānane vā surālaye ||

vilvamūle vivikte ca puṇyakṣetre suśobhane |
na śūdradarśanaṃ kuryāt kauṭilyaṃ dūre tastyajet ||

devatā śuddha varṇaṃ tu dhyātavyā susamāhitaiḥ |

30a)

trisaṃdhyaṃ devapūjā ca trisaṃdhyaṃ japamācaret |
rātrau mālāñca yantraṃ ca spṛśen naiva kadācana ||

na mantre muccared bhuktvā maunī syāt sarvakarmasu |
parvakāle striyaṃ naiva gacchet tu sādhakottamaḥ ||

puṣpaṃ gandhaṃ jalaṃ caiva svayamānīya pūjayet |
maithunaṃ tatkathālopaṃ tadgauṣṭhī parivarjayet ||

ṛtukālaṃ vināgacchen na ca svastriyamādarāt |
purāṇaśravaṇe śraddhā vedavedārtha tatparaḥ ||

na rātrau bhojayed vidvāṃstāmbūlaṃ parameśvara |
guruṇā yad yadā diṣṭaṃ tatsarvaṃ yatnataścaret ||

svajāta kusumaṃ caiva hetu dravya tu bhairava |
spṛstvā stutvā trirātraṃ ca pañcagavyena śuddhyati ||

raktaṃ vastraṃ na gṛhnīyāt devī bhakti parāyaṇaḥ |
viṣṇumantrokta kalpena tadanuṣṭhāna meva ca ||

kāryaṃ vīra kathālāpaṃ na kuryād vīravandite |
miśrāddhaṃ gavāgrāsaṃ saṃdhyāvandanameva ca ||

tīrthasnānaṃ pīṭha deśa gamanaṃ dharma tatparaḥ |
paśustu dvividho deva pūrve na saha bhairava ||

asyādhikāraḥ pūjāyāṃ na gurutve kathaṃcana |
pūrvasya nādhikārosti pūjā deśagalāgatau ||

pūrvastu sarvakaulayairvahiḥ kāryaḥ svadeśataḥ |
pūrvadarśanamātreṇa kuladarśanamācaret ||

brahmahatyādikaṃ pāpaṃ varaṃ sahyaṃ na ca prabho |
pūrvadarśanamevātra satyaṃ satyaṃ vadāmyahaṃ ||

mamātmā sopi deveśa vibhutvā yadyapi prabho |
tathāpi tatra meśaṅko jāyate tu pade pade ||

30b)

valaṃ kula parityāgaṃ varaṃ svakula hiṃsanaṃ |
varaṃ svakulanindā syād varaṃ svakulavarddhanaṃ ||

varaṃ svakulahānistu varaṃ svakula * ṭṭanaṃ |
kāyena manasā vācā varaṃ svakulapātanaṃ ||

na kuryāt pūrvasaṅgantu mamāṅgakṣati(?) kāraṇāt |
kulaśāstraṣvidaṃ pāpaṃ gariṣṭhaṃ kulanāyake ||

svakule mahatī nindāparayogaḥ(?) kuleśvare |
tasmādapi mahāpāpaṃ paśusaṅgāt prajāyate ||

sarvathā tatra bhāvau dvau * *? kāśyau kuleśvara |
sa bhāvaśca vibhāvaśca pa * * * ? vasthitaḥ ||

sa bhāvaḥ paśubhāvena janma dvayavibhāvanāt |
vīrabhāvo bhaved deva ta * * * * jāyate ||

daivānna? jāyate vīro vīro devaḥ prajā * *? |
daivavīre na saṃdeha * * * *? bhidhīyate ||

bhāvastu manaso dharmaḥ śabde sa * * * * *? |
tasmād bhāvā na *? ktavyo diṅmātraṃ samudāhṛtaṃ ||

yathe * * * *? sādha * *? nairjāyate * 
*? |
tathā bhāvavibhāvastu manaso paribhāvyataṃ ||

eka eva mahābhāvo nānātvaṃ(?) bhajate prabho |
upādhi bhāvabhedena * * * *? tadukṣati ||

ānandaghanasaṃdoha prabhuḥ paramarūpadhṛk |
surarūpaḥ sa evātmā prabhu niravayā mahān ||

srotavyaḥ suramantavyāni dhyātavyaḥ sa eva hi |
sākṣāt * *? śca deveśa āsanairvividhaiḥ(?) pra 
*? ||

vā vadhyaḥ śruti vācya * *? ntavyo manasādibhiḥ |
sopapannibhirevāyaṃ dhyātavyo gurudeśitaiḥ ||

31a)

tadā sa eva sarvātmā pratyakṣo bhavati kṣaṇāt |
tasmin deve tu deveśa pratyakṣa parameśvara ||

bhāvairvahuvidhairdeva bhāvastatrāpi līyate |
bhuktvā nānāvidhaṃ grāsaṃ gavicaiko yathārasaḥ ||

dugdhādyabhyāsa yogena nānātvaṃ labhate prabho |
tṛṇena jāyate deva rasastasmāt paro rasaḥ ||

tasmād dadhi tato havyaṃ tasmādapi rasodayaḥ |
sa eva kāraṇaṃ tasya tatkāryaṃ sa ca labhyate ||

dṛśyate ca mahādeva na kāryaṃ na ca kāraṇaṃ |
dugdhatvādyabhisaṃbandhād bhedaiḥ sa hi vibhajyate ||

tathaivāyaṃ sa evātmā nānāvigraha yoniṣu |
jāyaja niṣpate jātaḥ kālabhedād vibhijyate ||

sa jātaḥ sa mṛto vaddhaḥ samuktaḥ sasukhī pumān |
sastrīsa napuṃsakaḥ sopi sa vidvān sa ca eva saḥ ||

nānā dhyāna samāyogān nānātvaṃ bhajate prabhuḥ |
eka eva sa evātmā rasarūpī sanātanaḥ ||

avyaktaśca saṃtradhyaktaḥ prakṛtyā jñāyate dhruvaṃ |
tasmāt prakṛti yogena jñāyate nānyathā kvacit ||

vinā ghaṭatva yogena pratyakṣo na yathā ghaṭaḥ |
itarād bhidyamānopi na bhedamupa gacchati ||

māṃ vinā puruṣe bhedā na ca yānti kathaṃcana |
na prayogairna ca jñānairna śrutyā na gurukramaiḥ ||

prakṛtyā jñāyate brahma prakṛtyā līyate pumān |
prakṛtyādhiṣṭitaṃ sarvaṃ prakṛtyā vāṃcchitaṃ jagat ||

prakṛtyā bhedamāpnoti prakṛtyā bhedamāpnuyāt |

31b)

varaṃ prakṛtirnaiva napumān parameśvaraḥ |
tayoḥ sarasaṃjñānaṃ vastu tatvaṃ maheśvara ||

ubhayoḥ samabhāvastu vastu tatvaṃ varaṃ padaṃ |
jñāte tasmin svayaṃ prajñāścit svarūpaḥ pumānasau ||

nirvikāraḥ sarva veda pramānā gocaraḥ pumān |
vyakto bhavati deveśi svayamātmā svayaṃ bhavaḥ ||

pāyoḥ santi vahudhā jñātuṃ brahma sanātanaṃ |
tathāpi prakṛteryogāt kṣipraṃ pratyakṣatāṃ vrajat ||

sāṃyātrikāḥ kudeśeṣu yathānāvaṃ pragṛhya ca |
nānādeśaṃ samuttirya samudraṃ viśate cirāt ||

taṭe vai nāvamādāya kṣaṇād yānti yathā prabho |
viṣṇubhaktyā labhed brahma tadbhāvādapi * prabho ||

vaijātyaṃ nāsti cet tatra kathaṃ tat tadvikāraṇaṃ |
tasmāt paraṃ parā yātāste hi kāraṇatāṃ gatāḥ ||

na kāraṇāni te vatsa sākṣyāt prakṛti kāraṇaṃ |
strī bhāvaḥ prakṛtirjñeyaḥ puṃbhāvaḥ puruṣo mataḥ ||

jñapti jñeyāli bhāgena jñānaṃ ca bhairaveśvara |
ghaṭa pratyakṣa māṇastu āloko raṃjako yathā ||

cakṣurādi na tātkiñcidadhikaṃ nirvikalpakaṃ |
śrīramāramaṇa devaśarmaṇānirmite nigamasāranirṇaye | āśu * * * 
* * * * * * * * * * * * * 
* * * * * * * ||


idānīṃ yatrāsti bhāgo na ca tatra mokṣo yatrāsti mokṣo na ca tatra bhogaḥ | 
śrīsundarī pūjana tatparā śaṃbhogaśca mokṣaśca karastha eva ||

iti stuti mukheno bhaya phalaśravaṇādubhayamādhanatve sa puruṣārtha tvāt 
prathamaṃ sundarī mantrābhi likhyante ||

32a)

iha yadyapi ṣoḍaśārṇā yo jāgrad rūpe tvāt prādhānyaṃ tathāpi trikūṭā sakalā 
bhadre ṣoḍaśārṇā bhavanti hi | iti vacanāt ṣoḍaśārṇāyā api trikūṭa ghaṭitatvāt 
prathamaṃ trikūṭamantrābhi likhyante ||

tatra jñānārṇave ||

bhūmiścandraḥ śivo māyā śaktiḥ kṛṣṇādhva mādanau |
arddhacandraśca binduśca navārṇo merurucyate ||

mahātripurasundaryā mantrāmeru samudbhavāḥ |
bhūmirlakāraḥ candraḥ sakāraḥ śivohakāraḥ māyā īkāraḥ śaktirakāraḥ 
kṛṣṇādhvā rephaḥ mādanaḥ kakāraḥ ete varṇāḥ sundarī mantrāṇāṃ meravaḥ 
sundarī mantreṣve tadakṣarāti riktākṣaraṃ nāstītyarthaḥ || atha kāmarājaḥ ||

śaktitastūryarṇāyaḥ kalamadhye varānane |
vāgbhavaṃ paṃcavarṇastu kāmarājamathocyate ||

mādane śivacandrādye śivānte mīlalocane |
kāmarājamidambhadre ṣaḍvarṇaṃ sarvamohanaṃ ||

śaktibījamvarārohe candrādyaṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ |
caturakṣara rūpantu tryakṣarā tripurā bhavet ||

śaktirekāraḥ tūrya varṇo dīrgha īkāro yaḥ sakakāralakārayormadhye likhanīyaḥ 
tena ka e ī la rūpantato māyā bījaṃ hrīmiti ||

atha tantre māyā bījasyālikhanāt kathaṃ labhyate iti cet sakālā bhuvaneśānī 
kāmeśī bījamuttamaṃ | anayā sakalā vidyāḥ kathayāmi viśeṣata ityato labhyate |

32b)

atha śakti tastūrya parṇasya sakala madhya vidhānāt kathaṃ śakterapi 
kalamadhyavidhānamiti cenna tantrāntare virodhāt tathā hi catuśmatyāṃ || 

mādanaṃ tadadhaḥ śaktistadante vindu mālinī |

aindrañca bhuvaneśānī bījantadvāgbhavambhavediti likhanān ||
kāmabīja mohamādana iti mādane kakāra śiva candrādye yasyarthaḥ śivānte 
śivāhakāraḥ anto lakāraḥ tāvanta yasya madhyānta śabdalopaḥ tena hasakala 
hrīmiti pūrva vacanāt sarvatra bhuvaneśī vījasamvaḥ | atha mādane śiva candrādya 
ityasya śivacandrasyādau mādanasya sadbhāvaḥ kathan na syāditi ratna(##?

śivabījaṃ tridhā kṛtvā sṛṣṭi sthiti naya kramāt |
dvayamādyena rahitaṃ ādyādho madanākṣaraṃ ||

para sthiti śivādhastādindra bījanniyojayet |
tathā laya śivādhopi vahnistūrya surendu māniti likhanāt ||

ādyādho madanākṣaramiti pāṭhāllikhita samāsa eva bhavati | asyārthaḥ ||

śivabījaṃ hakāraṃ tridhā likhitvā dvayaṃ ādyena upasthita śivādyena sakāreṇa 
rahitaṃ ekaṃ sakāre sahitaṃ ādyādho hakārottara sakārādho madanākṣaraṃ 
kakāraṃ datvā tatparasthiti hakārādhastādindra bījaṃ lakāraṃ dadyāt tato laya 
hakārādho vahnistūrya svarādau repha īkārau nidhāya madhya bījamuddharediti 
śaktibījamāha candrādyamiti kala svarūpamityarthaḥ ||

33a)

tena sakala hrīmiti atha mantra pratipādaka tantre kalasva rūpā na bhidhānāt kathaṃ 
kalasvarūpaṃ labhyate iti ccanna tantrāntare candrādya kalasvarūpasyābhidhānāt 
tathā hi catuśmatyāṃ ||

māyābījaṃ maheśāni mādanaṃśakrasaṃyutaṃ |
candrabījaṃ kevalaṃ tu viniyujya varānane ||

saṃhāra kramayogena śaktibījaṃ samuddharet |

asyārthaḥ ||

māyā hrīṁ mādanaṃ kakāraḥ śakro lakāraḥ candraḥ sakāraḥ saṃhāra krameṇa 
candrādya krameṇeti apasaṃhāra krameṇa sakārottara lakāro labhyata iti cet 
śakrasaṃyutamityasya viśeṣaṇatvenāpradhāna tvāt pūrvanipātaḥ | etena 
pañcadaśākṣarī trikūṭākāmarājākhya vidyeti dik || ||

athātra sāpoddhāraḥ || yathā muṇḍamālāyāṃ || 

kāmarājākhya vidyāyā viddhāsā puṣpadhanvano |
śakreṇa pūjitā pūrvaṃ bhuvanaśyā pratiṣṭhitā ||

tena kāmaśāpādindreṇa bhuvaneśyāsaṃyujya uddhāraḥ kṛtaḥ bhuvaneśī hrīṃ 
|| tathā kāmarājākhya vidyāyāḥ śaktiṃ tūryañca sundari hitvā * * 
mukhe śivendubhyāṃ lopāmudrā tviyaṃ bhavet || asyārthaḥ ||

kāmarājākhya vidyāyāḥ śaktiṃ ekāraṃ tūryamīkārañca tyaktā mukhe ādau 
śivacandrābhyāṃ hakāra sakārābhyāṃ yojanāllopāmudrā bhavatīti ||

33b)

tena ha sakala hrīmiti prathama bījaṃ bījadvayaṃ kāmarājā * *? || 
* *? kāmajākhyā vidyāyāstṛtīyaṃ suravandite | sahādyaśakti 
bījaṃ * *? dyā sastyarcitā(?) bhavet ||

* * * * *? kāmarājākhya(?) 
vidyāyāstṛtīyaśakti dīkṣādau(?) saha rūpākṣara dvaya dvitīya 
lopāmudrā bhavati || te * * * * * * * *? 
tṛtīya bījaṃ ādya dvayaṃ pūrvavat || brahmavidyā(?) *? 
raṃ prade(?) *? mi śaktimādana madhyagaṃ | 
śivaṅkuryād vāgbhavetha śivādyaṃ kāmarājākhya(?) || * 
* * *? tṛtīyaṃ * *? vidyeyaṃ mana 
pūjitā(?) || asyārthaḥ || 

* * * * *? vidyāyā vāgbhavā śaktimādanayorekāra 
kakārayormadhyagaṃ śivaṃ hakā * * * * * * * 
* * *? madhya bījaṃ śivādyai hakārādyaṅkuryāt 
tṛtīyabījacandrādyaṃ kuryāt || tena ka ha e īla hrīṃ ha ha sakahala hrīṃ sa sakala 
hrīmiti svarūpā(?) manu pūjitā bhavati || anyathā śivādyañca 
ādyamiti asyo nanuyā * *? ttaḥ etena yaduktaṃ śeṣavījadvayaṃ 
yathāstitamiti devanāthopādhya *? stadapi parāstaṃ || tathā lopāmudrā 
dvitīyāntu vilikhya suravandita | punarvilikhya nāmeva caturthe pañcame sthitāṃ || 

hitvā tu bhuvaneśānimekā kāreṇa coccaret |
catuḥkūṭātmikā vidyā śaṅkarorādhitā bhavet ||

asyārthaḥ ||

dvitīya lopāmudrāmuddhṛtya pustāmeva 

34a)

vilikhya caturthapañcama kūṭasthāṃ bhuvaṇeśvarīṃ tyaktā ekatāpattau 
śaṅkarārādhitā bhavati || 

tathā lopāmudrāntathā devi vilikhya tadanantaraṃ |
nandikeśvara vidyāñca ṣaṭkuṭā vaiṣṇavī bhavet ||

asyārthaḥ ||

lopāmudrā dvitīyā meva tatheti śravaṇāt |

etena dvitīya lopāmudrā nandikeśvarekātrī karaṇe ṣaṭkūṭā vaiṣṇavī bhavati || 
nandikeśvara vidyā yathā || tathā kāmarājākhya vidyāyā vāgbhavaṃ mādanaṃ 
tyaja | 

candrantathaiva saṃyojya kāmarāje tataḥ paraṃ |
hitvā candraṃ mukhe kuryād vidyeyaṃ nandi pūjitā ||

asyārthaḥ || 

kāmarājākhya vidyāyā vāgbhave prathama bīje mādanaṃ kakāraṃ tyajeccandraṃ 
sakāraṃ dadyāt | tena sa e ī la hrīṃ tataḥ kāmarāje candrabījaṃ sakāraṃ hitvā 
mukhe mādanaṃ kakāraṃ kuryāt || tena kaha kaha la hrīṃ na tu candraṃ hitvā 
mukhe candraṃ sakāraṅkuryāditi kāmantatra viniḥkṣipediti dakṣiṇāmūrti tacca 
surasāt tṛtīyaṃ kāmarājavaditi || 

tathā hasādyaṃ vāgbhave devi candrādyaṃ śiva madhyagaṃ | 
mādanaṃ kāmarāje tu śaktikūṭaṃ hasānvitaṃ ||

candro rādhita vidyeyaṃ bhogamokṣa phalapradā |

asyārthaḥ ||

kāmarājākhya vidyāyā vāgbhavaṃ hasādyaṃ kuryāt tena ha sa ka e ila hrīmiti 
kāmarājaṃ candrādyaṃ kuryāt tataḥ śivayorhakārayormadhyagaṃ 
mādanambhavati tena saha ka pha la hrīṃ śaktibījaṃ hasānvitaṃ tena ha sa sakala 
hrīmiti iyaṃ candrārādhitā bhavati || tathā hasādyamvāgbhavaṃ bījaṃ śivādyaṃ 
hasamadhyagaṃ | 

mādanaṃ kāmabīje tu tṛtīyaṃ śṛṇu pārvati |
hasādyaṃ śaktibījastu kuvereṇa samarcitā ||

asyārthaḥ ||

hasādyaṃ vāgbhavaṃ ha sa varṇādyaṃ vāgbhavamityarthaḥ | tena ha sa ka e ī la 
hrīṃ śivādyaṃ hakārādyaṃ mādanaṃ ha sa madhyagaṃ mādanaṃ kakāraḥ 
kuryāditi samvandhaḥ | tena hakāra syād ya vidya sthitaṃ kakāraṃ ha sa madhyagaṃ 
kuryād ityarthaḥ | tena ha ka sahala hrīṃ tṛtīyaṃ śaktibījaṃ hasādyaṃ ha sa 
sakala hrīmiti kuverārādhitā vidyā || tathā kāmarājākhya vidyāyā hitvā bhūmiṃ 
tṛtīyake |

śaktibīja sthitāṃ devi candrādhaḥ kuru tatra ca |
indrārādhita vidyeyaṃ bhukti mukti phalapradā ||

asyārthaḥ ||

kāmarājākhya vidyāyāḥ tṛtīyake śakti bīje bhūmi lakāraṃ ukta sthānāt hitvā 
candrādhaḥ sakārādhaḥ kurute tena sakala hrīmiti caramaṃ ādya dvayaṃ 
kāmarājavat iti saṃkṣepaḥ ||

evaṃ trikūṭa vidyāyāṃ nirūpitāyāṃ ṣoḍaśyāśca tannirūpaṇādhīna tvāt 
idānīṃ ṣoḍaśī likhyate | 

atha vakṣyāmi tāṃ vidyāṃ sarvādyāṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ |
śabdabrahmamayī nityāṃ ṣoḍaśārṇa svarūpiṇīṃ ||

brahmavidyā svarūpāñca bhukti mukti phalapradāṃ |
vinā guru padena śrīvidyāṃ yojayed budhaḥ ||

35a)

yoginīnāṃ bhaved bhakṣyaḥ śrīguroḥ śāpamālabhet |
ṣoḍaśārṇāṃ mahāvidyāṃ na dadyāt kasyacid budhaḥ ||

rājyaṃ deyaṃ dhanaṃ varaṃ deyaṃ kadācana |
prakāśāt siddhihaniḥ syādityājñā śambhunā kṛtā ||

rājñarājya pradāyopi putrāya prāṇadaya vā |
deyantu sarvasāmrājyaṃ putramitra kalatrakaṃ ||

śiropi prāṇasahitaṃ na deyā ṣoḍaśākṣarī |
uccārya mānā mantrā ye te sarvavācikāḥ smṛtāḥ ||

uccārya rahitaṃ vastu śrīvidyā ṣoḍaśākṣarī |
caturvidha prakāreṇa kathayiṣyāmi sudurlabhāṃ ||

dakṣiṇāmūrtitantrasya yā yā vidyā samuddhṛtā |
sā sā ca ṣoḍaśī vidyā sakalā bīja bhedataḥ ||

candrādyaṃ vāruṇāntañca śakrādi sahitaṃ pṛthak |
vāmākṣi bindunādādyaṃ viśvamātṛkalātmakaṃ ||

vidyādau yojanādeva jāgrad rūpā ca ṣoḍaśī candraḥ sakāraḥ sa evādye yasya 
hakārasya taccandrādyaṃ varuṇo vakāraḥ tasyāntaḥ sakāraḥ tadityarthaḥ śakrādi 
sahitau ityarthaḥ | śakro lakārastasyādī rephaḥ tatsahitaṃ pṛthak akṣara dvaya 
rūpaṃ vāmākṣi dīrgha īkāraḥ taduktaṃ bindunādādyaṃ bindunāda yuktaṃ tena 
hrīṁ śrīṁ rūpaṃ etayorvidyādau yojanā deva jāgrad rūpā ṣoḍaśī bhavati vidyā 
cātra kāmarājaḥ | 

utpattirjāgraro bodho vyāvṛttirmanasaḥ sadā | 
kalācatuṣṭayaṃ jāgradavasthāyāṃ avasthitaṃ jāgrat * *? guṇa 
* * * *?kalā(?) śaktirūpiṇī |

35b)

trikūṭā kevalā bhadra pañcakūṭābhavanti hi |
vaiṣṇavī vasukūṭā syādevaṃ nārāyaṇī bhavet ||

tena etadbīja * * * * * * * *? ṭāḥ pañca 
kūṭā bhavati ṣoḍaśī vaiṣṇavī nārāyaṇī ca vasu kūṭā bhavati etayoḥ ṣaṭkūṭa 
kāmini(?) * *? pāpaṃ prakārāntasyā * *

kalā catuyaṃ * * * * * * * * * * * 
* * * * * *? pañcakūṭā * * *?

asyāḥ smaraṇa * * * * * * * * * * 
*? ||

* * * *? tu kathitaṃ * *? sārataraṃ mahat |

asyārthaḥ ||

pañcakūṭātmikāyo ṣoḍaśī kathitāṃ * *? vayadādiḥ oṃkāraḥ tena 
yu * * * *? kūṭā śivaśakti * * * * 
*? bhavati |

yathā oṃ hrīṁ śrīṁ iti kāmarājaḥ ato jāgrat susupti svapnarūpā svavasthāsu 
bheda trayaṃ kathitamiti aparā ca ṣoḍaśī kāmarājākhyā tridhā bhavati bījataḥ 
datvāśopāra bījantu kāmarājasya pūrvataḥ ||

36a)

ṣoḍaśī kāmarājākhyā kandarpyārādhitā matā |
asyāḥ prasādāt kāmopi trailokya vijayānvitaḥ ||

sarvasaundarya śubhagaḥ kārayet trijedvaśaṃ |

asyārthaḥ ||

kāmarājasya pūrvataḥ gopālabījaṃ klīmiti niyojya likhyate kandarpyārādhitā 
ṣoḍaśī bhavati | tathā māyā puraskṛtā vidyā ṣoḍaśī sarvadurlabhā || 

śrīkṛṣṇārādhitā seyaṃ yato bhūdīśvaraḥ svayaṃ |
yat prasādāt svayaṃ kṛṣṇo vibharti ca guṇatrayaṃ ||

asyārthaḥ ||

seyaṃ kandarpya rādhitā māyā hrīṁ rūpayā puraskṛtā cet śrīkṛṣṇārādhitā 
bhavati | na tu māyā puraskṛtā kāmarāja vidyeti | jāgrad rūpā ṣoḍaśī tu bījaika 
viniyojanādityanenā bhedāpapatteriti | 

tathā ṣoḍaśī vidyā sarvasaubhāgyadāyinī |
lakṣmyā cārādhitā vidyā vibharti ca guṇatrayaṃ ||

yat praśādāllakṣmīrvaiṣṇavī vallabhā |

asyārthaḥ ||

pūrvoktā kandarpyārādhitā ṣoḍaśī śrīmataṃ |
śrīmiti bījayutā lakṣmyā cārādhitā bhavati na tu kāmarāja vidyā pūrvā 
bhedāpatteriti | tathā aparāñca pravakṣyāmi ṣoḍaśīṃ sarvadurlabhaṃ | 
kāmarājākhya vidyādau kāmayoniḥ śrī yaḥ kramāt | 

japtvā ca sādhako bhaktyā vāgīśatvamuyivān | 
eṣā guptatamājñeyā sapta tantreṣu gopitā ||

anayā sadṛśī vidyā matkalā(?) na bhaviṣyati |

asyārthaḥ ||

pūrvokta kāmarāja vidyādau kāmayo *? śriyaḥ klīṁ aiṁ śrīṁ *
