* * * * * * * * * * * * * 
* * * * * * * * * *? saṃdheyaḥ | 
viśu * * *? deveśi tato nyāsaṃ * * * * 
*? tathā * * * * maheśāni dakṣiṇāmūrti * * 
* * * * * * * *? tvāt * * * 
* * *? mukhe priye | devatā hṛdaya * * *? 
yunā * * * *? bījaṃ vāgbhave * *?

65a)

ddiṣṭaṃ śaktiṃ tārtīyamūhayet |

etena asya mantrasya dakṣiṇāmūrti ṛṣiḥ paktiḥ cchandau vālātripurā devatā 
vāgbhavaṃ bījaṃ hsauḥ śaktirabhīṣṭasiddhyai viniyogaḥ iti smṛtvā śirovadana 
hṛtsu ṛṣicchando devatādīn nyaset | nābherācaraṇaṃ nyasya galādārābhi 
cāparaṃ | 

mūrddhādi galaparyanta tryakṣarīṃ vinyaset kramāt |

iti kavacaṃ tryakṣarī śabdena trikūṭālakṣate bhairavyādīnāntathā darśanāt tathā 

vāme pāṇi tale caikaṃ dakṣapāṇi tale tathā | 
ubhayoḥ sampuṭe vidyāttribījaṃ vinyasecchive ||

tathā 

mūrddhnyādhāra hṛdi nyaset bhūyo bīja trayaṃ kramāt | 
evamukteṣu sthāneṣu kramād vīja trayaṃ nyaset || 

pañcavānān krameṇaiva pañcāṅguliṣu vinyaset |
aṅguṣṭhādi kaniṣṭhāntaṃ krameṇa parameśvari ||

drāṃ drīṃ klīṃ vlaṃ iti proktaṃ candramāḥ sargavān bhavet ||

pañcavāṇāni deveśi nāmāni śṛṇu pārvati |
kṣobhanaṃ drāvaṇo devi tathā karṣaṇasaṃjñakaḥ ||

vaśyonmāda krameṇaiva kathitā vīravandite |

etena drāṃ kṣobhanāya nama iti krameṇāṅguṣṭhādiṣu nyaset | candramāḥ 
sakāraḥ sargavān visargavān | tena saḥ iti cāṇa catuṣṭaye pramāṇamuktaṃ ||

jñānārṇave ||

khānta dvayaṃ samālikhya vahnisaṃsthaṃ krameṇa hi |
mukhavṛttena netreṇa vāmeṇa parimaṇḍitaṃ ||

vāṇadvayamidaṃ proktaṃ mādanaṃ bhūmisaṃyutaṃ |

65b)

caturthasvaravindvāḍhyaṃ nādarūpaṃ vārānane |
phāntaṃ śakrasamāyuktaṃ vāmakarṇavibhūṣitaṃ ||

vindunādasamāyuktaṃ ityādi || thāntaṃ dakāraḥ vahnīrephaḥ mukhavṛtta ākāre 
vāmanetra īkāraḥ mādanaṃ kakāra bhūmirlakāraḥ caturthasvara īkāraḥ phāntaṃ 
vakāraḥ śakro lakāraḥ vāmakarṇa ūkāraḥ | tena drāṃ drīṃ klīṃ vlūṃmiti || 

kāmāstatraiva vijñeyāsteṣāṃ nāmāni saṃśṛṇu |
parābījaṃ madhyavāṇaṃ vāgbhavaṃ parameśvari ||

tūryavāṇaṃ tataścaiva strībījaṃ tatkramān nyaset |

parābījaṃ krīṃ madhyavāṇaṃ klīṃ vāgbhavaṃ aiṁ turyavāṇaṃ vlūṃ strībījaṃ 
strīṃ | tathā kāmamanmatha kandarpyamakaradhvajasaṃjñakāḥ | 

mīnaketurmaheśāni pañcamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |

tena hrīṃ kāmāya nama iti krameṇāṅguṣṭhādiṣu nyaset | tathā mūlavidyā 
dvirāvṛtyā sarvāṃguli taleṣvapi | ṣaḍaṅgamapi nirvartya 
mātṛkānyāsamācaret || tena aiṁ hṛdayāya iti krameṇa aṅguṣṭhādi talānteṣu 
hṛdādiṣu ca ṣaḍaṅga nyāsaṃ vidhāya sundarī mantrokta mātṛkā nyāsaṃ 
kuryāt tathā vidyā nyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryāt tribhirbījaiḥ kramācchive || 

karayorvinyased ādau maṇibandhe talegrake | 
dakṣe vāme ca vinyasya kakṣakurpara pāṇiṣu ||

punadakṣa ca vāme ca pādayośca tathā nyaset |
nādānte hṛdaye liṅga nyased devi tataḥ paraṃ ||

66a)

eteṣvaṅgeṣu deveśi saṃhāra kramato nyaset |

eteṣu ukteṣu triṣu triṣvaṅgeṣu kramād bījatrayaṃ nyaset | evaṃ saṃhāra krameṇa 
viparīta krameṇetyarthaḥ | 

evayonyāṅkitaṃ nyāsaṃ kuryādbījaistribhistataḥ |
karṇayościbuke bhūyaḥ śaṃkhayorvadane punaḥ ||

netrayonasi vinyasya vāhuyugme hṛdambuje |
tataśca kurpayoḥ kukṣau jānunordhvaja mūrddhani ||

pādayorguhya deśe ca pārśva hṛtsustana dvaye |
kaṇṭhe ca navayonyākhyaṃ nyased bīja trayaṃ kramāt ||

etenokta sthāneṣu vidyāṃ krameṇa trirāvṛtyā navabhirvījairnava yoni nyāsaṃ 
kuryāt tathā | 

ṣaḍaṅgamācared devi dvirāvṛtyā krameṇa tu |
tri * kaika daśa tridvisaṃkhyayā śaila sambhave ||

iti vacanādyathā vasaraṃ karaṇīyamiti |

tathā ||

aṅganyāsaṃ punardevi vāṇān kāmāṃśca vinyaset |
lalāṭa galadvannābhi mūlādhāreṣu vai kramāt ||

etena pūrvokta vāṇān kāmāṃśca lalāṭādiṣu nyasedityarthaḥ | tato mūlena 
vyāpakaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret | etadādau mātṛkānantaraṃ kara 
nyāsaḥ | tathā anyepi kāmyā nyāsāstantrāntareṣvanu saṃdheyaḥ ||

tathā jñānārṇave ||

evaṃ nyasta śarīrau sau samāhita manāstataḥ |
paramānanda sandoha sānandaścintayet kramāt ||

tato'ntaryajanaṃ kṛtvā vāhya pūjāṃ samācaret |

66b)

tatra prāṅmukha āsīno yantrodāraṃ prakalpayet |
īśānādagni paryantaṃ ṛjurekhāṃ samālikhet ||

īśādagre tadagrādyāṃ rekhe ākṛṣya deśikaḥ |
ekī kṛtvā devāruṇyāṃ * * * *? parāśive ||

trikoṇākāra(?) rūpantu śaktidvaya mudāhṛtaṃ |
pūrvaśaktyagra bhāge tu mānayaṣṭi vadā likhet ||

rekhāntu parameśāni vāyurākṣasā * ṇgaṃ | 
* *? bheda krameṇaiva tayoḥ śaktyāstataḥ paraṃ ||

rekhe ākṛṣyakoṇābhyāṃ tadagrāt pūrvagān kuru |
vahni *? rṇa metat syāt pūrvāgraṃ suravandite(?) ||

navayoni viśe *? ḍhyaṃ cakrarājamidaṃ bhavet |
etad vāhye tu saṃlekhyaṃ vṛttaṃ pūrṇendu sannibhaṃ ||

tallagnamaṣṭapatrantu granthibhiścāṣṭabhiryutaṃ |
granthayaḥ praṇavābhyāntu saṃpuṭatvena kārayet ||

triśūlāṣṭaka mālikhya caturasraṃ likhecchive |
caturdvāra viśobhāḍhyaṃ sarvānandakaraṃ sadā ||

haṁsauḥ kāraṃ samālikhya trikoṇāstṛ sureśvari |
vāmabījaṃ madhyamaṃ yadaṣṭakoṇe saṃlikhet ||

svarān ṣoḍaśa deveśi yugmayugma prabhedataḥ |
dalāṣṭaka tu saṃlikhya paścimādi pradakṣiṇaṃ ||

granthi sthāneṣu varṇānāṃ kādīnāṃ parameśvari |
vilikhet saptavargāṇi madhya tu parameśvari ||

kṣakāramaṣṭa?me yojyaṃ śeṣavarṇa krameṇa tu |
mūlāṣṭake tu saṃlikhyāḥ pañcamādi krameṇa tu ||

tad vāhyai mātṛkā vṛttaṃ likhecca parameśvari |
caturasre maheśāni mātṛkāṃ kāmagarbhitāṃ ||

vilikhya pūjayed yantraṃ hemarūpyādi paṭṭake |

67a)

bhūmau candanapīṭhe ca kuṅkumāruṇa candanaiḥ |
sindurarajasā vāpi kastūrī ghusṛṇāmvubhiḥ ||

vilikhya yantraṃ deveśi pūjā dravyāṇi yojayet ||

vārāhītantre ||

madhupātraṃ tathā cāgre kṣīrapātrantu dakṣiṇe |
kuṇḍadvayaṃ guggulāḍhyaṃ vāmadakṣe niveśayet ||

tripurā ca tathā ṅantaṃ vidmahe padamuccaret |
bhairavyai ca tathā coktvā dhīmahīti tato vadet ||

tanno devī padaṃ coktā pracodayādudīrayet |
gāyatrī traipurī khyātā rāgādyena samuddharet ||

ṅentaṃ caturthantamityarthaḥ vāk aiṁ kuśodake japtena yāgavastūni śodhayet | 
tato devīṃ dhyāyet ||

mūlādi brahmarandhrāntaṃ viśatantu tanīyasīṃ |
bhramadbhramaralīlābhadhammillāmalapuṣpinīṃ ||

brahmarandhra bhramadbhṛṅgamuktārekhā virājitāṃ |
muktārekhālagnaratna tilakāṃ mukuṭojvalāṃ ||

viśuddhamuktāratnāḍhyaṃ candrarekhā kirīṭinīṃ |
bhramadbhramaka līlābha nayanatraya bhūṣitāṃ ||

sūryabhāsvanmahāratna mukūṭālaṅkṛtāṃ parāṃ |
śubhrākārasphuranmuktā hārabhūṣaṇabhūṣitāṃ ||

graivaiyāṃgada patrāṇi sphūrat kānti jitāmṛtāṃ |
gaṅgātaraṅgakarpūra śuddhāmvaravirājitāṃ ||

śrīkhaṇḍavallī śadṛśa vāhūvallī virājitāṃ |
kaṅkālādi lasadbhūṣā maṇivaddha lasat prabhāṃ ||

pravālapallavākāra pādapallavabhūṣitāṃ |
nakṣatramālāsaṃkāśa muktāmañjīramaṇḍitāṃ ||

67b)

vāme na pāṇi naikena pustakaṃ cāpareṇa tu |
abhayena prayacchantī sādha *? nāṃ varānane ||

akṣamālāñca varadāṃ dakṣapāṇi dvaya kramāt |
dadhatīṃ cintayed devīṃ viśvasaubhāgya vāk pradaṃ ||

kṣīrakundendu dhavalāṃ prasannāṃ saṃsmarecchivā |
iti dhyātvā mānasairupacāraiḥ saṃpūjya arghasthāpanaṃ kuryāt || 

tataḥ pīṭhapūjāṃ vidhāyādauravi devīṃ virājitāṃ |
ravi devīṃ dvādaśā devīṃ vāmājyeṣṭhā ca raudrī ca ambikecchā? tataḥ 
paraṃ | 

jñānā kriyā kubjikā ca vibhī caiva viṣaghnikā |
bhūcarī caiva ānandā devi dvādaśa śaktayaḥ ||

pūrvādi paritaḥ pūjyā ravisaṃkhyā varānane |
sampūjya madhyai deveśi pretasiṃhāsanaṃ yajet ||

etena pūrvādi prādakṣiṇyena oṃ vāmāyai nama iti krameṇa pūjayet | tataḥ 
pretasiṃhāsanaṃ yajet | 

cakramadhya sthitaṃ bījaṃ sadāśiva mahāpadaṃ |
pretapadmāsanaṃ ṅentaṃ namo'nto mururīritaḥ ||

mahāśivasya ma * *? kulārṇava parameśvari |
cakramadhya sthitaṃ bījaṃ hasauḥ tena hklauḥ(?) sadāśiva mahāpreta 
padmāsanāya namaḥ iti mantraḥ | tathā āvāhya cakramadhye tu tarpayet kulasundarī | 
etenāvāhanādi sa * * * * * * * *? 
vāhanādikāṃnmatryaśca tatraiva likhitāḥ tathā 

upacārairalaṅkāraistoṣayet parameśvarīṃ |
etena * hannāḍyānī * * *? saṃsthāpya jīva nyāsādikaṃ 
vidhāyā vāhya arghodakena tarpayet |

68a)

tataḥ pādyādibhirupacāraiḥ pūjayed ityarthaḥ | 
ṣaḍaṅgaṃ kalpayed deha samyak saṃtarpayecchivāṃ ||

parivārārcanaṃ paścādādāvaṃgāvṛtiḥ śive | 

etena ṣaḍaṅgamantreṇa ṣaḍaṅgaṃ saṃpūjya gurupūjāṃ kuryāt | taduktaṃ |

guru pūjānantarantu paramaṃ gurumarcayet |
koṇamadhyayonyantarāle tathā naijaṃ guruṃ yajet ||

etena prati madhyayonyantarāle gurumaya pūjanaṃ tato viśeṣamudrāṃ darśayet | 
yathā yonyākhyā hṛdi vikṣiptā trailokya kṣobhaṇī bhavet | 

trailokye drāviṇī nāma mukhasthā parameśvari | 
bhrūmadhyasthā maheśāni trailokyā karṣaṇī bhavet ||

lalāṭasthā maheśāni trailokya vaśakāriṇī |
brahmarandhra sthitā saiva sarvāhlādakarī matā ||

pañcamudrā mayī devī yonimudrāṃ pradarśayet |
tataḥ ṣaḍaṅgāvaraṇaṃ paritaḥ pīṭhamadrije ||

agnīśā suravāyavya madhye dikṣu ca pūjayet |

paritaḥ pīṭhaṃ pīṭhasya paritaḥ | tathāgra koṇe ratiṃ paścāt pritiñcottara koṇake 
|| 

mano bhavāṃ dakṣakoṇe tato vānān samarcayet |

etena ratnairnama iti krameṇa pūjayet || vāṇapūjana sthānamuktaṃ || jñānārṇave ||

uttarasyāṃ dvayaṃ devi dakṣiṇasyāṃ dvayaṃ diśi |
agre caikaṃ krameṇaiva paṃcavāṇān samarcayet ||

paṃcakāmāstathā devi prāgrādi kramato yajet |
aṣṭayoniṣu deveśi krameṇa paripūjayet ||

etenā yoniṣu anaṅga kusumāyai nama ityādi krameṇa | tathā tato brāhmyādikaṃ 
yajet | 

yugma yugma prabhedenavasuvatre varānane | 
asitāṅgaṃ tathā brāhmīṃ ru ruṃ māheśvarīṃ śive ||

caṇḍaṃ kaumālikāṃ caiva krodhīśaṃ vaiṣṇavīntathā |
unmattañcaiva vārāhīṃ māhendrīñca kapālinaṃ ||

bhīṣaṇañcaiva cāmuṇḍāṃ saṃhāraṃ cāṣṭamaṃ yajet |
mahālakṣmīṃ maheśāni yugmāni paripūjayet ||

paścimādi krameṇaiva tataḥ pīṭhān samarcayet |

etena paścimādi krameṇa aṃ asitāṅga sahita brāhmai namaḥ ati aṣṭadaleṣu yajet | 
tataḥ pīṭhān samarcayet | 

kāmarūpañca malayaṃ tataḥ kolagirintathā | 
kulāntakañca cauvāntaṃ jālandhara mataḥ paraṃ ||

uḍḍiyānaṃ devakūṭaṃ pīṣṭantaṃ kraman kramāt |
granthimūleṣu saṃpūjyaṃ paścimādi krameṇa tat ||

tena granthi mūleṣu paścimādi krameṇa kāmarūpāya nama ityādi krameṇa pūjayet 
|| 

triśūlaṃ parito devi mātṛkā catu maṇḍale |
bhairavā daśasaṃpujyāḥ paścimādi kramācchive ||

hetukañcaiva(?) vetālaṃ tanmadhye tripurāntakaṃ |
agnijihvañca kālāntaṃ kapālina manantaraṃ ||

ekapādaṃ bhīmarūpaṃ malayaṃ hāṭakeśvaraṃ |

etena tri?śūlasya parita vṛttamaṇḍale hetukāya nama ityādi krameṇa pūjayet 
| 

caturasre lokapālān digvidikṣu samarcayet |

tathā 

kṣauṃkāraṃ bījamuccārya vaṭukāya namo vadet | 

yāṃkāra bījamuccārya yogiṇībhyo namastathā |

69a)

kṣāṃkāraṃ bījamucārya kṣatrapālāya vai namaḥ |
gaṃkāraṃ bījamuccārya tathā gaṇapatiṃ vadet ||

ṅentannamaḥ paścimādi dikṣu pūjyāḥ krameṇa ta |

tena ṅentaṃ caturthyantaṃ namontaṃ gaṇapati padamityarthaḥ || tathā caturasre tu 
saṃpūjyā vidikṣu parameśvari vāyavyādi krameṇaiva vakṣamāṇā maheśvari | 

vasavo dvādaśādityā rudrāñcaiva tataḥ śive |
sarvabhūtā varārohe krameṇa paripūjayet ||

etena vāyavyādi koṇeṣu vahutva caturthyā pūjanamiti tathā pūjāṃ vidhāya 
deveśīmupacāraiḥ prapūjayet || jñānārṇave ||

dhūpaṃ vistārayet samyaṅmūlamantraṃ hṛdi smaran |
vanaspati rasotpannā gandhāḍhyā gandha uttamaḥ ||

āghreyaḥ sarvadevānāṃ dhūpoyaṃ pratigṛhyatāṃ |
suprakāśā mahātejāḥ sarvatra timirāpahaḥ ||

sa bāhyābhyantaraṃ jyotirdīpoyaṃ prati gṛhyatāṃ |
tathaiva dīpamantroyaṃ vidyate parameśvari ||

ārātrika vidhiṃ kuryāt sarvakāmasamṛddhidaṃ |
kuṅkumeṇa likhet padmaṃ vasupatraṃ manoharaṃ ||

candrarūpaṃ cārukṛtvā tanmadhye masake śive |
dīpamekaṃ vinikṣipya vasupatreṣu dīpakān ||

abhimantrya maheśāni ratneśvaryā tataḥ paraṃ |
śrībījañca parābījaṃ vilikhya parameśvari ||

gasau cayavalāḥ paścādindrasthā kramataḥ śive |
vāmakarṇa samāyuktā nādabindu vibhūṣitāḥ ||

vījapañcakametantu pañcaratnāni sundari |
pūrvabīje vilomena ratneśī yaṃ navākṣarī ||

śrībījaṃ śrīṃ parā hrīṃ indro lakāraḥ vāmakarṇa ūkāraḥ pūrvabīja śrīṃ hrīṃ 
svarūpe tena śrīṁ hrīṁ glūṁ mlūṁ plūṁ vlūṁ llūṁ hrīṁ śrīṁ iti 
anayāratnaśvaryā abhimantrya tathā mūlamantreṇa cābhyarcya tataścārātrikaṃ 
caret | 

tataḥ pātraṃ samuddhṛtya mastakāntaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
navavāraṃ maheśāni tato nīrājanaṃ caret ||

samasta cakra cakreśi patra devi navātmake |
ārātrikamidaṃ divyaṃ gṛhāṇaparameśvari ||

nīrājane manurdevi vidyānte prakaṭī * *? ḥ |

etena mūlamantrānte samastacakretyādi mantraṇā rātrikaṃ samarpya cakramudrāṃ 
kuryād vāmahastāṅguṣṭha garbha dakṣiṇasya kaniṣṭhikāṃ | 

kaniṣṭhā garbha javāmudga * *? ṣṭaṃ viniḥkṣipet | 

anyānya yogato devi cakramudreyamīritā |
ārātrika vidhiṃ kṛtvā naivedyantu nivedayet ||

hemapātra gataṃ vindvaṃ paramānnaṃ susaṃskṛtaṃ |
paṃcadhā ṣaḍrasopetaṃ gṛhāṇa parameśvari ||

vidyānte parameśāni naivedyamanurīritaṃḥ |
pariṣicya tato devimi * * *? samācaret ||

mūlamantrānantaramanena valiṃ dadyādityarthaḥ | tathā mūlamantreṇa deveśi 
hunet pañcāhutiḥ kramāt | 

prāṇāpāṇau tathā vyāna udānaśca samānakaḥ |
etat svarūpaṃ jānīyād āhutīnāñca pañcakaṃ ||

ṣaḍāhutīḥ ṣa * ṅgeṣu nityā homoyamīritaḥ |

70a)

etena mūlena prāṇāya svāhā ityādi krameṇāhuti pañcakaṃ datvā 
ṣaḍaṅgamaṃtreṇa ṣaḍaṅgāhutīrjuhuyāt | tato yathāśakti japtvā 
guhyātiguhyetyādinā japaṃ samarpyasaṃhāramudrayā nirmālyaṃ gṛhītvā yathā 
mukhaṃ bhuñjīta || tathā 

nitya homaṃ vidhiṃ kṛtvā validāna vidhiñcaret |
iśānādiṣu koṇeṣu maṇḍalānāñcatuṣṭayaṃ ||

śrīcakramabhīto devi trikoṇaṃ vyomamālikhet |
athavā vāmabhāge tu maṇḍalāñcaikamālikhet ||

etena maṇḍalacatuṣṭayamekaṃ vā vidhāya śrīcakraṃ cakrasya paritastrikoṇaṃ 
vikhikhedityarthaḥ | tathā 

pūrvamantraiḥ samabhyarcya vaṭukādikamadrije | 

pūrvamantreṇa kṣauṁ vaṭukāya namaḥ ityādi mantreṇa saṃpūjya valiṃ dadyān 
mantro yathā | ehyehi devi putra vaṭukānte tu nātha ca | 

kapilānte jaṭābhāra bhāsvarānte triṇetra ca | 
jvālāmukha ca sarvānte vighnān nāśaya nāśaya ||

sarvopacārasahitaṃ valiṃ hṛhna dvidhā padaṃ |
vahni jāyānnito mantro vaṭukasya samīritaḥ ||

vahnijāyā svāhā tena ehyehi devi putra vaṭukanātha kapila 
jaṭābhāvabhāsuretreṇetra jñānāmukhasarvavighnānanāśayanāśayasarvopacāra 
sahitaṃ valiṃ gṛhna gṛhna svāhā iti mantraḥ | evaṃ yāṃ yoginībhyo namaḥ iti 
saṃpūjya || ūrddhaṃ brahmāṇḍato vādivi 

70b)

gagaṇatale bhūtale niskale vā pātāle vā tale vā pavana śalilayoryatratagra sthito vā | 
kṣetrapīṭhopapīṭhādiṣu ca kṛta padāḥ puṣpadhūpādiko na prītā devyaḥ 
sadānaḥ śubhavalividhināpāntu devendra vandyāḥ | 

etadante maheśāni yāṃ bījaṃ yoginī tataḥ |
bhyaḥ svāhā sarvavarṇānte yoginī padamālikhet ||

kavacaṃ cāstramālikhya vahnijāyāṃ punarlikhet |
anena manunā devi yoginī valimāharet ||

kavacaṃ hūṃ astraṃ phaṭ vahni jāyā svāhā tena yāṃ yoginībhyaḥ svāhā yoginī 
hūṁ phaṭ svāhā iti mantreṇa yoginī valiṃ dadyāt | tathā ṣaddīrgha 
svarasambhinnaṃ kṣakāraṃ punarālikhet | punaḥ kṣatrapadaṃ pālaṃ dhūpadīpādi 
cālikhet | sahitaṃ vali *? likhya gṛhna gṛhna tato vadet | 

vahnijāyānvito mantraḥ kṣetrapālasya sundari | 

tena kṣāṁ kṣatrapālāya nama iti saṃpūjya kṣāṃ? kṣīṃ? kṣuṃ? 
*? kṣauṃ kṣaḥ kṣetrapāladhūpadīpādi sahitaṃ valiṃ gṛhna gṛhna 
svāhā iti mantreṇa valiṃ dadyāt tathā * *? gūṃ trayamālikhya 
ṅentaṃ gaṇapatiṃ vadat | 

varānte varadāntai ca sarvānte janamālikhet |
mevaśa *? nayetyuktā valiṃ gṛhna dvidhā paraṃ ||

vahni * * *? to mantro gaṇapasya valiṃ haret |
vāmāṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ vaṭukasya valirbhaved ||

gāṃ gīṃ gūṃ gaṇapataye nvavarada sarva * *? meva śamānaya 
valiṃ gṛhna gṛhna svāhā iti mantreṇa gaṇapati valiṃ dadyāt ||

71a)

atha valimudrā ||

saiva vaṭukamudrā dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭha sahitā yoginīnāntu kathyate | tathā | 

aṃguṣṭha madhyamānāmā yonyākāreṇa yojayet | 
vāmāmuṣṭiṃ vidhāyādau tarjanīṃ savalāṃ kuru ||

anayā mudrayā devi kṣatrapāla valiṃ haret |
tathā muṣṭiṣu madhyasthā aṃgulī daṇḍavat kuru ||

gajadanta mahāmudrā gaṇapasya valirbhavet |
ato'dhikaṃ tantrāntareṣvanusandhyeyamiti ||

atha puraścaraṇaṃ || jñānārṇave || 

varṇalakṣaṃ japenmantraṃ taddaśāṃśaṃ hunecchive |

ityalambistaraḥ | yatrādhika saṃkhyā śruyate tatrādhika phalamiti bhāvaḥ ||

atha bhairavī mantrāḥ || bhairavyā api vālā ghaṭitatvādvālāyāḥ prāgupanyāsaḥ iti 
|| tathā jñānārṇave ||

paṃca siṃhāsana gatā kathaṃ sā tripurā parā |
kathayasva mahādevi kathaṃ siṃhāsanam bhavet ||

śivacandrau vahnisaṃsthau vāgbhavaṃ tadanantaraṃ |
kāmabījaṃ tathā devi śivacandrānvitantataḥ ||

pṛthvībījaṃ tu vahyāḍhyaṃ(?) tārtīyaṃ śṛṇu vallabhe |
kumāryāḥ parameśāni hitvā sargantu vaindavaṃ ||

tripurā bhairavī devi mahāsesyat pradā śive |

śivohakāraḥ candraḥ sakāraḥ vahnīrepha vāgbhavaṃ aiṁ kāmabījaṃ klīṁ 
śivacandrānvita hakāra sakāre yuktamityarthaḥ tanmadhyastha pṛthvībījaṃ 
lakāraṃ vahnī rephaḥ tadāḍhyaṃ tadyuktaṃ tena svatantra varṇatva māyāti | tena 
hasakalara īṁ iti siddhyati | kumāryā vālāyā tārtīyaṃ śaktibījaṃ vilikhya sargaṃ 
hitvā vaindavaṃ kuryāt kimbhūtaṃ śivacandrānvitaṃ ityanu ṣaḍgaṃ vidhāya 
tṛtīya bījamuddharanti | devanāthādayaḥ hasa auṁ iti atha kathaṃ visarga 
parityāgaḥ sampatpradā bhairavīyaṃ tatra tārtīya bījake | 

bindu hitvā tataḥ sargaṃ niḥkṣipet surasundari 

ityatra vindoreva parityāgaḥ śruyate iti cennaḥ atra sargaṃ hitvā binduṃ 
niḥkṣipediti vyavasthitānvayenopapattiriti tanna caramabījavarṇa catuṣṭayāpatti 
anusaṃgāvattirvyavahitānvayāvattiśca kumāryāstārtīyaṃ sauḥ tatra 
śivacandrānuṣaṅgeha sasaumiti varṇacatuṣṭayāpatti anyadbādhaka dvayaṃ 
vyaktameva visargabādhavacanī satvācca ||

sāvadāyāṃ manaḥ || sargendukhaṇḍa vā nidarśanācca vayantu kāmabījaṃ tathā 
devi ityatra kumāryā vāgbhavaṃ yathā śiva candrasthaṃ tathā | śivacandrasthaṃ 
kumāryāḥ kāmabījamiti iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ kāmabījastha pṛthvībījaṃ tu 
vahnyāḍhyāṃ tataḥ paraṃ kumāryāḥ tārtīyaṃ śiva candrānvitaṃ kṛtvā 
uddharet sasarga aindavaṃ vā hitvā kimvā asargaṃ aiṃdavaṃ hitvā parityajya 
aiṃdavaṃ indoridaṃ sakāraḥ sasargaṃ śaktibījasthaṃ asa *? 
śivacandretyādinādvṛtaṃ hitvā uddharedityarthaḥ 

72a)

nanu sasargaṃ hitvetyatra sargasyāpi pakṣe tyāgaḥ kathaṃ na syāditi cedakasminneva 
tantre mantra bhedāpatteḥ | ataḥ sakāratyāga evavikalpapūrvasthaṃ vā parasthaṃ 
veti ata evavarṇa trayarūpaṃ caramabījaṃ bhavati ato nānuṣaṅgo 
vyavasthitānvayaśca binduṃ hitvā tataḥ sargaṃ niḥkṣipedityatra visargasyaiva 
prāpānikatvāt | ata eva ripu nikṛntinī mantroddhāre dvitīyabījoddhāre sampat 
pradāya bhairavyāḥ kāmarājaṃ tadeva hītyuktvā sadāśivañca bījañcetyuktaṃ 
ataḥ sadāśiva bīje visargasadbhāvo varṇatraya rūpatā ca ata eva śāpoddhāra 
mukhenoktaṃ sārasamuccaye || śaptā pūrvaṃ yena vidyeya māyā devyā tena 
prāṇahīnā bhavatsā | vidyā mūlotpattireṣā mayoktā jñātavyeyaṃ sarvathā 
siddhikāmaiḥ || adyāvālā śivaśaktibīja mata evaśambhunā nihitaṃ 
tayoruparipūrvabījayoḥ | akulaṃ paropari ca madhya mādhava dahanaṃ | tataḥ 
prabhṛti sorcitābhavat || śivaśaktibījaṃ hasarūpaṃ pūrvabījayorupari ataḥ paraṃ 
parāsakārastadupari akulaṃ hakāraḥ madhyame kāmarāja adhare dahanaṃ rephaḥ 
etena sarvamano kulamiti dik ||

tathā 

dhyānamasyāḥ pravakṣyāmi mahāsampat pradā śive | 
ātāmrārka sahasrābhāṃ śaraccandrajaṭāsphuret || 

kirīṭa ratna vilasannetra vimbitamauktikāṃ | 

śravadrudhirapaṅkā * muṇḍamālāvibhūṣitāṃ |

72b)

nayanatraya lobhāḍhyāṃ pūrṇendu vadanā matāṃ(?) | 
muktāhāralatārājanmīnonnata ghanastanīṃ ||

raktāmvara parīdhānāṃ yauvanonmattarūpiṇīṃ |
pustakaṃ cobhayaṃ vāme dakṣiṇe cākṣamālikāṃ ||

varadāna ratāṃ nityaṃ(?) mahāsa * * *? smaret |
nyāsapūjādikaṃ pūrvaṃ kumāryā eva suvrate ||

tathā vāgbhavaṃ prathamaṃ bījaṃ jīvaprāṇasamanvitaṃ |
sakalā bhuvaneśānī dvitīyaṃ(?) bīja(?) muddhṛtaṃ ||

jīvaṃ prāṇaṃ vahnisaṃsthaṃ śakrasvaravibhūṣitaṃ |
visargāḍhyaṃ maheśāni vidyā trailokya mohinī ||

caitanya bhairavī nāma cetanā niścale śive |

jīvaḥ sakāraḥ prāṇo hakāraḥ vāgbhavamaikāraḥ tena saha aiṃ sakalā sakala 
varṇasthā bhuvaneśānī hrīṃ tena sakala hrīṃ jīvaprāṇa vahnayor *? 
khyātāḥ śakrasvaraḥ aukāraḥ tena hasara auḥ ||

atha dhyānaṃ ||

udyad bhāskara rūpābhāṃ nānālaṅkālabhūṣitāṃ |
mukuṭojvalāllasaccandra(?) * * *? 
ścarāmvitāṃ(?) ||

pāśāṅkuśa dharāṃ nityaṃ(?) vāmahasta(?) 
kapālinīṃ |
varadābhaya śobhāḍhyāṃ pīnonnata ghanastanīṃ ||

evaṃ dhyātvā yajed devīṃ siṃhāsana gatāṃ śive | 
dvirāvṛtyā ṣaḍaṅgāni nyaset sarvāṅga(?) lakṣaṇaṃ ||

yantramasyāḥ pravakṣyāmi devyāstrailokya mohanaṃ |
trikoṇañcaiva ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vasupretaṃ varānane ||

caturasraṃ caturdvāramevaṃ maṇḍalamālikhet |

73a)

tatra vāhya yajed devīṃ pūrvavat parameśvarīṃ |
ratyādikāṃstano devīṃ pūrvavat paripūjayet ||

agre vasantaṃ vāme tu kāmo devaṃ varānane |
candraṃ dakṣiṇa dik koṇe vānān pūrvavadadrije ||

ḍākinīṃ śākinīñcaiva kākinīṃ lākinīntataḥ |
rākiṇīṃ hākinīñcaiva paścimādi kramād yajet ||

parabhūt sārasau caiva śukameghā hvayau punaḥ |
apāṅgabhrūvilāsau ca bhārahārau ca pūrvavat ||

pūrvasiṃhāsana gatā kathitā vīravandite || tathā 

etasyā eva vidyāyā bījadvayamudāhṛtaṃ |
tadante parameśāni nitya klinne madadrave ||

etasyā eva tārtīyaṃ rudrārṇā parameśvarī |

etasyāścaitanya bhairavyā bījadvayānantaraṃ nityakinne mada draveti vilikhya 
caramabījamuddharedityarthaḥ | ata evoktaṃ || 

dhyānapūjādikandevi caitanyā eva pūrvavat |
trikoṇe tu viśeṣosti kathayāmi varānaghe ||

agrakoṇa krameṇaiva nityakinnāṃ madadravāṃ |
pūrvasiṃhāsanā muktā dakṣiṇasiṃhāsanamāha || jñānārṇave ||

śivacandrau mādanasthaṃ vāntaṃ vahni samanvitaṃ |
śaktibhinnaṃ bindunāda kalāḍhyaṃ vāgbhavaṃ śive ||

sampat pradāyā bhairavyā kāmarājantadeva hi |
sadāśivañca bījañca mahāsiṃhāsanasya tu ||

eṣā siṃhāsane devi dakṣiṇe parameśvari |

śivacandrau hakāre sakārau mādanaḥ kakāraḥ vāntaṃ lakāraḥ vahnī rephaḥ śakti 
rekāraḥ tena hasakala aiṁ śeṣa dvayaṃ sampat pradāyāḥ ||

73b)

udyat sūrya sahasrābhāṃ candracūḍāṃ(?) trilocanāṃ | 

nānālaṅkālasubhagāṃ sarva vairinikṛntinīṃ |
śravadrudhiramuṇḍālī kalitāṃ raktavāsanīṃ ||

evaṃ dhyātvā maheśāni pūjāmaṇḍalamālabhet(?) |
trikoṇandevi ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vṛttāṣṭadalanīrajaṃ ||

vṛttaṃ bhūmaṇḍalārīta meṣā tripura yantrikā |
vāmāṃ jeṣṭhāñca raudrīñca kālīñca va?ralocane ||

kalāḍhyāñca valāḍhyāñca yajed vikaraṇīntathā |
valapramathanīṃ sarva bhūtānāṃ padamāharet ||

damanīñca tathā yaṣṭvā manonmana padānvitāṃ |
aghore vāgbhave coktrā(?) ghore ca bhuvaneśvarīṃ || 

ghoraghoratare caiva rakṣamāṃ rakṣāṃ(?) *? caret | 

sarvataḥ sarvva sarveśi devyā bījayutāṃ likhet |
triṃśadbhiśca tribhirvarṇairvidyeyaṃ kathitā śive ||

anena manunā devi tataḥ siṃhāsanaṃ yajet |

etena vāmādi sarvabhūtamanonmanadamanī paryantaṃ sampūjya anena manunā 
siṃhāsanaṃ yajet | devī bījaṃ hrīṃ tena aghore vāgbhave * *? 
bhuvaneśvari ghoraghoratare rakṣamāṃ rakṣa sarvataḥ sarvasarveśī hrīṁ iti tathā 

bhūdipalokapālāṃśca(?) sāyudhān parameśvari | 
dvitīya siṃhāsana * *? pūjayet parameśvarīṃ || 

dakṣiṇa siṃhāsanā muktvā paścima siṃhāsanā māhaḥ || 

jñānārṇave ||

ḍākinī rākinī bīja lākinī kākinī yuge |

74a)

bījānyā hṛtya deveśi yojayeccandra sūryayoḥ |
ādyamaiṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ anyadīṅkāramaṇḍitaṃ ||

śakrasvarānvitaṃ devi tārtīyaṃ bījamālikhet |
bindunādakalākrāntaṃ tṛtīyaṃ śailasambhave ||

eṣā vidyā maheśāni paścimāsanagā bhavet |

ḍākinī ḍakāraḥ rākinī rephaḥ lākinī lakāraḥ kākinī kakāraḥ etāni 
bījācandrasūryayo rupari sakāra hakārayorupari ākṛtya kūṭatraya rūpamuddharet | 
tatra ādyaṃ aiṃkāraḥ saṃyuktaṃ tena ḍaralakasaha aiṁ anyadīṃkāramaṇḍitaṃ 
tena ḍaralakasaha īṃśakrasvarānvitaṃ devi tārtīyaṃ śakrasvara aukāraḥ 
vindunādādi yuktaṃ tena ḍaralakasaha auḥ tṛtīyaṃ śailasambhava ityanukte 
pūrvabījadvaye bindudvayā patteḥ atra tṛtīya bīje visargopi dṛśyate taduktaṃ 
bindunādānvitaṃ cānyadyugmamantyaṃ visargavaditi tathā ca dhyānaṃ ||

indragopa prabhāṃ devi javākuṅkumasannibhāṃ |
muṇḍamālāvalī ramyāṃ vālasūrya samatviṣāṃ ||

suvarṇa kalasākāra pīnonnata payodharāṃ |
pāśāṅkuśau pustakañca tathā ca japamālikāṃ ||

evaṃ dhyātvā yajed devīṃ mānasairupacārakaiḥ |
dvirāvṛtyā ṣaḍaṅgāni vidhāya parameśvari ||

yantramasyā varārohe trikoṇaṃ tatpuṭaṃ likhet |
vahiraṣṭa dalaṃ padmaṃ ravi patraṃ tato likhet ||

caturasraṃ caturdvāramevaṃ maṇḍalamālikhet |

74b)

pūrvoktena vidhānena pūjayen nitya bhairavīṃ |
etasyā eva vidyāyā ṣaḍvarṇan kramatasthitān ||

viparītān vada prauḍhe vidyeyaṃ bhogamokṣadā |

etasyā vidyāyāḥ ḍakārādi * *? nyantān varṇān hakārādi ḍakārān 
kṛtvā uddharedityarthaḥ tena hasakala re ḍa aiṃ ityādi krameṇoddhāraḥ | 

nityā śrībhairavī devī ripu(?) hārāpahāriṇī |
nyāsapūjādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvavat parikalpayet ||

paścimasiṃhāsano muktvā uttare siṃhāsanamāha || jñānārṇave ||

ha *? dyaṃ vāgbhavañcādyaṃ(?) hasa(?) 
kānte sureśvari |
bhūbījaṃ bhuvaneśānī dvitīyaṃ bījamuddhṛtaṃ ||

śivacandrau maheśāni bhuvaneśī ca bhairavī |
vāgbhavaṃ aiṃkāraḥ haśca(?) saśca hasau(?) 
*? vādya(?) yasyetyarthaḥ ||

tena hasa aiṃ hasakānte hasakavarṇānte bhūbījaṃ lakāraḥ bhuvaneśānī krīṃ tena 
hasakala hrīṃ śivacandrau hakāra sakārau? aukāra visargā *? 
tena(?) hasa(?) auḥ(?) || atha kathamatra 
aukāro labhyate visargaśceti cettantrāntare surasāt ||

taduktaṃ || vārāhītantre || haṃsāstrayopī hasakān yuktāne svarddha(?) 
paṃktisvarasaṃvibhinnāḥ | madhye viriñcīndra harāgni yuktā ādyau sandūparato 
visargīti haṃso hakāraḥ te traya sakāra yuktāra * * * *? 
kāraḥ klīvā nāmagananāt | tadarddha īkāraḥ paṃktisvara aukāraḥ viriñciḥ 
kakāraḥ indro lakāraḥ haro hakāraḥ agnīrephaḥ ityeka vākyatayaiva labhyata

75a)

iti devanāthādibhiruktaṃ bhuvaneśīti bhairavī viśeṣaṇamiti vastu tastu tantra 
bhedena mantra bhedādaika vākyataiva na saṅgacchate ||

jñānārṇave ||

paṃktisvara bodhakā bhāvāt | tathā ca | śivacandrau bhuvaneśī ca bhairavī 
viśeṣaṇe bhuvaneśī padavai arthyāpattiriti tena hasahrīṃ iti caramabījaṃ | tathā 
madhyabījena deveśi ṣaḍdīrghasvara bhedinā | 

ṣaḍaṅgāni pravinyasya dhyāyed devīṃ caturbhujāṃ |
javākuṅkumasaṅkāśāṃ dāḍimī kusumopamāṃ ||

candrarekhā jaṭājūṭāṃ trinetrāṃ raktasasīṃ |
nānālaṅkāra śubhagāṃ pīnonnata ghanastanīṃ ||

pāśāṅkuśa varābhītiṃ dhyayantīṃ śivāṃ śive |
evaṃ dhyātvārcayed devīṃ yantroddhāraṃ ca pūrvavat ||

caitanya bhairavī devī mantravat parameśvari |
ratyādyāḥ pūjayed devi trikoṇe tadanantaraṃ ||

brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca pūjyāstatraiva sundari |
teneyaṃ prāpitā vidyā dhanarājaḥ saco bhavet ||

tathā 

tārañca bhuvaneśānī śrībījaṃ kāmarājakaṃ |
hṛdante bhagavatyarṇān māheśvari padaṃ vadet ||

annapūrṇe agnijāyā ca vijñeyā viṃśadakṣarī |
anayā sadṛśī vidyā siddhidā nāsti bhūtale ||

tena oṃ hrīṁ śrīṁ klīṃ bhagavati māheśvari maheśvari annapūrṇe namaḥ svāhā 
||

75b)

tathā 

bhuvaneśvari bhairavyā bhedāntaramathocyate |
sahādyā saiva deveśi tadā sā sakaleśvarī ||

dhyānapūjādikaṃ sarvaṃ etasyā eva pārvati |

sāsaṃsaṃpat pradā sa *? dyā cet sakareśvarī bhave tīrthaḥ bhuvaneśvarīti 
samvodhanaṃ | ata evoktaṃ svarasamuccaye | bhairavīya muditā kula pūrvā deśikai 
yadi bhavet kula * *? saiva śīghra phaladeviti akulaṃ hakāraḥ kulaṃ 
sakāraḥ | tathā hasa vādyau bhairavī sā * * * * *? 
pārvati || etasyā eva vidyāyā ādyantara * * *? ti | tadeyaṃ 
parameśāni nāmnā sakala siddhidā || sampat pradā bhairavīva dhyāna pūjā * 
* * * * * * *? bhairavī sā samyat pradā bhairavī 
etasyāstriṣu bījeṣu ādyante ādi bījasyānte vāgbhave repha varjite sati sakalasiddhidā 
nāma bhavati * * * *? bhairavī mantrasya prathama kūṭe 
*? varjite satītyarthaḥ etat triṣu bījeṣu hasa vādyāṃ madhya parama bīja 
rephā bhāvāt ||

atha * * * *? sāradā tilake ||

dīkṣāṃ prā * *? yenmantraṃ tatvalakṣaṃ jitendriyaḥ | 
puṣpairbhānu sahasrāṇi juhuyād brahma vṛkṣajaiḥ || 

tatvāni dvādaśa tatvārddha lakṣāvapīti prapañcasāraḥ | sārasamucca(##?
tarujaistrimadhvaktairhomaṃ tadanudiśivāreṇa juhuyāditi tathā 
mārgesminnavalakṣamakṣaya padaprāptyau 

76a)

tato lakṣayediti nānātacacaneṣu nānā prakāraḥ śruyate | tatra devanātha * 
* * * * * * * * mantroktameva puraścaraṇaṃ 
tatva lakṣamiti | tanna tatvarakṣanyūnāti rikta pratipādaka vacanānāṃ nirarthakatvā 
patteriti || vayantu tripurāsārasamuyokta lakṣameva puraścaraṇaṃ 
adhikapratipādakavacanamadhika phalārthaṃ ata evākṣaya pada prāptaye ityādi 
yathā śrībhairavī tathetyabhidhānācca sarvatra taddharmāti daśa iti bhāvaḥ ||

śrīramāramaṇadevaśarmaṇānirmite gamasāranirṇayo bhairavī 
tripuravālikāmanūddhārakastu paṭalo dvitīyakaḥ ||


atra yadyapi mahāvidyā nirūpaṇānantaraṃ siddhi vidyā nirūpaṇamevamucyate || 
tathāpi athāntara vidyānāṃ ubhayācāra saṃvalitatvādubhaya madhya eva 
nirūpaṇamucitamiti || tatra prathamaṃ bhuvaneśvarī mantrā likhyante sāradā 
tilakānusāreṇa yathā || 

atha vakṣye jagaddhātrī madhunā bhuvaneśvarīṃ |
brahmādayopi yāṃ jñātvā lebhire śriyamūrjitāṃ ||

nakulīśogni mārūḍho vāmanetrārddha candrabhāk |
nakulīśohakāraḥ agnīrephaḥ vāmanetrā dīrgha īkāraḥ tena hrīṃ || atha dhyānaṃ ||

udyadinadyutimindu kirīṭāṃ tuṅga kucāṃ nayana trayasaṃyuktāṃ | smeramukhīṃ 
varadāṅkuśa pāśāṃbhīti karāṃ prabhajed bhuvaneśīṃ ||

76b)

ṣaḍdīrgha bhājābījena kuryādaṅgāni ṣaṭkramāt |
hrāṃ hṛdayāya nama iti krameṇa || tathā puraścaraṇaṃ ||

japenmantraṃ tato mantrī dvātriṃśallakṣamānataḥ ||

atrāpi śāpaḥ ||

yathā muṇḍamālāyāṃ bhuvaneśī mahāvidyā devarājena vaipurā | 

ārādhitā ca vidyeyaṃ rājena nāma mohitā |
ekākṣarī bījahīnā vāgbhavenoddhṛtā purā ityādi darśanāt || ata eva 
vāgbhavādye śāpo nāstītyarthaḥ || tathā 

vāgbhavaṃ śambhuvanicatā ramābīja trayātmakaṃ |
mantraṃ samuddharen mantrī trivarga phalasādhanaṃ ||

vāgbhavaṃ aiṃkāraḥ śambhu vanitā hrīṃ ramā śrīṃmiti || atha dhyānaṃ || 

sindūrāruṇa vigrahāṃ trinayanāṃ mānikya maulisphurattārā nāyake śeṣarāṃ 
smitamukhī mā pīna vakṣoruhāṃ |
pāṇibhyāṃ maṇipūrṇaṃ rasakaṃ raktotpalaṃ vibhratīṃ saumyāṃ 
varhaghaṭasthale vyacaraṇāṃ dhyāyet parāmambikāṃ ||

puraścaraṇaṃ || 

ravilakṣaṃ japenmantraṃ pāyasairmadhurā plutaiḥ |
daśāṃśaṃ homayen mantrī yajet pīṭhe samāhitaḥ ||

tathā vāgbījapuṭitā mayā vidyeyaṃ tryakṣarī bhavet |
vāgbīja saiṃkāraḥ tena puṭitā māyābījamityarthaḥ ||

māyā hrīṃ śyāmāṅgīṃ śaśiśeṣarāṃ nijakarairjñānañca raktotpalaṃ 
ratnāḍhyaṃ casakaṃ paraṃ bhayaharaṃ saṃvibhratīṃ sāsvatīṃ |

77a)

muntāsta(?)ralasatpayodharanatāṃ netra trayollāsinīṃ vandehaṃ 
surapūjitāṃ haravadhūṃ raktāravinda sthitāṃ ||

madhyena dīrghayuktena vākpuṭena prakalpayet ||

aiṃ hrāṃ aiṃ hṛdayāya nama iti krameṇa || atha puraścaraṇaṃ ||

tatvalakṣaṃ japenmantraṃ taddaśāṃśaṃ hunet tataḥ |
tathā ananto bindusaṃyukto māyā brahmāgni tāravān ||

pāśādi tryakṣaro mantraḥ sarvavaśya phala pradaḥ |

anantaḥ ākāraḥ māyā hrīṃ brahmā kakāraḥ agnīrephaḥ tāraḥ oṃkāraḥ tena 
krāṃ iti || atha dhyānaṃ ||

varāṅkuśau pāśamabhīti mudrāṃ karairvahantīṃ kamalāsanasthāṃ |
vālārka koṭi pratimā trinetrāṃ bhajehamādyāṃ bhuvaneśvarīntāṃ ||

atha ananto vindusaṃyukta ityādinā akṣaratraya rūpamantro bhavati sa 
evapāśādiścet caturakṣara rūpatā bhavitu marhatīti cet ādiśabdotra avayavārthaḥ 
tena sa bindvananta brahmāgni tāravān mantraḥ pāśādiḥ tryakṣarasyādya vayavaḥ 
pāśa ityarthaḥ ||

tathā puraścaraṇaṃ ||

haviṣya bhuk japenmantraṃ tatvalakṣaṃ jitendriyaḥ |
saṃhārasṛṣṭi mārgeṇa mātṛkānyasta vigrahaḥ ||

mantra nyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryād devatā bhāva siddhaye |

tena sundarī mantrokta krameṇa prātaḥkṛtya sandhyādi snānādikaṃ 
bhūtaśuddhyādikaṃ ca vidhāya anuloma vilomeṇa mātṛkā kuryāt tato ṛṣyādi 
kanyāsaṃkṛtvā mantra nyāsaṃ kuryād viśeṣānaṃbhidhānādekatra nirṇīta iti 
nyāyāt | tathā 

ṛṣiḥ śaktirbhavecchandā gāyatrī devatā manoḥ |
kathitā svarasaṃhena deveśī bhuvaneśvarī ||

tena śiro vadane hṛdatsu ṛṣicchando devatā vinyasya mantra nyāsaṃ kuryāt | 
yathā 

hṛllekhāṃ mūrddhni vadane gaganāṃ hṛdayāmbuje | 
rakṣāṃ karālikāṃ guhye mahacchuṣmāṃ pada dvaye ||

tena mūrddhni hṛllekhāyai namaḥ | ityādi krameṇa nyaset | tathā 

brāhmāṇaṃ vinyased bhāle gāyatryā sahasaṃyuktaṃ |
sāvitryāsaṃyutaṃ viṣṇuṃ kapaule dakṣiṇe nyaset ||

vāgīśvaryā samāyukte vāmagaṇḍe maheśvaraṃ |
nyasecchriyādhanapatiṃ vāmakarṇāgrake punaḥ ||

mukhe nyasya nyaset pṛṣṭhe gaṇeśaṃ parito nyaset |
savyakarṇau parinidhī kanagaṇḍāntarālayoḥ ||

nyastavyau vadane padmaṃ bhūyaścaitān tatau nyaset |
kaṇṭhamūlastana dvande vāmāṃśe hṛdayāmbuje ||

savyāṃśe pārśve yugale nābhideśe ca deśikaḥ |
bhālāṃśa pārśvajaṭhare pārśvāśāparaka hṛdi ||

brāhmītyāḍhyāstato nyasyā vidhinā prokta lakṣaṇāḥ |
malena vyāpakaṃ nyasya dehī devīṃ vicintayet ||

etena pūrvoktarūpāṃ dhyāyeta mānaso pacāra pūjanaṃ viśeṣārgha sthāpanañca 
tato yantraṃ purato nidhāya āvāhanaṃ mudrādi bhirāvāhanaṃ vidhāya pādyādīni 
dadyādityācāraḥ yantroddhāraṃ yathā ||

78a)

padmamaṣṭalaṃ vāhyai vṛttaṃ ṣoḍaśabhirdalaiḥ |
vilikhet karṇikā madhye trikoṇamati sundaraṃ ||

tataḥ saṃpūjayet pīṭhaṃ navaśakti samanvitaṃ |
jayā ca vijayā caiva ajitā cāparājitā ||

vidyā vilāsinī dogdhrī aghorāmaṅgalā nava |
bījāḍhya māsanaṃ kṛtvā mūrtiṃ mūlena kalpayet ||

bījādyaṃ māyābījādyamityarthaḥ | tathā 

tasyāṃ sampūjayed devī māvāhyā varaṇaiḥ kramāt |
prāṅmadhyayāmya saumyeṣu paścime ca yathā kramaṃ ||

hṛllekhādyāḥ samabhyarcya pañcādbhuta taraḥ prabhā |
varapāśāṅkuśābhīti dhāriṇyomita bhūṣaṇāḥ ||

sthāneṣu pūrvamukteṣu pūjayed aṅga devatāḥ |
indrakoṇe ca gāyatrīṃ brahmaṇā saha pūjayet ||

vakṣaḥ koṇe ca sāvitrīṃ pūjayed viṣṇunā saha |
sarasvatī vāyukoṇe rudreṇa saha pūjayet ||

vahnikoṇe mahālakṣmīṃ dhanadena tadaṃkagāṃ |
vāruṇe kāma devena samāśliṣṭāṃ ratiṃ yajet ||

aiśānyāṃ pūjayet samyak vighnarājaṃ priyānvitaṃ |
digaṃvarī tataḥ pūjyā saṃsārārṇava tāriṇī ||

kārṇakāyāṃ nidhī pūjyau ṣaṭkoṇasyātha pārśvayoḥ |
anaṅga kusumā paścādanaṅga madanā tathā ||

anaṅgadhāriṇī tatva manaṅga madanāturā |
tato bhuvanapālākhyā gagaṇavegā tataḥ paraṃ ||

śaśirekhā galarekhā pūjyatā hyaṣṭa śaktayaḥ |
tataḥ ṣoḍaśa patreṣu karālī vikalyumā ||

sarasvatī śrīrddhi doṣā lakṣmī satyā smṛtirdhṛtiḥ |
śraddhā medhāmatiḥ kāntirādyāḥ ṣoḍaśa śaktayaḥ ||

padmād vahiḥ samabhyarcyāḥ śaktayaḥ paricārikāḥ |
prathamānaṅgarūpāsyādanaṅga madano tathā ||

madanāturātha bhuvane vegā bhuvana pālikā |
syāt sarvaśiśirānaṅga vadanānaṅga mekhalā ||

casakaṃ tālavṛrtañca tāmbūlaṃ cchatramujvalaṃ |
cāmaracāṃśubhaṃ puṣpaṃ vibhraṇākarapaṅkajaiḥ ||

evaṃ saṃpūjya deveśīṃ sādhakaḥ kiṃ na sādhayet |

evamuktasthāneṣu saṃpūjya mūlena tristarpayitvā prāṇāyāmaṃ vidhāya yathā 
vibhavaṃ pūjayitvā japtvā japaṃ samarpya saṃhāre mudrayo nimālyaṃ gṛhītvā 
aiśānyāṃśa nirmālya vāsinyai namaḥ iti saṃpūjya visarjayediti saṃkṣepaḥ ||

atha lakṣmī yantrāḥ || śāradā tilake ||

atha vakṣye śriyo mantrān śrīsaubhāgya phala pradān |
yasyāḥ kaṭākṣa mātreṇa trailokyamapi labhyate ||

vāntaṃ vahni samāyuktaṃ vāmanetrandu saṃyutaṃ |
bījametacchriyaḥ proktaṃ cintāmaṇimivāparaṃ ||

vāntaṃ śakāraḥ vahnīrepha vāmanetra ikāraḥ | tena śrīṃ ||

atha dhyānaṃ ||

kāntyākāñcana sannibhāṃ haragiri prakhyaiścaturbhirbhujairhastautkṣipta 
hiranmayā mṛtaghaṭairāsicyanānāṃ śriyaṃ |
vibhrāṇāṃ varamabjayugmamabhayaṃ hastaiḥ kirīṭo jvalāṃ 
kṣaumāvaddhanitamba bimbagalitāṃ vanderavinda sthitāṃ ||

79a)

ṣaṭdīrghabījena kuryādaṅgāni ṣaṭ kramāt |
bhānu lakṣaṃ japenmantraṃ dīkṣito vijitendriyaḥ ||

tathā 

vāgbhavaṃ vanitā viṣṇormāyāmakaraketanaḥ |
caturbījātmako mantraścaturvarga phala pradaḥ ||

bhānulakṣaṃ haviṣyāśī japedante hunedapi |

vāgbhavaṃ aiṃkāraḥ viṣṇorvanitā śrīṃ māyā hrīṃ makaraketanaḥ | klīṃ ||

atha dhyānaṃ ||

mānikya pratimaprabhāṃ himanibhaistuṅgaiścaturbhirbhujairhastāgrāhita ratna 
kumbha salilairāsicyamānaṃ sadā |
hastāgrairvaradānamambujayugābhītirdadhānaṃ haraḥ kāntāṃ kāṃkṣita 
pārijātalatikāṃ vande sarojāsanāṃ ||

ṛṣirbhṛgūnivṛṭ(gūṭeti pāṭhaḥ)cchando devatā śrīḥ samīritāḥ |
ṣaḍdīrgha yuktamadhyena kuryādaṅgāni ṣaṭkramāt ||

śrāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādi krameṇa | 

tathā atra samyak yajet pīṭhaṃ navaśakti samanvitaṃ |
vibhūtirunnatiḥ kānti sṛṣṭiḥ kīrtiśca sannatiḥ ||

vyutaṣṭirutkṛṣṭi ṛddhiśca saṃproktā navaśaktayaḥ |
tatrā vāhya yajed devīṃ parivāra samanvitāṃ ||

bījāḍhyamāsanaṃ datvā mūrtiṃ mūlena kalpayet |

iha tantrādau yadyapi bhūtaśuddhyādi na śruyate tathāpi sāmānya 
paṭaloddhṛtatvādūhanīyaṃ || tathā ca pūrvavat prātaḥ kṛtya snāna sandhyārgha 
sthāpana bhūtaśuddhi lipi nyāsādi vidhāya viśeṣa nyāsa pūrvādikaṃ karttavyaṃ 
pratthagaurava bhayāt sarva * *? likhyate | teṣāmāvaśyakatvāditi | 
tathā bījāḍhya mānasaṃ datvā mūrtiṃ yathoktaṃ ca parikalpya deyaṃ | yajed 
devīmityane naiva vinā upacāreṇa pūjābhājā?padyādikamūhanīyaṃ 
āvāhyetyādinā āvāhanādi mudrā sambadhyateti tathā yajet pūrva vadaṅgāni 
ṣaḍaṅgaṃ mantreṇa pūrvavat ṣaḍaṅgaṃ pūjayet | tathā viśvandhanamvarcayet 
tataḥ digbandhaneṣu aṣṭadaleṣu catudaśasu | āvaraṇānāmaṣṭasaṃkhyatvāt | yathā 
vāsudevaṃ śaṅkarṣaṇaṃ | pradyumnamani ruddha(?)kaṃ | 
viśeṣaṇamāha ||

hemapīta tamālendra nīlābhaṃ pītavāsasaṃ |
śaṃkhacakradharaṃ devaṃ gadāpadmadharaṃ tathā ||

kamalaṃ salitaṃ rūpo gugguluñca kuraṇḍakaṃ || 4 ||

tathā yajecchaṃkha nidhī devyādakṣiṇa kramayogataḥ |
dalāgreṣu yajed deva varākyādyāḥ samantataḥ ||

valākīni malā caiva kamalā ca na mālikā |
vibhīṣikā mālikā ca śaṃkarī ca sumālikā ||

paṅkaje dvaya dhāriṇye muktāhārasamaprabhāḥ |
lokeśān pūjayedante vajrā?dyastrāṇi tad vahiḥ ||

itthaṃ saṃpūjya mūlena triḥsaṃpūjyasaṅketa mudrayā tarpayitvā prāṇāyāmaṃ 
vidhāya yathāśakti japtvā guhyātiguhyetyādinā japaṃ samarpyastulā nalā 
saṃhāra mudrayā nirmālyaṃ gṛhītvā visarjayediti saṃkṣepaḥ |

80a)

itthaṃ yo bhajate nityaṃ devīṃ vidhinā sādhakottamaḥ |
dhanadhānya samṛddhiḥ syācchriya māpnotya nindatāṃ ||

vakṣaḥ prāmāṇe salile sthitvā mantramimaṃ japet |
trilakṣaṃ sayato mantrī devīṃ dhyātvārka maṇḍale ||

sa bhavedalpa kālena ramāyā vasatiḥ sthirāṃ |
viṣṇugehañca nindasya mūlamadhyāsya mantravit ||

trilakṣaṃ prajapenmantrī vāñchitaṃ labhate dhanaṃ |
etena trilakṣameva puraścaṇamiti saṃkṣepaḥ ||

athānna pūrṇamantraḥ || śāradā tilake ||

māyā hṛdbhagavatyante māheśvari padaṃ vadet |
annapūrṇe vahnijāyā manuḥ saptadaśākṣaraḥ ||

māyā hrīṃ hṛnnamaḥ vahnijāyā svāhā ||

tataḥ prāgukta krameṇa prātaḥkṛtya snāna sandhyā sāmānyārgha sthāpana 
bhūtaśuddhi prāṇāyāma mātṛkā nyāsādikaṃ vidhāya ṛṣyādi nyāsaṃ 
kuryāt | ṛṣirbrahmā tṛṣṭupchando'nna pūrṇā ca devatāḥ iti śiro vadana 
hṛtsundasya mūlena ṣaḍaṅga nyāsaṃ kuryāt || taduktaṃ 

mūlenāṅgāni vinyasya tato devīṃ vicintayet |

raktāṃ vicitra vasanāṃ navacandracūḍāṃ annapradāne niratāṃstama 
bhāranamrāṃ | nṛtyanta sindu sakalo bharaṇaṃ vilokya hṛṣṭāṃ bhaje 
bhagavatīṃ bhava duḥkhahantrīṃ ||

iti dhyātvā mūlenā vāhya pādyādibhiḥ saṃpūjya mūlānte saparivārāyai 
annapūrṇāyai nama iti | 

80b)

tathā saparivārāṃ annapūrṇāṃ tarpayāmīti triḥsantarpya yathāśakti japtvā 
guhyātiguhyatyādinā japaṃ samarpya stutvā natvā saṃhāramudrayā nirmālyaṃ 
gṛhītvā visarjayediti saṃkṣepaḥ ||

tathā puraścaraṇaṃ || yathā vidhi japenmantraṃ vasuyugmasahasraṃkaṃ || 16 ||

sājyanānnena juhuyāt tad daśāṃśa manantaraṃ |
prāta * * japenmantrī nityamaṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ ||

etasyānnasamṛddhiḥ syācchriyā saha mahīyasī |
ato'syāḥ puraścaraṇaṃ ṣoḍaśa sahasramiti dik ||

atha tvari?tā mantraḥ || śāradā tirake ||

tārā māyā carma bīja mṛddhirīśakharānvitā |
kurmastha danto bhagavān kṣastrī dīrgha tanucchadaṃ ||

samvartā bhagavān māyā phaṭ ntā dvādaśākṣaraḥ |

tāra oṃkāraḥ māyā hrīṃ carmabījā hūṃ ṛddhiḥ khakāraḥ īśasvara ekāraḥ 
kūrmaścakāraḥ dantaścchakāraḥ bhaga ekāraḥ kṣastīsvarūpaṃ(?) 
dīrgha tanucchadaṃ hūṃ samvartaḥ kṣakāraḥ bhaga ekāraḥ māyā hrīṃ tena oṃ 
hrīṃ hūṃ khecacchakṣastrī hūṃ kṣa hrīṃ phaṭ iti mantraḥ || tato yathokta 
prakāreṇa prātakṛtya snānasandhyārgha sthāpana bhūtaśuddhi 
prāṇāyāmamātṛkā nyāsādikaṃ vidhāya ṛṣyādi nyāsaṃ kuryāt | tathā 

munirarjuna ākhyāto virāṭcchandaḥ samīritaṃ | 
tvaritā devatā proktā puruṣārtha phala pradā ||

81a)

tathā ca asya mantrasyārjuna ṛṣirvirāṭ cchandastvaritā devatā puruṣārtha 
siddhaye viniyogaḥ iti smṛtvā śivo vadana hṛtsu vinyasya mantra nyāsaṃ kuryāt 
| yathā māyā vivarjitān varṇān mūrddhni bhāle gale hṛdi | 

nābhiguhyāruyugmeṣu jānujaṃghā padeṣu ca |
vinyasya vyāpakaṃ kuryāt samasta naiva sādhakaḥ ||

tena mūlamantraṃ māyā bīja vivarjitaṃ kṛtvā mūrddhnādi nyaset | tataḥ 
samastena vyāpakaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ | tathā kūrmādyaiḥ saptabhirvarṇaiḥ 
pūrvapūrvavivarjitaiḥ | 

dvābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ ṣaḍaṅgāni kalpayet sādhakottamaḥ |

tathā ca kūrmādyaiśca kārādyaiḥ saptarbhirvarṇaiḥ ṣaḍaṅganyāsaṃ kuryāt 
pūrva pūrva varjitān kṛtvā dvābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃcāra dvayaṃ pratyekaṃ 
paṭhitvā nyased ityarthaḥ | tathā ca cacche hṛdayāya namaḥ chakṣa śirase 
svāhā kṣaṃstrī śikhāyai vaṣaṭ ityādi krameṇa nyaset | tato mūlena vyāpakaṃ 
vidhāya dhyāyet || śyāmāṃ varhi kalāpa śeṣarayutāmāvarddhapaṭṭāṃśukāṃ 
guñjāhāralasatyayo dharena tāmaṣṭā hi pān vibhratīṃ | tāḍaṅkāṅgadamekhalā 
gaṇarenanmañjīratāṃ prāpitān kairātī varadābhayodyatakarāṃ devīṃ trinetrāṃ 
bhaje | iti dhyātvā mānasairupacāraiḥ saṃpūjya viśeṣārgha sthāpanaṃ vidhāya 
pīṭhaṃ purato vilikhya pūjayet |

81b)

yathā madhye saroje daśapata yukta māyāṃ likhed vāñchita sādhyagarbhāṃ | 
tārādi varṇān daśapatra saṃsthān ṣaṭkoṇavītaṃ vasudhā purasthaṃ | 

kṛtyādrohādi samanaṃ vyālacaurabhayāpahaṃ |
vidhṛtaṃ tvaritā yantraṃ viśeṣādvijaya pradaṃ ||

tena ṣaṭkoṇaṃ daśapatraṃ pīṭhaṃ pīṭha mantreṇa pūjayen mantro yathā 
samvartatako vindu yutaḥ * * *? sakalaṃ viyat | 

vajra deha kuru dvandvamābhāṣya hiṅgulu dvayaṃ |
garja yugmaṃ viyet sendurvarmānto dīrghabindun ||

paṃcāmanāya hṛdayaṃ pīṭhamantraḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
dadyādāsanametena mūrti mūlena kalpayet ||

saṃvartaḥ kṣakāraḥ kavacaṃ hūṃ viyet hakāraḥ |
kiṃ bhūtaṃ sakalaṃ kalayā saha vartamānaṃ hūṁmiti tena kṣaṃ hūṁ haṃ 
vajradeha kuru kuru hiṅgulu hiṃgulu garja garja haṃ hūṃ kṣaṃ paṃcānanāya 
namaḥ viyad? hakāraḥ | varma hūṃ a *?ḥ kṣakāraḥ dīrghabindu 
dīrghaśca binduśca dīrgha ākāraḥ binduranusvāraḥ | athavā 
dīrghavinduvisargastadyukta ityarthaḥ | iti pīṭha mantreṇa pīṭhaṃ saṃmpūjya 
mūlena * * *? dyā * * *? saṃpūjya * 
*? nmudrādarśayet || tathā aṅgaiḥ pranītāṃ gāyatrī keśare a?rcayet 
kramāt | tathā keśareṣu * *? praṇādāṃ(?) 
hrāṃ(?) hrīṃ(?) hrūṃ(?) hraiṃ(##?
taduktaṃ |

deveṣu pūjayedetāḥ śrībījādyāḥ svabhūṣitāḥ |

82a)

hūṃkārīṃ khecarīṃ caṇḍāṃ cchedinīṃ kṣepaṇīṃ striyaṃ |
hūṃkārīṃ kṣemakārīñca lokeśāṃ varabhūṣaṇāṃ ||

tena śrīṃ hūṃkāryai namaḥ ityādi krameṇa pūjayet tathā |
phaṭkārī nagrato vāhye kodaṇḍaśaradhāriṇīṃ ||

dvārasya pārśvayoḥ pūjye hemavatra karāmbuje |
jayākhyā vijayākhyā ca kaṃ kalāya padaṃ tataḥ ||

vakṣa yugma padasyānte tvaritāṅgā sthiro bhava |
varmāstrāntena manunā kiṅkaraṃ tad bahiryajet ||

na guḍaṃ vibhrataṃ kṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇavarvaramūrddhajaṃ ||

tathā ca || kiṃkarāyarakṣarakṣatvaritāṅgā sthiro bhava hūṃ phaṭ kiṃkarāya 
namaḥ iti pūjayet || tato mūle tarpayitvā yathā śakti japaṃ vidhāya 
guhyātiguhyatyādinā japaṃ samarpya stutvā natvā saṃhāramudrayā nirmālyaṃ 
gṛhītvā visarjayet ||

atha puraścaraṇaṃ ||

lakṣaṃ saṃjapya mantrajño mantramenaṃ jitendriyaḥ |
daśāṃśaṃ juhuyādvillaistrimadhvaktaiḥ samidvaraiḥ ||

evaṃ siddha manurmantrī nārī naranareśvaraiḥ |
mānyate vatsuro darvāk lakṣmājita dhaneśvaraḥ ||

ityādi saṃkṣepaḥ || atha trikūṭā mantraḥ ||

śrīmāyāmadanaiḥ proktā mantrau bīja trayātmakaḥ |

śrīḥ śrīṃ māyā hrīṃ madana klīṃ iti tato yathokta pūrva prakāreṇa 
prātaḥkṛtyādi mātṛkā nyāsāntaṃ varmasamāpya ṛṣyādi nyāsaṃ kuryāt |

82b)

yathā ṛṣiḥ samvāhanaṃ cchando gāyatryaṃ devatā manāḥ |
tripuṭākhya trirukṣaistairbījairaṅgāni ṣaṭkramāt ||

tena śiro vadana hṛsu ṛṣicchando devatāṅgāni vinyasya śrīṃ hṛdayāya 
namaḥ | hrīṃ śirase svāhā | hrīṃ śikhāyai vaṣaṭ | śrīṃ kavacāya hūṃ | hrīṃ 
netratrayā vauṣaṭ | klīṃ astrāya phaṭ | iti ṣaḍaṅga nyāsaṃ kṛtvā dhyāyet ||

pārijāta vane ramye maṇḍape mani kuṭṭime |
ratna siṃhāsane saumye padme ṣaṭkoṇa śobhite ||

adhastāt kalpavṛkṣasya nisannāṃ devatāṃ smaret |
cāpaṃ pāśāmbuja sarsi jānyaṅkuśaṃ puṣpavānān ||

vibhrāṇā kṣāṃkara sarasijai ratna mauktī trinetrāṃ |
hemābjābhāṃ kuca bharanatāṃ ratnamañjīra kāñjīgrevayādya vilasita tanuṃ 
bhāva * *? ktimādyāṃ ||

cāmarādarśatāmbūla karaṇḍaka sa * * * *? |
vahantāṃ bhiḥ kucārtābhi dūtībhiḥ parivāritāṃ ||

karuṇāmṛtavarṣinyā paśyantī sādhakaṃ dṛśā |

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasairupacāraiḥ saṃpūjya kiśakaṃ(?) *? 
sthāpanaṃ vidhāya pīṭhaṃ purato nidhāya pūjayet ||

tatra sthāpayitvā samāvāhya mudrāḥ darśayitvā padyādibhiḥ saṃpūjya agnyādi 
koṇeṣu pūjayet || taduktaṃ ||

* *? di ṣaṭsu koṇeṣu lakṣmyādyāḥ paripūjayet |
lakṣmī hemaprabhāṃ tanvīṃ satarā dva?yugā bhayā || 1 ||

śaṃkhacakra gadāmbhoja dharaṃ hema nibhaṃ hṛdiṃ || 2 ||

* *? ṅgaśā bhayābhīṣṭadharāṃ gaurīṃ javāruṇāṃ |

tāmṛgaṭaṅkā bhayābhīṣṭadharaṃ svarṇanibhaṃ śivaṃ || 4 ||

nilotpala karāṃ saumyāṃ ratiṅkāñcana sannibhāṃ || 5 ||

dhṛtapāśāṅkuśeṣvāsa puṣpeṣu maraṇaṃ smaraṃ |
tena lakṣmādi smarāntāḥ ṣaṭkoṇeṣu pūjayed ityarthaḥ || 6 ||

pūrvavannidhi yugmantu yajedubhaya pārśvataḥ |
vahiraṅgāni saṃpūjya pūjyāḥ patreṣu mātaraḥ ||

mātaro brahmāṇyādyā ityarthaḥ lokeśān vanitā rūpānarcayet saumya vigrahān | 
tena indrādi lokapālān saṃpūjya mūlena santarpya puṣpādibhiḥ saṃpūjya 
yathāśakti japtvā guhyāti guhyetyādinā japaṃ samarpya saṃhāramudrayā 
nirmālyaṃ gṛhītvā visarjayediti tathā 

itthaṃ yaḥ pūjayed devīṃ nityaṃ bhakti samanvitaḥ |
saṃprāpya kavitāṃ divyāṃ prāpyalakṣmī manaśrāaṃ ||

saubhāgya matulaṃ labdhvā viharet suciraṃ bhuvi |

atha puraścaraṇaṃ ||

bhānulakṣaṃ japen mantraṃ enantāvat sahasrakaṃ |
javāpuṣpaiśca juhuyāt toṣayecca svakaṃ guruṃ ||

iti saṃkṣepaḥ ||

atha mahiṣamardinī mantraḥ || śāradā tilake ||

bhāntaṃ viyet sa nayanaṃ śveto mardini ṭha dvayaṃ |
aṣṭākṣarī samākhyātā vidyā mahiṣa mardinī ||

bhāntaṃ makāraḥ viyat hakāraḥ nayanaṃ ikāraḥ śvetaḥ ṣakāraḥ dvayaṃ svāhā 
|| tato ṛṣyādi nyāsa kulacūḍāmanau ||

nāradosya ṛṣiḥ proktaścchando gāyatryamīritaṃ |

83b)

devatā mahiṣaghnīyaṃ pūrvabījaṃ parāparā ||

evaṃ prātaḥkṛtyādi pūrvoktaṃ mātṛkāntaṃ vidhāya viśeṣa nyāsaṃ kuryāt | 
taduktaṃ 

mahiṣa hiṃsike hūṃ phaṭ hṛdayaṃ parikīrtitaṃ |
mahiṣa śatrorvi hūṃ phaṭ śiroṅgaṃ parikīrtitaṃ ||

mahiṣaṃ bhīṣaya dvandaṃ hūṃ phaṭ ḍantaḥ śikhāmanuḥ |
mahiṣaṃ hana yugmānte devi hūṃ phaṭ tanucchadaṃ ||

mahiṣānte sūdini hūṃ phaḍantamantramīritaṃ ||

tathā kulacūḍāmanau || 

trailokya bījabhūjatānte sambodhana padantataḥ | 
sṛṣṭisaṃhāravarṇau dvau vidyā mahiṣa mardinī || 

ati guhyā mahāvidyā sṛti sthiti vidhāyinī |

trailokya bījabhūtaṃ hrīṃ sambodhana padaṃ mahiṣa mardini sṛṣṭisaṃhāra 
varṇau svāhā | tathā 

na kasmaicit prayoktavyā kathitā siddhi nāsinī |
a * * nta gurubhaktāya śuddhāya yadi kathyate ||

tadāṣṭa varṇaṃ dātavyaṃ na bījaṃ nāpi sādhanaṃ |
sādhāraṇī prāṇa vidyā hṛllekhā siddhi gocara ||

etat pūrva sthitā devī gurusiddhi vināsinī |

trailokya bījabhūta padena hrīmiti kathyate tadeva vyaktī kṛtaṃ sādhāraṇī 
prāṇavidyā hṛllekhetyādi hṛllekhā hrīṃ | tenāsya mantrasya nāvada 
ṛṣirgāyatrī chando mahiṣamardinī devatā abhiṣṭasiddhau viniyoga iti smṛtvā 
śiro vadana hṛtsu vinyasya mahiṣa hiṃsika hūṃ phaṭ hṛdayāya namaḥ 

84a)

mahiṣa śatrorvi hūṃ phaṭ śirase svāhā mahiṣaṃ bhīṣaya bhīṣaya hūṃ phaṭ 
śikhāyai vaṣaṭ mahiṣaṃ hana hana hūṃ phaṭ kavacāya hūṃ mahiṣa bhūdini hūṃ 
phaṭ astrāya nama iti vinyasya etāne vāṅguṣṭhādiṣu nyaset | tato mūlena 
vyāpakaṃ vidhāya devīṃ dhyāyet || gāruḍāpamasannibhāṃ maṇimauli 
kuṇḍalamaṇḍitāṃ naumi bhālavilocanāṃ mahiṣottamāṅganiṣaduṣīṃ | 
cakraśaṃkha kṛpāṇakheṭakavānākārmukaśūle karāṃ tarjanīmapi vibhratīṃ 
nija vāhubhiḥ śaśiśeṣarāṃ ||

iti dhyātvā mānasairupacāraiḥ saṃpūjyaviśeṣārgha sthāpanaṃ kṛtvā pīṭhaṃ 
purato vidhāya tatrā vāhya tat tanmudrāḥ pradarśya pādyādibhiḥ saṃpūjya 
pūrvokta ṣaḍaṅga mantreṇa ṣaḍaṅgaṃ pūjayet || taduktaṃ ||

saṃpūjyāṅgāni patreṣu durgākhyāṃ varavarṇinīṃ |
āryāñcaiva tṛtīyāṃ ca caturthīṃ kanaka prabhāṃ ||

pañcamīṃ kṛrtikā saṃjñāṃ ṣaṣṭhīmapya bhayapradāṃ |
kanyāṃ svarūpāṃ prayajen mantrī dīrghasvaraiḥ kramāt ||

dīrghasvarai āṃ īṃ ūṃ aiṃ auṃ aḥ ityādi || tenāṣṭa patreṣu āṃ durgāyai nama 
ityādi krameṇa pūjayet | tathā 

yajedagreṣvāyudhāni cakraśaṃkhāni kheṭakān | 
vāṇaṃ sarāsanaṃ śūlaṃ tarjanīmādibhiḥ kramāt ||

ādibhirdīrghasvarairityarthaḥ | tena āṃ cakrāya namaḥ ityādi krameṇa pūjayet | 
tathā 

lokapālāstataḥ pūjyāstadastrāṇi tataḥ paraṃ |

84b)

evaṃ krameṇa saṃpūjya gandhādibhiśca punaḥ pūjayitvā mūlena tarpayitvā yathā 
śakti japtvā guhyāti guhyetyādinā japaṃ samapya stutvā natvā daṇḍavat 
praṇamyasaṃhāramudrayā nirmālyaṃ gṛhītvā visarjayediti ||

* * * * * * * * * * *? lakṣa 
japaṃ *? ntraṃ |
tatsaha anti(?)laiḥ śubhaiḥ | hutvā prāgītite pīṭhe yajen mahiṣa 
mardinīṃ || ityādi vistaraḥ ||

devi cintāpareṇāsya * * * *? makulaṃ | 

sa jīvaṃ doṣa rahitaṃ kathayasvamamāgrataḥ | 
vedavighnasaṃdu(?) * * *? nantogaṇanāyakaḥ ||

aikāraḥ tena oṃ oṃ duṃdu * *? namaḥ | * * * *

yojanīyaṃ na sā devī yojanīyā  ca bhogadā | etena asya mantrasyādaudumiti bījaṃ 
tyāktā * * * * *? dyatanaṃ dadyāti * * *

bījapūrvakamityabhidhāya vāgbījaṃ sṛṣṭibījasya * *?

kāmabījaṃ * * * * *? tantracūḍāmanau ||

85a)

ugradurgā tu vā proktā parā sā siṃhavāhinī |
trailokyabījabhūtānte sā parāmardinī kulaṃ ||

varaṃ vahni priyā yuktā dayānanamasāyutā |
sarvatra kulaśāstrajña mahāśāpa pradāyinī ||

tasmāt sarva prayatnena jñātavye yaṃ navākṣarī |

trailokya bījabhūtaṃ hrīṃ asyā ante yadi ugra durgāyāṣṭākṣarī tadā sarvathaiva 
adeyā iti | tathā pūrvokta krameṇa prātaḥkṛtyādi mātṛkāntaṃ karmasamāpya 
ṛṣyādi nyāsaṃ kuryāt || tadukte

nāradosya muniḥ proktaḥ gāyatryaṃ cchanda īritaṃ |
ugradurgeti vikhyātā vidyā mahiṣamardinī ||

tathā ca śiro vadana hṛtsu ṛṣichando devatādīni vinyasya mūlena vyāpakaṃ 
vidhāya devīṃ dhyāyet || 

ghorāṃ durvādalaśyāmāṃ mahābhīmāṃ caturbhujāṃ |
śaṃkhacakradhanurvāṇān dhārayantīṃ mahonmukhīṃ ||

siṃhāsana gatāṃ devīṃ vidyaiśvarya pradāyinīṃ |

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasairupacāraiḥ saṃpūjya viśeṣārgha sthāpanaṃ kṛtvā 
pīṭhaṃ purato niyojayet | yathā 

yantre vasudale'nuṣṭup vīte mātṛkayānvite | 
aṣṭārṇa dalasaṃyukte madhye bīja samālikhet ||

madhye garbhaṃ tāramadhye pūjayet parameśvarīṃ |

tena madhye bījadvaya yuktaṃ dale aṣṭakṣara yuktaṃ yantramuddharedityarthaḥ | tatra 
devīṃ mūlena saṃsthāpya āvāhya tat tanmudrāḥ | pradarśya pādyādibhiḥ 
pūjayitvā ṣaḍaṅga mantreṇa namontena ṣaḍaṅgaṃ sampūjya patreṣu pūjayet || 
yathā

85b)

prabhā māyā jayā śūkṣmā viśuddhā nalinī punaḥ |
suprabhā vijayā sarva siddhidā navaśaktayaḥ ||

pūjyā ityarthaḥ || tathā svarairlaghu klīva hīnairbījaiḥ siṃhāsanaṃ jayet | tena āṃ 
īṃ duṃ eṃ auṃ aḥ siṃhāsanāya namaḥ iti sampūjya tatra devīṃ saṃpūjya astrāṇi 
pūjayet || taduktaṃ ||

prathamā kṛttiraṅgādyai dvitīyā śaktibhiḥ punaḥ |
astrāvṛttistṛtīyāsyāllokapālaiścaturthikā ||

parāvṛttistadastrāni sampūrṇaṃ kathitaṃ tava |

etena pañca vidhatva māyāti aṅgādyaiḥ ṣaḍaṅgādyaiḥ śaktibhiḥ dvitīyā 
punaḥ śaktibhirityarthaḥ yathā 

jayā ca vijayā kīrtiḥ prītiḥ paścāt prabhā punaḥ |
śraddhāmeṣādhṛtiḥ proktā svanāmādyākṣarānvitāḥ ||

jaṃjayāyai nama ityādi krameṇa | tathā 

śaṃkhacakragadāpadma pāśāṅkuśa śarān dhanuḥ |

pūjayedityarthaḥ | tathā indrādyā lokapālā caivajrādyāyudha daivataṃ etena 
pañcavidhatva māyāti tato mūlena tarpayitvā gandhādibhiḥ sampūjya yathā śakti 
japtvā guhyāti guhyetyādinā japaṃ sapapyastutvā natvā saṃhāra mudrayā 
nirmālyaṃ gṛhītvā visajayediti tathā 

sarvaṃ rahasyaṃ kathitaṃ tavasnehān maheśvari | 
na deyā paramā vidyā sarvatra vijaya pradā ||

vāgīśatva pradā devī mahāmokṣa pradāyinī |
aṣṭasiddhi pradā devī kimanyairvahujalpitaiḥ ||

86a)

yasmai kasmai na dātavyā na dātavyā kathañcana |
yena yaṃ dīyate vidyāmaduccātane kāriṇī ||

vajrena śastrayātena tasya jīvaṃ harāmyahaṃ ||

atha puraścaraṇaṃ ||

evaṃ dhyātvā japellakṣaṃ taddaśāṃśaṃ hunet tataḥ |

ityādi saṃkṣepaḥ ||

atha navadurgāmantraḥ || śāradā tilake ||

uttiṣṭha padamābhāṣya puruṣi syāt padantataḥ |
pitāmahaḥ sa netrenduḥ svapiṣisyād bhayañca me ||

samupasthitamuccārya yadi śakya manantaraṃ |
aśakyamvā punastanme vaded bhagavatintataḥ ||

śamayāgni vadhūḥ sapta triṃśadvarṇātmako manuḥ |

pitāmahaḥ kakāraḥ netra īkāraḥ binduranusvāraḥ tena uttiṣṭha puruṣi kiṃ 
svapiṣibhayaṃ me samupasthitaṃ yadi śakyamaśakyamvā tanme bhagavati śamaya 
svāhā | tataḥ prātaḥkṛtyādi mātṛkāntaṃ karma samāpya ṛṣyādi nyāsaṃ 
kuryāt || taduktaṃ ||

ṛṣirāraṇyakachando'pyanuṣṭup chando hṛtaṃ |
devatā vanadurgāsyāḥ sarvadurgavimocanīṃ ||

evaṃ śiro vadana hṛtsu ṛṣicchando devatā vinyasya eteṣvaṅgeṣu 
mantrākṣarāṇi nyaset || taduktaṃ ||

padāṣṭa sandhiṣu gudaliṅgādhāro daleṣu ca |
pārśva hṛtsu na kaṇṭheṣu punarvāhvaṣṭa sandhiṣu ||

mukhanāśā kapolākṣi karṇa bhrumadhyamūrddhasu |
mantrākṣarāṇi vinyasyed devatābhāvaśuddhaye ||

tena uṃ mana ityādi krameṇa padādiṣu nyaset |

86b)

tathā 

ṣaḍbhiścaturbhiraṣṭābhiraṣṭābhiḥ ṣaḍbhirindriyaiḥ |
mantrārṇairaṅgaṃ kṛptiḥ syājjāti yuktairyathākramaṃ ||

tena mantrasya ṣaḍbhirakṣarairvidyādi krameṇa hṛdādiṣu aṅguṣṭhādiṣu nyaset | 
tato mūlena vyāpakaṃ vidhāya dhyāyet || sauvarṇāmbuja madhyagāṃ triṇayanāṃ 
saudāminī sannibhāṃ cakraṃ śaṃkhaṃ varābhayānidadhatīmindoḥ kalāṃ 
vibhratīṃ | graiveyāṅga dahārakuṇḍala dharāmākhaṇḍalādyaistutāṃ dhyāyed 
vindhya nivāsinīṃ śaśimukhīṃ pārśvastha pañcānanāṃ || evaṃ 
dhyānānasairupacāraiḥ saṃpūjyārgha sthāpanaṃ kṛtvā tato navakoṇa 
samanvitaṃ aṣṭadala bhūrādyaṃ yantraṃ purato nidhāya pūrvokta rūpāṃ dhyātvā 
yantre saṃsthāpyā vāhya pādyādibhiḥ pūjayitvā ṣaḍaṅgenābhyarcya dalaṣu 
pūjayet | 

āryādurgā ca bhadrākhyā bhadrakālī tato'mbikā |
kṣamyānyāvadagarbhākhyā kṣamaṅkarpaṣṭha śaktayaḥ ||

tena oṃ āryāyai namaḥ ityādi krameṇa pūjayet |
tantrāṇi patra madhyeṣu cakraṃśaṃkhāśikheṭakān ||

vāṇaṃ ko daṇḍa śūlādi kapālāntāni pūjayet |
brāhmādyāḥ syurdalāgreṣu lokapālāstataḥ paraṃ ||

tena yathokta sthāneṣvatānyastrāni pūjayet | tato mūlena tarpayitvā japtvā stutvā 
natvā visarjayet | siddhamantra prayogeṣu devīmitthaṃ prapūjayet || ityādi vistaraḥ ||

atha caṇḍikāmantraḥ || 

atha caṇḍīmanuṃ vakṣye maṅgalāṃ maṅgalālayāṃ |
vāntaṃ vahni samārūḍhaṃ caturthasvarabhūṣitaṃ ||

anusvāreṇa saṃyuktaṃ māyābījena saṃpuṭaṃ |

vāntaṃ śakāraḥ vahnī rephaḥ caturtha īkāraḥ māyā hrīṃ tena hrīṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ iti 
tathā ca prātaḥkṛtyādi mātṛkāntaṃ karma samāpya ṛṣyādi nyāsaṃ kuryāt 
ṛṣiḥ syād dakṣiṇāmūrtiranuṣṭup devatā śivaḥ | 

ṛtulakṣaṃ japen mantraṃ tadarddhaṃ nyāsamācaret |
tadarddhena careddhomaṃ tadarddhena ca tarpaṇaṃ ||

tadarddhena valiṃ datvā sahasrārddhena pūjayet |

yantra ityarthaḥ || 

ṣaṭkoṇe vilikhet padmaṃ sakalaṃ triguṇairyutaṃ |
vasudvāraṃ dharāgehaṃ bījāḍhyaṃ yantramīritaṃ ||

triguṇairyutaṃ rekhā yutamityarthaḥ vasudvāraṃ aṣṭadvāraṃ evaṃ yantraṃ purato 
nidhāya dhyāyet || yathā 

sudhāsāgara madhyasthaṃ mahadvīpaṃ suśobhanaṃ |
satṛṅga kusumopetaṃ kalpavṛkṣo yaśobhitaṃ ||

sapatāka dhvajo petaṃ śubhracāmararājitaṃ |
tanmadhye svarṇaparyaṅkaṃ nānāratna samanvitaṃ ||

vāriṣūṇḍaiḥ svarṇakumbhaiḥ sarvataḥ susamāvṛtaṃ |
cintayet paramāṃ devīṃ pūrṇasvarṇa ghaṭopari ||

pañcasiṃhāsagatāṃ tapta jāmbūnada prabhāṃ |?
aṭasī puṣpasaṃkāśāṃ vālārka kiraṇāruṇāṃ ||

varābhayadharāṃ devīṃ candrārddhakṛtaśeṣarāṃ |
smitaprasannavadanāṃ bandhūkādharapallavāṃ ||

evaṃ dhyātvā yantramadhye devī mantaḥ prapūjayet |

iti dhyātvā pādyādibhiḥ saṃpūjya japādikaṃ vidhāya samā(?) 
* * *? visarjayediti saṃkṣepaḥ ||

atha mahāmāyā mantraḥ ||

vāgbhavaṃ prathamaṃ bījaṃ kāmabījaṃ dvitīyakaṃ |
tṛtī * * * * * *? punarvāgbhavamucyate ||

lajjābījaṃ tataḥ paścāt padmābījantataḥ paraṃ |
varmacāgni priyāntotra mahāmāyā mahāmanuḥ ||

vāgbhavaṃ aiṃ kāmabījaṃ klīṃ śaktibījaṃ sauḥ vāgbhavaṃ aiṃ lajjābījaṃ hrīṃ 
padmābījaṃ śrīṃ varma hūṁ agni priyā svāhā || 

tena aiṃ klīṃ sauḥ aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hūṃ svāhā | iti tathā ca pūrvokta krameṇa 
prātaḥkṛtya sandhyā mātṛkāntaṃ karmasamāpya mantra nyāsaṃ kuryāt || 
tathā ṣaḍaṅga mantreṇa ||

aṃguṣṭhādi karatalāntasya nyaset hṛdādiṣu | yathā 

ṛṣirbrahmā samurddiṣṭo gāyatrīcchanda īritaṃ |
devatā ca samuddiṣṭā mahāmāyā sugopitā ||

iti ṛṣyādi nyāsaṃ vidhāya mūlena vyāpakaṃ kṛtvā dhyāyet || yathā ||

dhyāyed digvasanāṃ prahāsya vadanāṃ koṭyarka tejaḥ prabhāṃ padmasthāṃ 
varadāna paṅkajadharāṃ savye sutābhīṣṭadāṃ |
ādyāṃ muktimabhīpsubhiḥ parivṛtāṃ kaṇṭhe ca muṇḍa srajāṃ snigdhā 
mullasitastanīṃ paraśivāṃ śastorharermadhyagāṃ ||

iti dhyātvā mānasairupacāraiḥ saṃpūjya viśeṣārgha sthāpanaṃ kṛtvā yantraṃ 
purato nidhāya tatrāvāhya pādyādibhiḥ saṃpūjya yathā śakti japaṃ samarpya 
stutvā natvā saṃhāra mudrayā nirmālyaṃ gṛtvā visarjayediti saṃkṣepaḥ ||

88a)

tathā 

māyābījaṃ samuddhṛtya mahāmāyā padantataḥ |
ṅentamagni priyāntoyaṃ mahāmāyā mahāmanuḥ || 

māyābījaṃ hrīṃ agnijāyā svāhā || dhyānaṃ ||

sindūrāruṇa vigrahāṃ trinayaṇāṃ mānikya maulisphurahārā nāyaka śeṣarāṃ 
smitamukhīmāpīna vakṣoruhāṃ | 
pāṇibhyāmanipūrṇa ratna ca sakāṃ raktotpalāṃ vibhratīṃ saumyāṃ ratna 
ghaṭastha savya caraṇāṃ dhyāyet parāmamvikāṃ ||

tatvalakṣaṃ japenmantrī taddaśāṃśaṃ hunet tataḥ |
pūrvoktaṃ pūjayet pīṭhaṃ pūjāṃ pūrva vadācaret ||

tathā 

hāntānta pūrvo māntaśca nāntoṇāntastathaiva ca |
kaikādaśakādi ṣaṣṭhakhānto viṣṇupuraḥsaraḥ ||

ebhiraṣṭākṣarairmantraṃ śona padmasamaṃ likhet |
oṃkāraṃ purataḥ kṛtvā japyaḥ syānnārado muniḥ ||

cchandonuṣṭup vijñeyaṃ mahāmāyā ca devatā |

hāntaḥ sakāraḥ sa evāntaḥ ṣakāraḥ tatpūrvaṃ śakāraḥ mānto yakāraḥ māntaḥ 
pakāraḥ ṇāntastakāraḥ kaikādaśaṣṭakāraḥ kādi ṣaṣṭhaścakāraḥ khāntaḥ 
kakāraḥ viṣṇuṃ rakāraḥ tena vaiparītyena aka ca ṭa ta pa ya śa iti tatrādau 
praṇavaṃ datvā japyamityarthaḥ || atha vaiparītyenoddhāraḥ kathaṃ labhyate iti ced 
viṣṇupuraḥsara ityato labhyate anyaṃthā puraḥsara tvameva na syāt |

88b)

aṣṭalakṣaṃ japenmantraṃ prathamāñcaiva sādhakaḥ ||

atha dhyānaṃ ||

tatrekṣu sāmarāntasthaṃ svarṇadvīpaṃ vicintayet |
tanmadhye * *? paryaṅka ratnamaṇḍapasaṃsthitaṃ ||

ākāśagaṃṅgātoyādyaiḥ sadaivā siñcitaṃ śubhaṃ |
tat paryaṅke rakta padmaṃ prasastaṃ sarvadā śivaṃ ||

cintayet svarṇamālāṅkaṃ saptapātāla nālakaṃ(?) |
ābrahma bhuvanasparśiṃ svarṇavarṇañca karṇikaṃ ||

tatra sthitāṃ mahāmāyāṃ *? yedavyagramā * * *? |
śāna padma pratīkāśāṃ yuktamūrddhajalānvinīṃ(?) ||

calatkāñcana samvaddha kuṇḍalojvalamālinīṃ |
suvarṇaratna saṃvaddha kirīṭavaradhāriṇīṃ ||

* *? kṛṣṇāruṇairnetraṃ stribhiścāsu(?) vibhūṣitāṃ 
|
sandhyā tāmra samaprakhya kapālā lolalocanāṃ ||

vipaṅka?dāḍimī bījadantāṃ vajraguṇojvalāṃ |
* * * *? dantavasanāṃ śirīśa prabhunāśikāṃ ||

kambugrīvāṃ viśālākṣīṃ sūryakoṭi samaprabhāṃ |
caturbhujāṃ vivasanāṃ pīnonnata payodharāṃ ||

dakṣi * * * * * *? pareṇa si * * * 
*? |
vibhratīṃ vāmahastābhyāṃ abhīti varadhāriṇīṃ ||

nimnanābhiṃ kramāṃ yattu kṣīṇa madhyāṃ manoharāṃ |
āla * *? savāmoruṃ gūḍha * * * * *? 
kāṃ ||

varddhaparyaṅkasaṃkalpa niviḍāsanarājitāṃ |
grā?treṇa ratnastambhañca namyagālamvya saṃsthitāṃ ||

kimicchasīti vacanaṃ vyāharantīṃ muhurmuhuḥ |

89a)

pañcānanaṃ puraḥsasthaṃ nirīkṣantīṃ svavāhanaṃ |
muktāvalī svarṇa ratna hārakaṃ kaṇakādibhiḥ ||

sarvvairalaṅkālagaṇairulvanāṃ saṃmitānanāṃ |
sūryakoṭi pratīkāśāṃ sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutāṃ ||

tādṛśīmambikāṃ dhyātvā saivāhamiti cintayan |
aṃganyāsa karanyāsau tataḥ kuryāt krameṇa tu ||

ebhirmantraiḥ svaraiḥ saṃhairāmīsūmaiḥ kramānvitaiḥ |
aumaścete sa praṇavā raktavarṇāmanoharāḥ ||

aṅguṣṭhādi kaniṣṭhānta mantrasaṃveṣṭanaṃ phaṭā |
prāntena kuryād vinyāsaṃ tataḥ karatala dvaye ||

tena oṃ āṃ phaṭ hṛdayāya nama ityaṅguṣṭhayoḥ iti krameṇa nyaset | 
haṭā prāntena antastha phaṭkāreṇetyarthaḥ | tathā 

hṛṭ śiraḥ sikhā kavaca netreṣu kramato nyaset |
tāneva varṇān hṛdādiṣvapi nyaset tathā tatastu mūlamantrasya cakre pṛṣṭhe 
tathodare | 

vāṭkorguhye padayośca jaṃghayorjaghane tathā |
vinyasedakṣarānyaṣṭau oṃkārañca tathā smaran ||

cakre mūlādhāre mūlamantrasya pratyekamakṣaraṃ praṇavādyaṃ kṛtvā 
cakrādiṣu nyaset | tathā 

pañcarūpāntu kāmākhyāṃ pūjayet parameśvarīṃ |
sa kalpakoṭi lakṣāṇi mama loke ca mānavaḥ ||

sthitvā devī prasādena paraṃ nirvāṇa māpnuyāt |
anyat sarvaṃ nyatsarvaṃ pūrvavaditi saṃkṣepaḥ ||

atha vārāhī mantraḥ ||

89b)

tathā 

vārāhī ca tathā ṅentaṃ māyādiṣṭa dvayāvadhi |
vārāhīti ṅentaṃ caturthyantamityarthaḥ ||

māyā bhuvaneśvarī hrīṃ ṭha dvayaṃ svāhā || tena hrīṃ vārāhyairsvahā iti 
mantraḥ | tathā ca prātaḥkṛtyādi mātṛkāntaṃ samāpya ṛṣyādi nyāsaṃ 
kuryāt tathā ṛṣirṇorāyaṇaḥ proktaḥ striṣṭup cchandaśca devatā vārāhī kathitā 
cātra sarvārthe viniyojayet | tato mūlena vyāpakaṃ vidhāya yantraṃ purato nidhāya 
dhyāyet || taduktaṃ ||

trikoṇaṃ cakramālikhya aṣṭapatramanantaraṃ |
dharaṇī sadanaṃ paścāt sa vajraṃ dvāra yuk kramāt ||

dhyāyet tapta suvarṇābhāṃ nānālaṅkārabhūṣitāṃ |
pāśāṅkuśau dakṣiṇena sṛṣṭi sthiraṃ varameva ca ||

dadhatīṃ ratnamukuṭāṃ trailokyamiti rāpahāṃ || iti dhyātvā mānasairupacāraiḥ 
saṃpūjyārgha sthāpanaṃ kṛtvā cakre samāvāhya tat tanmudrāḥ pradarśya 
pādyādibhiḥ sampūjya mūlena tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā japaṃ vidhāya samarpyastu 
visarjayediti | tathā 

tritatvaṃ tārayormadhye vārāhītya parā bhavet |

tritatvaṃ hūṃ tāraṃ oṃ tena oṃ hūṃ oṃ dhyātādikaṃ pūrvavat ||

atha mā?lāmukhī mantraḥ || rudrajāmale ||

vahni hīnendra yuk māyā vagalāmukhi sarvayuk |
duṣṭānāṃ vācamityuktā mukhaṃ stambhaya kīrtayet ||

jihvāṃ kīlaye tadbuddhiṃ vināśaya padaṃ vadet |
punarbījaṃ tatastāraṃ vahnijayāvadhirbhavet ||

tārādikā catustriṃśadakṣarī vagalāmukhī |

90a)

māyā hrīṃ kim bhūtā vahni hīnā indrayuk lakāra yuktā tena hlīṃtāra oṃ 
vahnijāyā svāhā tena oṃ hlīṃ oṃ hlīṃ vagalāmukhi sarvaduṣṭānāṃ vācaṃ 
mukhaṃ stambhaya jihvāṃ kīlaya buddhiṃ vināśaya hlīṃ oṃ svāhā ||

tathā ca prātaḥkṛtyādi mātṛkāntaṃ vidhāya ṛṣyādi nyāsaṃ kuryādasya 
mantrasya nārada ṛṣiḥ paṃkticchandaḥ śrīvagalāmukhī devatā hlīṃ bījaṃ 
svāhā śaktiḥ samasta śatrormukhastambhana vidhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ iti smṛtvā 
śiro vadana hṛtsu vinyasya ṣaḍdīrgha bhājāvījena ṣaḍaṅganyāsaṃ vidhāya 
dhyāyet || madhye sudhābdhi manimaṇḍapamadhya vedyāṃ siṃhāsanopari gatāṃ 
paripītavarṇāṃ | pītāmbarā bharaṇamālyavibhūṣitāṅgīṃ devīnnamāmi 
dhṛtakuṇḍala vairi jihvāṃ ||

iti dhyātvā mānasairupacāraiḥ saṃpūjyārgha sthāpanaṃ kuryāt tato yantra 
samāvāhya pādyādibhiḥ saṃpūjya japtvā japaṃ samarpya visarjayediti saṃkṣepaḥ 
||

śrīramāramaṇadevaśarmaṇā nirmite nigamasāranirṇaye | 
ityavāntarasaparyayānvitaḥ pūrva eṣa paṭalastṛtīyakaḥ ||


idānīmavāntara vidyānirūpanānantaraṃ siddhavidyā nirūpyate |
tatra prathamaṃ alpaśramasādhyā nirūpaṇīyā ityādau ||

tatra mātaṅgīmantraḥ || 

90b)

mantraṃ śṛṇu mahādevi yathāvat kathayāmi te |
dadyāducchiṣṭa śavdantu(?) tathācāṇḍālinīti ca ||

sumukhīti tato devīṃ kīrtaye tadanantaraṃ |
mahāpiśācinīntasmān māyābījamanantaraṃ ||

bindunādasamāyuktaṃ ṭhakāra tritayaṃ tataḥ |
savisarga mahādevi sarvasiddhi phalapradaṃ ||

māyā hrīṃ tena ucchiṣṭa cāṇḍālinī sumukhī devī mahāpiśācinī hrīṃ ṭhaṃḥ 
ṭhaṃḥ ṭhaṃḥ || tathā 

athavocchiṣṭa cāṇḍāli mātaṅgi padamīrayet |
tataḥ sarvava?ñcānta *? ri hṛdvahni vallabhā ||

ekoṇa viṃśatā varṇaiḥ sarvābhīṣṭa karo bhavet |

hṛnnamaḥ vahni vallabhā svāhā tenocchiṣṭa * *? li mātaṅgi 
sarvavaśaṅkari namaḥ svāhā || tathā 

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi devīṅgupta tamāṃ priye |
ucchiṣṭapūrvikāṃ devīṃ mātaṅgīṃ sarvasiddhidāṃ ||

sarvapaccā?riṇī * *? sarvadoṣavivarjitāṃ |
yayāvijñātayā devyākṣayamicchanti cāpadaḥ ||

sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ caiva kṛntrimaṃ cāṅgajantathā |
imāṃ vidyāṃ japeśāni pare *? ṅga samāśritāḥ ||

iyaṃ vidyā mahāvidyā sarvapāpāpahāriṇī |
svargadā mokṣadā caiva ra * *? bhāgyadā tathā ||

sakṛduccāritā vidyā brahmahatyā pahāriṇī |
sadocchiṣṭamu *? bhūtvā smared devīṅkadācana ||

tadā cāntā bhaved devi vinaivā vasane(?) kṛte |
durbhagā subhagā * *? duḥkhitā sukhitā bhavet ||

vandhyāpi labhate putraṃ śatahāyanajīvitaṃ |
vidhānañcātra vakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi varānane ||

91a)

bhojanānantarandevi vinaivā ca mane kṛte |
valindatvā prathamato mūlamantreṇa sādhakaḥ ||

tato mantraṃ japeddhyātvā devīntāmiṣṭa siddhayet |
na tithirṇe ca nakṣatraṃ na cāṅgaṃ nyāsa eva ca ||

nāri doṣo na vidveṣau nāśaucaṃ niyamopi na |
yasya tiṣṭhati mantroyaṃ na sa vighnairavāpyate ||

tathā ca prātaḥkṛtyādi mātṛkāntaṃ karma kṛtvā mūlena vyāpakaṃ vidhāya 
dhyāyet || 

śavopari samāsīnāṃ raktāmvara parichadāṃ |
raktālaṅkārasaṃyuktāṃ guñjāhāravibhūṣitāṃ ||

ṣoḍaśābdāñca yuvatīṃ pīnonnata payodharāṃ |
kapālakartṛkā hastāṃ paraṃ jyotiḥ svarūpiṇī ||

vāmadakṣiṇa yogena dhyāyet tāṃ mantravittamaḥ |

evaṃ dhyātvā mānasairucāraiḥ saṃpūjyārghaṃ saṃsthāpya yantra sthāpayitvā 
samāvāhyā vāhanādi mudrā pradarśya pādyādibhiḥ saṃpūjya mūlena tarpayitvā 
japaṃ vidhāya samarpya stutvā natvā visarjayediti | tathā 

ucchiṣṭena valindatvā japet taṅgatamānasaḥ |

ucchiṣṭe naiva kartavyo japo syāḥ siddhimicchatā ucchiṣṭa japamānasya jāyante 
sarvasiddhayaḥ | 

aparañca pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi phalapradaṃ |
homañca tarpaṇañcaiva sarvakāmārtha siddhaye ||

devīṃ dhyātvā careddhomaṃ dadhisirddhārtha taṇḍulaiḥ |
sahasra mātrahomena rājānaṃ vaśamānayet ||

mārjārasya tu māṃśena devyāhomaṃ samācaret |

91b)

saṃprāptoti parāṃ vidyāṃ śastraśāstra vaśīkṛtāṃ |
bilvapatraiśca samadhvaktairmasamekaṃ nirantaraṃ ||

vandhyāpi * *? te putraṃ cirajīvinamuttamaṃ |
prātarutthāya yaḥ kaściddhomaṃ kukuryād dina dina ||

japāpuṣpena sājyena śatamantrottaraṃ śive ||

idānīmavāntara vidyā cidrūpamānantaraṃ siddhi vidyā nirūpyate ||
tatra prathamamalpa śramasādhyānirūpaṇīyā ityādau ||

* * * * * * * * * *? 
mantraṃ śṛṇu mahādevi yathāvat kathayāmi te |
dadyāducchiṣṭaśabdantu tathā cāṇḍālinīti ca ||

sumukhīti tato devīṃ * * *? tadana * * |
* * * * * * * *? māyābījamanantaraṃ ||

bindunādasamāyuktaṃ ṭhakāra tritayaṃ tataḥ |
sa visarga mahādevi sarvasiddhi phalapradaṃ ||

māyā hrīṃ tena ucchiṣṭa cāṇḍālinī sumukhī devī mahāpiśācinī hrīṃ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ 
ṭhaḥ || tathā ||

atha vā cchiṣṭa cāṇḍāli mātaṅgi padamīrayet |
tataḥ sarvavaśañcāntaka vikṛdvahni vallabhā ||

ekona viṃśatā varṇaiḥ sarvābhīṣṭakaro bhavet |

hṛnnamaḥ | vahni vallabhā svāhā || tenācchiṣṭa cāṇḍāli mātaṅgi 
sarvavaśaṅkari namaḥ svāhā || tathā | 

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi devīṅguptatamāṃ priye |
ucchiṣṭa pūrvikāndevīṃ mātaṅgīṃ sarvasiddhidāṃ ||

sarvāpadvāriṇīndevī sarvadoṣa vivarjitāṃ |
yayā vijñātayā devyā kṣayamicchanti cāpadaḥ ||

92a)

sthāvaraṃ jaṅga * * * vakṛttimaṃ cāṅgajantathā |
imāṃ vidyāṃ japeśāni paratāṅga samāśritāḥ ||

iyaṃ vidyā mahāvidyā sarvapāpāpahāriṇī |
svargadā mokṣadā caiva rājyasaubhāgyadā tadā ||

sakṛduccaritā vidyā brahmahatyā pahāriṇī |
sadācchiṣṭa mukho bhūtvā smare devīṅkadācana ||

tadā cānto bhaved devi vinaivā camane kṛte |
durbhagā subhagānūnaṃ duḥkhitā sukhitā bhavet ||

vandhyāpi labhate putraṃ śatahāyana jīvitaṃ |
vidhānañcātra vakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi varānane ||

bhojanānantarandevi vinaivā camane kṛte |
valindatvā prathamato mūlamantreṇa sādhakaḥ ||

tato mantraṃ japeddhyātvā devīntāmiṣṭasiddhaye |
na tithirna ca nakṣatraṃ na cāṅganyāsa eva ca ||

nā videśo na vidveṣo nāśaucaṃ niyamopi na |
yasya tiṣṭhati mantroyaṃ na sa vighnairavāpyate ||

tathā ca prātaḥkṛtyādi mātṛkāntaṃ karma kṛtvā mūlena vyāpakaṃ vidhāya 
dhyāyet || 

śavoparisamāsīnāṃ raktāmbara paricchadāṃ |
raktālaṅkārasaṃyuktāṃ guñjāhāravibhūṣitāṃ ||

ṣoḍaśābdāñca yuvatīṃ pīnonnata payodharāṃ |
kapāla kartṛkā hastāṃ paraṃ jyotiḥ svarūpiṇī ||

vāmadakṣiṇa yogena dhyāyet tāṃ mantravittamaḥ |

evaṃ mānasairupacāraiḥ sampūjyārghaṃ saṃsthāpya yantre 

92b)

sthāpayitvā samāvāhyā vāhanādi mudrāṃ pradarśya pādyādibhiḥ saṃpūjya 
mūlena tapayitvā japaṃ vidhāya samarpyastu svāvisarjayediti ||

tathocchiṣṭena valiṃ datvā japet tadgata mānasaḥ |
ucchiṣṭe naiva kartavyo japāsyāḥ siddhimicchatāḥ ||

ucchiṣṭa japamānasya jāyante sarvasiddhayaḥ |
aparañca pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi phalapradaṃ ||

homañca tarpaṇaṃ caiva sarvakāmārthasiddhaye |
devīndhyātvā careddhomaṃ dadhisiddhārtha taṇḍulaiḥ ||

sahasramātra homena rājānaṃ vaśamānayet |
mārjārasya tu māṃsena devyā homaṃ samācaret ||

saṃprāpnoti parāṃ vidyāṃ śastraśāstra vaśīkṛtāṃ |
vilvapatraiśca madhvaktairmāsamekaṃ nirantaraṃ ||

vandhyāpi labhate putraṃ cirajīvi na muttamaṃ |
prātarutthāya * * * * ddhomaṃ kuryād dine dine ||

javāpuṣpeṇa yojyena śatamaṣṭottaraṃ śive |
ujjaṭe vā śmaśāne vā śūnyāgāre catuḥpathe ||

vali pradānato devī pratyatyakṣā bhavati dhruvaṃ |
ucchiṣṭa bhūṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā sapavitro bhaveddhruvaṃ ||

yadyapi puraścaraṇaṃ na likhitaṃ tathāpi || aṣṭasahasrāṇi jāpaḥ || taduktaṃ || 

yeṣāṃ japecca home ca saṃkhyānoktā maṇīṣibhiḥ |
teṣāmaṣṭasahasrāṇi saṃkhyāsyājjapa homayoḥ ||

ityabhidhānāditi saṃkṣepaḥ || atha cchinnā mantrāḥ || bhairavatantre || 

lakṣmīrlajjā tathā māyā mātrāṃ dvādaśikī matha |

93a)

vajravairocanīya dve māye phaṭ svāhayā yute ||

lakṣmīḥ śrīṃ | lajjā hrīṁ | māyā hūṁ | dvādaśakī aiṁ || tathā ca || śrīṁ hrīṁ 
hūṁ aiṁ vajravairocanīye hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ svāhā || iti mantraḥ || tathā ||

lakṣmī bījaṃ padadyāsyāntasya śrīḥ sarvamukhī |
lajjābījena ādyena vaśyatāṃ yāti yoṣitaḥ ||

māyā bījena cādyena mahāpātaka nāśanaṃ |
mātrā dvādaśakīmādyena syān mahāmokṣa pradāyinī ||

tena pūrvokta mantrādau || etad bījānyatamadānena mantraviśeṣato bhavati || tathā || 

anādi bījaṃ mantrasya kīrtayet mantravittamaḥ |
vajravairocanīye syān māyābīje tataḥ paraṃ ||

phaṭ svāhayā samāyuktaṃ mantraṃ mantra mahoragaṃ |
sarvasiddhi pradaṃ puṇyaṃ bhuktimukti sukhapradaṃ ||

anādi bījaṃ oṃ vajravairocanīye hūṁ hūṁ phaṭa svāhā iti || tathā ||

śakti tritanu vāgbījatāramantrāt samuddharet |
vajra vairocanītyante bhagavān māruto bhavet ||

kūrcamamtraṃ dviṭhānto sau triśaktiḥ samudīritaḥ |

śaktibījaṃ tritanuṃ śakti hrīṁ | tritanuṃ hūṁ | vāk aiṁ | tāraṃ oṃ | mārutaḥ 
pakāraḥ | bhaga ekāraḥ | astraṃ phaṭ | dviṭhaḥ svāhā || tena hrīṁ hrūṁ aiṁ oṃ 
vajravairocanīye hūṁ phaṭ svāhā || iti pañcadaśākṣaraḥ ||

taṃtrāntare tathā ||

ramākūrcantathā lajjā vāgbhavaṃ vajra vai padaṃ |
rocanīye parā dvandva mamtrāgni vallabhā tataḥ ||

93b)

iyaṃ sā ṣoḍaśī proktā triśaktirabhidhīyate |

ramā śrīṁ | kūrcaṃ hūṁ | lajjā hrīṁ | vāgbhavaṃ aiṁ | parādvandvaṃ hrīṁ 
hrīṁ | astraṃ phaṭ | agnivallabhā svāhā || tena śrīṁ hūṁ hrīṁ aiṁ 
vajravairocanīye hrīṁ hrīṁ astraṃ phaṭ svāhā(?) tathā || 

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi devyāḥ saṃkṣepato vidhiṃ |
* * *? myag vidhānena sarvasiddhimavāpnuyāt ||

tathā ca ||

prātaḥkṛtyādi mātṛkāntaṃ karmavidhāya puṣpādivyā saṃkuryāt || taduktaṃ || 
bhairavo'sya ṛṣi * * *? śchandaḥ saṃmrāṭ varānane | 

devatācchinnamastāsyā bījaṃ māyā dvayaṃ bhavet |
iti vinyasya mūlena vyāpakaṃ kṛtvā purato yantraṃ nidhāpayet ||

taduktaṃ ||

* *? pādyādikaṃ datvā maṇḍale purato nyaset |
athāntaryajanaṃ vakṣye yathāvada vadhāraya ||

vāmenānilamākṛṣya kiñcadākuñcayeddhruvaṃ |
recayed bhānumārgeṇa jihvāyāṃ tālu * *? caret ||

śaktṃ śiva pade yojya kiṃcidā kuñcite kṣaṇaṃ | 
antaryāga vidhiṃ kṛtvā vahiryajanamācaret ||

tathā ||

uccaret pūrvamāraṃ bindulāñchita mastakaṃ |
khaḍga cakra(?) dayāyati svāhā yukṣa kanīyasīṃ ||

īkāraṃ ca tato devi candrakoṭi samaprabhaṃ |
sukhaḍgāya tato vācyaṃ śirase tadanantaraṃ ||

svāhāyuktaṃ tato vācyaṃ pavitrāṅguli saṃyutaṃ |
ūkāraṃ ca tato devi vindulāñchita mastakaṃ ||

śrīvajrāya tato yuktaṃ śikhāyai ca tataḥ paraṃ |
svāhāntaṃ madhyamāyāṃ tu vinyaset tadanantaraṃ ||

94a)

mātrāṃ dvādaśikān devi vinyaset tadanaṃtaraṃ |
svāhāntaṃ vinyasen mantraṃ tarjanyāṃ tadanantaraṃ ||

akāraṃ ca tato devi bindulāñcchita mastakaṃ |
netratrayāya saṃyuktamaṅguṣṭhe karayordvayoḥ ||

akāraṃ ca visargāntaṃ surakṣayāyasaṃyutaṃ |
asurakṣayāya saṃyukta mamtrāyeti tataḥ paraḥ ||

phaḍakṣara samāyuktaṃ vinyaset karayordvayoḥ |
hṛdādiṣu ca vinyasya mūlena vyāpakaṃ bhavet ||

tena āṁ khaḍgāya hṛdayāya svāhā || iti kaniṣṭhayorityādi krameṇa nyaset || 
tathā ||

athāsyā maṇḍalaṃ vakṣye yathāvadavadhāraya |
śivaṃ kuryād dalaṃ pūrvamāgneyaṃ raktavarṇakaṃ ||

yāmye kṛṣṇamataḥ pītaṃ śuklaraktaṃ tatodhikaṃ |
tataḥ pītaṃ prakurvīta maṇḍalaṃ caṇḍaroviṣaḥ ||

rajaḥ satya tamorekhā raktaśuklāsitāḥ kramāt |
māyā yugmaṃ tathānyasya phaḍakṣara samaṃvitaṃ ||

vāhyāntarasya cakrasya kuryāt tridvārasaṃyutaṃ |
pūrvaṃ raktaṃ tataḥ pītaṃ śuklaṃ pītaṃ tathākramaṃ ||

caturdvārasamāyuktaṃ kṣetrapālairadhiṣṭhitaṃ |
etena rajaḥsatva tamorekhetyanena trikoṇaṃ bhavati ||

dalādi pramāṇaṃ tu śitaṃ kuryād dalaṃ pūrvamityādau |
āvaraṇa pūjāyāñca jñeyaṃ | tathā ||

pūrvadvāre karālañca vikarālaṃ ca dakṣiṇe |
paścime cāti kālaṃ ca mahākālaṃ tathontare ||

caturthyāṃ ca namontena pūjāmeṣāṃ samācaret |

94b)

tena kalāya nama ityādi krameṇa pūjayet || tathā ||

ādhāraśaktiṃ kūrmañca nāgarājamataḥ paraṃ |
padmanālaṃ ca padmañca pūjayen mantra vinnaraḥ ||

maṇḍalañcaṇḍarasmiśca rajaḥ satvaṃ tamastathā |
ratikāmau ca saṃpūjya śaktipūjāṃ samācaret ||

tena ādhāraśaktaye nama ityādi krameṇa pūjayet || viśeṣya * *? 
praṇavādi namonte eṣa mantraḥ | tato devīndhyāyet || 

puṇḍarīkaṃ tato dhyāyecchuddha vikasitaṃśitaṃ | 
tatpadmakoṣa madhye ca maṇḍalañcaṇḍarociṣaḥ ||

javākusumasaṃkāśaṃ navabandhūkasannibhaṃ |
rajaḥsatva tamorekhā yonimaṇḍalasannibhaṃ ||

madhye caiva sadā devīṃ sūryakoṭi samaprabhāṃ |
tāñca devīṃ karavāme dhārayantīṃ svamastakaṃ ||

prasārita mukhaṃ bhīmaṃ lelihānogra jihvikāṃ |
prapivadraudhinīndhārāṃ nijakaṇḍasamunnatāṃ ||

vikīrṇakeśanisvayāṃ nānābhūṣaṇabhūṣitāṃ |
digambarāṃ mahāghorāṃ pratyālīḍha padāsthitāṃ ||

ratikāmoparisthā tāṃ ke viddhyāyanti mantriṇaḥ | tathā ||

viparītaratasthaṃ ca dhyāyedrati mavābhavaṃ |
varṇinī ḍākinī yuktāṃ vāmadakṣiṇapārśvayoḥ ||

vāmake varṇinīṃ dhyāyeḍḍākinī dakṣiṇe tathā |
varṇinī lohitāṃ saumyāṃ muktakeśīndigamvarāṃ ||

kapālakartikā hastāṃ vāmadakṣiṇayogataḥ |
devī galocchaladrakta dhāraṃ pānaṃ pra * *? tīṃ ||

95a)

asthimālāṃ dharāṃ devīṃ nāgayajñopavītinīṃ |
ḍākinī dakṣapārśve tu kalpānta jvalano pamāṃ ||

vidyucchatābha nayanāṃ dantapaṃkti valākinīṃ |
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanāṃ pīnonnata payodharāṃ ||

mahādevīṃ mahāghorāṃ muktakeśīndigamvarāṃ |
lelihāna mahājihvāṃ muṇḍamālāvibhūṣaṇāṃ ||

kapālakartṛkā hastāṃ vāmadakṣiṇayogataḥ |
devī galocchaladrakta dhārāpānaparāyaṇāṃ ||

karasthena kapālena bhīṣaṇena tu bhīṣaṇāṃ |
ābhyāṃ niṣevitāṃ dhyāyed devīṃ sādhakasattamaḥ ||

tathā ca ||

aparaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi yathākramaṃ |
svanābhau nīrajaṃ śubhraṃ bhānumaṇḍalamaṇḍitaṃ ||

yonivaktra samāyuktaṃ guṇatrayasamanvitaṃ |
tejamadhye mahādevīṃ dhyāyet tāmadhi daivatāṃ ||

pradīptakalikākārā matibīmāṃ vyavasthitāṃ |
karakacchayi yuktāṃ ca raktapadmāsanasthitāṃ ||

yoganidrāsamāyuktāṃ hṛdaya sthita locanāṃ |
itīdaṃ niṣkalaṃ dhyānaṃ tathā cāti kalaṃ sthitaṃ ||

iti dhyātvā mānasairupacāraiḥ saṃpūjyārgha sthāpanaṃ vidhāya devīṃ 
svahṛdayādānīya dakṣiṇanāśāpuṭena yantre saṃsthāpya āvāhayedanena yathā 
||

sarvasiddhi prade pūrvamurccaret sādhakottamaḥ |
varṇinīye tato vācyaṃ sarvasiddhi prade tataḥ ||

ḍākinīye tato vācyaṃ devī nāma tataḥ paraṃ |
ehyetīti tato vācyamihati tadanantaraṃ ||

95b)

tiṣṭha tiṣṭheti vācyañca māyāyugmantataḥ paraṃ |
ṣaḍakṣarasamāyuktaṃ svāheti tadanantaraṃ ||

ityāvāhanamuddiṣṭaṃ sthāpanena samanvitaṃ |

māyā hūṃ devī nāmasambodhanaṃ | tena sarvasiddhi prade varṇinīye sarvasiddhi 
prade ḍākinīye cchinnamaste ehyehi tiṣṭha tiṣtha hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ svāhā || iti 
mantreṇāvāhyā vāhanādi mudrāḥ pradarśya yoni mudrādikaṃ ca darśayitvā 
mūlena pādyārghācamanīyādi yathā sanniveśaṃ nivedyamāṃsabhakta valiṃ 
dadyāt || vali mantrastu uccaret || 

praṇavaṃ pūrva * * * sarvasiddhi pradānvite |
varṇinīye tato vācyaṃ sarvasiddhi prade tataḥ ||

ḍākinīye tato vācyaṃ devīnāma tataḥ paraṃ |
ehyehīti tato vācyamimaṃ vali mataḥ paraṃ ||

gṛhna gṛhna tataḥ proktā mama siddhintataḥ paraṃ |
dehi dehīti māye ca tataḥ phaṭ svāhā hasta yute ||

valimantraḥ samākhyātaḥ pūjitoyaṃ sureśvari |

tena oṃ sarvasiddhi prade varṇinīye sarvasiddhi prade ḍākinīye cchinnamaste ehyehi 
imaṃ valiṃ gṛhna gṛhna mama siddhi dehi hūṁ hūṁ phaṭ svāhā || iti vali 
mantraḥ || 

ityanena valiṃ datvā mūlena triḥ santarpya ṣaḍaṅgaṃ pūjyaṃ ca kṛtvā daleṣu 
pūjayet || 

* * * * *? naṃ mābhidhāṃ devīṃ yoginī ḍākinī tathā | 
bhairavī cama pūrvāṃ * bhairavīntadanantaraṃ ||

96a)

indrākṣīmasitāṃ devīṃ tataḥ saṃhāriṇīṃ kramāt |
pūrvādike dale pūjyāḥ śaktayaḥ kramayogataḥ ||

tena oṃ ekānaṃgābhidhāyainena ityādi krameṇa pūjayet | mūlena yathāvibhavaṃ 
pūjayitvā gāyatrīṃ japtvā mūlaṃ yathāśakti japtvā japaṃ samarpya stutvā natvā 
saṃhāre mudrayā nirmālya uttaratiśiniḥkṣipyāstra mantreṇa tataścaṇḍaraśmau 
niveśayet ||

tatra mantraḥ ||

uttare śikhakhe jāte bhūmyāṃ parvata vāsini |
brahmaṇā samanujñāte gaccha devi yathecchayā ||

gāyatrī yathā ||

oṃ vairocanīye vidmahe cchinnastāyai dhīmahi tanno devi pracodayāt | iti saṃkṣepaḥ 
|| 

tathā ||

atha kiṃ vahunoktena brahmahatyādikaṃ ca yat |
tajjvālayati mantroyaṃ lakṣajāpānna saṃśayaḥ ||

agamyā gamanaṃ caiva tathā bhakṣasya bhakṣaṇaṃ |
sakṛduccatito mantro dahatyagnirindhanaṃ ||

arcitavyā sadā rātrau māṣamāṃsasurādibhiḥ |
eṣa mantraḥ sadā gopyo na prakāśyaḥ kadācana ||

anāsthā vighnamatyanta aśraddhā japa karmaṇi |
ālasyaṃ bhīrutā śaktiḥ mantrahānirna saṃśaya || iti

nāridoṣo na vidveṣo nāśau ca niyamopi na |
siddhamantra tayā nātra pūrvase vā pariśramaḥ ||

jāgracchayā na uttiṣṭhan bhuṃjāno maithunodyataḥ |
sadākālaṃ japen mantramiṣṭasiddhi pradāyakaṃ ||

raṇe rāja kule * te saṃgrāme ripusaṃkaṭe |
yaḥ sadā japate mantraṃ mārastasya na vidyate ||

likhitvā yaḥ kare mantraṃ muṣṭivaddhaṃ vidhīyate |
stambhayetyāśu sarvāṇi yāvanmuṣṭirvimuñcati ||

likhitvā svakare mantraṃ māture saṃspṛśennaraḥ |
vimuñcati dṛḍhādrogī dīrghāyuśca vindati ||

śukleṇa dhyānamātreṇa mṛtamutthāpayennaraṃ |
mahoragaśatairdaṣṭaṃ mṛtamutthapayeddhruvaṃ ||

raktena vaśya māyāti sarvamākṛṣyate jagat |
pītena stambhayet sarva lokavāji gajādikaṃ(?) ||

kṛṣṇena(?) dhyāna mātreṇa ripunuccāṭayennaraḥ |
mārayeda prayatnena kṛṣṇa dhyānaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

tarpaṇaṃ ca prakurvaṃti payasā pāyasena vā |
āpyena madhunā vāpi surayā mantrasādhakaḥ ||

brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyairvaiśyaiḥ śūdrairasmayathākramaṃ |
tarpaṇasya vidhānena haṭhāt siddhiḥ pravartate ||

atha puraścaraṇaṃ || tathā ||

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi vahnisthāpanasaṅgatiṃ |
jāyante siddhayo yena sādhakasya tathepsitāḥ ||

homakarma vidhātavyaṃ kāmanā mantraviccaret |
tato japaṃ prakurvīta jāyante yena siddhayaḥ ||

lakṣaṃ japtvāsya mantrasya labhet siddhi yathepsitaṃ |
sarvaṃ sampadyate devi japahomānnasaṃśayaḥ ||

āpyayuktena valirdeyaḥ sadāmiṣaiśca sādhakaḥ |
tāmbūlena vinā caiva matsyamāṃsaiḥ sadāsavaiḥ ||

māṃsabhakṣavalirdevi mantrasiddhi sayacchati |

97a)

tasmā sarva prayatnena valirdeyaḥ sadāmiṣaiḥ |
āmiṣeṇa vinā tatra devīṃ tuṣṭirnayacchati ||

ṣaḍmāsaṃ dadhisaṃyukto lakṣmīstasya sthiro bhavet |
cchāgamāṃsaṃ saraktañca ghṛtena plāvitantathā ||

juhotya yutaṃ mantrī ca rājā tasya vaśo bhavet |
śvetena karavīreṇa homaṃ samyak karoti yaḥ ||

lakṣamātravidhānena sa jīvat kāladaṣṭakaṃ |
juhuyāt tila puṣpeṇa yo juhoti ghṛtaiḥ saha ||

sa prāpnoti parāṃ siddhimīpsitāṃ surasundari |
vandhūka kusumaṃ hutvā ghṛtenāyutasaṃkhyakaṃ ||

tasya prītā bhaved devī sarvasiddhi phalapradāṃ |
tilataṇḍula home tu yo ghṛtena samācare ||

jaganti tasya vaśyāni jāyante surasundari |
pāyasena careddhomaṃ ghṛtena sahasundari ||

trisandhyaṃ māsamātraṃ ca vāgīśatyamavāpnuyāt |
ityekaḥ kathito devi kramahomavidhirmayā ||

aparaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi kāmanāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
gomāsaṃ madhuloḍya * * vāmahastena homayet ||

api devāvaśaṃ yānti kiṃ punaḥ kṣudramānuṣāḥ |
niṣṭhīvanaṃ dantakāṣṭhaṃ svedehod vartanantathā ||

nāmamnāmadhvakta homena trailokyaṃ vaśamānayet |
rajasvalā yā raktena yutamudgīrṇa bhojanaṃ ||

sahitaṃ mānuṣaiḥ keśaiḥ sadya ākarṣayeddhruvaṃ |
rurumāṃsena sājyena kṛtvā homaṃ varānane ||

sarvasiddhimavāpnoti surāṇāmapi durlabhāṃ |

97b)

mahāmāsena yaḥ kaściddhomayenmadhusaṃyutaṃ |
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ devi trisandhyaṃ sādhakottamaḥ ||

sabhuṃkṣe medinīṃ sarvāṃ ṣaṇmāsena na saṃśayaḥ |
rājyaṃ tasya prayacchanti ḍākinyāḥ prīti mānasāḥ ||

ityādi vistaraḥ | tat tasya(?) tvaka(?) mimāṃ | 
tantrāntare'nusandheyamiti ||

atha samvatsaraṃ yāti phalāhārī yato vrataḥ |
viṣṇubhakti paraścaiva śivaśakti parāyaṇaḥ ||

yathāśakti japen nityaṃ devīṃ dhyātvā yathoditāṃ |
phalepyalāvukuṣmāṇḍau varjanīyau prayatnataḥ ||

atha saṃvatsare tīte saurya(?)nāntaka tulyakaḥ |
sāmarthena bhaved viṣṇurvidyayā ca vṛhaspatiḥ ||

sarvajñaḥ śastunā tulyastejasā ca divākaraḥ |
saślāghyaḥ saguṇī dhanyaḥ sakulīnaḥ sabuddhimān ||

saśūraḥ sadā vikrāntaḥ sasukhī sundaraḥ kṛtī |
varṣādūrddhaṃ ca tad dehaṃ devarūpaṃ ca dṛśyate ||

yad yatra vyabhicāraḥ syāt tadā devī tu taṃ naraṃ |
śrīghraṃ mārayati kruddhā sarvakāma phalapradā ||

aparañca pravakṣyāmi vidyā dravya prakāśakaṃ |
atha kṛṣṇa trayodaśyāmandhakāre niśārddhake ||

gṛhābhyantara go bhūtvā viyukte daśavādyataḥ(?) |
devīrūpaṃ smared bhaktyā dhyāyed vā sādhakottamaḥ ||

ānīya yuvatīmekāṃ pañcapuruṣa gāminīṃ |
sudatīṃ sundarīmetāṃ suśīlāṃ cāruhāsinīṃ ||

vastrālaṃkāra kanakaṃ datvā tasyai yathoditāṃ |
paridhāya pated vastraṃ dūraṃ kuryāt susaṃyutaḥ ||

98a)

pūrvamaṇḍalamālikhya ṣaṭkoṇantatra saṃlikhet |
tasya paritatāṃ nārīṃ nirlajjāṃ bhūṣaṇānvitāṃ ||

vivastrāmukta keśīñca tatra tāmupaveśayet |
pūjayed aṅga devāṁśca maṇḍale kathitā priye ||

tatra tāṃ vividhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ śvetapītakalohitaiḥ |
devī rūpeṇa sañcintya pūjayet sādhakottamaḥ ||

sahasramayutaṃ vāpi prajaped bhakti bhāvataḥ |
valiñca tatra mantreṇa dadyāduṣasimajjanāt ||

tāṃ nārīṃ toṣayen mantrī vastrālaṅkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ |
brāhmaṇān bhojayed dadyād gurave dakṣiṇā valāt ||

anena vidhinā lakṣmīrvidyāṃ sarvāmavāpnuyāt |
vidyāmadṛṣṭa pūrvāñca dṛṣṭyā pāṭhayate naraḥ ||

kṣudrā nāmapi lakṣmīḥ syāt kavitye cāsya kākathā |
ekārātreṇa labhate vidyāṃ lakṣmīṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

dvirātreṇa yaśorāśiṃ trirātreṇa guruḥ svayaṃ |
* naśaḥ prātarutthāya śayyā tala samāśritaḥ ||

dhyātvā nyastvāṃ chinnamastāṃ japedaṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ |
triśatamvā japennityamaṣṭottara sahasrakaṃ ||

ṣaṇmāsena mahādevi vāṇī gadyamayī bhavet |
vatsarārddhena śāstrāṇāṃ niścitaṃ pārago bhavet ||

trivatsarād bhavedūrddhaṃ vāgīśvara ivāparaḥ |
tasya darśanamātreṇa vādito niḥprabhā matāḥ ||

parāyante gatā bhūmau yathā siṃhabhayādgajāḥ |
daśāṃśenāṅgamantrāṇāṃ mūlamantrānusārataḥ ||

śakṛdvā prajapen nityaṃ kecit pa * simaṃ śive |

98b)

ityādi vistaraḥ || oṃ ||

takāraṃ ca tato dadyāccaturthaśca * * * *? |
