99a)

iti vaśiṣṭhaśāpa śravaṇāt | sakāra rahite śāpaḥ paryavasyati || ata evoktaṃ || 

kṣipra prasādā jagatāṃ sadānīla sarasvatī |
viśeṣataḥ kali yuge matprasādād bhaviṣyati ||

iha yadyapi sakārastantreṣu na śrūyate | vadhūbījanoddhārāt || sakāro labhyate | 
strībījasyaiva vadhūtva pratipādanāditi ||

ata evoktaṃ muṇḍamālāyāṃ || 

tataḥ pratiṣṭhitā vidyā tārācandra svarūpiṇī |
candraḥ sakārastat svarūpiṇī tad yuktā pratiṣṭhitā ||

śāpare hitetyarthaḥ | sa eva tryakṣaraḥ | phaḍantaścet | ekajaṭā mantraḥ | sa eva 
phaḍantastārādistārā mantraḥ || taduktaṃ ||

tryakṣarosau mahāmantraḥ phaṭkārānto hṛdi sthitaḥ |
pañcaraśmi samāyuktopyajñānendhana dāhakaḥ ||

pañcaraśmiroṅkāraḥ | taduktaṃ ||

astrānteyaṃ mahāvidyā jaṭā puṃgavadhāriṇī |
vedādi mukhayuktā cet tārāṃ tribhavatāriṇī ||

vedādiḥ oṃkāraḥ | tathā tantrāntare || 

lajjāyugmaṃ vadhūbījaṃ tato dīrgha tanucchadaṃ | 
sārasvato paro mantraḥ saṃproktaścaturakṣaraḥ || 

lajjāyugaṃ hrīṁ hrīṁ vadhūbījaṃ strīṁ | dīrgha taruchadaṃ hūṃ || tena hrīṁ hrīṁ 
strīṁ huṁ | 

tadante yadi phaṭkāro manuḥ pañcākṣaro bhavet || 

tadante caturakṣarānte || tathā 

tāraṃ śaktirvadhubījā * nte dīrgha tanucchadaṃ | 
astramagni vadhūrante manuḥ saptākṣaro bhavet ||

99b)

tāraṃ oṃ | śaktiḥ hrīṁ | vadhūḥ strīṁ | dīrgha tanucchadaṃ hūṃ | astraṃ phaṭ | 
admi(?) vadhūḥ svāhā || tena oṃ hrīṁ strīṁ hūṁ phaṭ svāhā ||

tathā 

mantraḥ śāstreṣu saṃproktastathā dīrgheṇa varmaṇā | 
puṭitaṃ ca vadhūbījamaparāsau guṇākṣaraḥ ||

dīrghacarmā huṃkāraḥ madhūbījaṃ strīṁ | tena hūṃ strīṁ hūṃ ||

tathā ||

svatantraścedvadhū bījastvekākṣaramihocyate |

vadhūbījaṃ strīṁ | tena strīṁ || tathā | ekā * * * * * * 
* * * * *? khyamākṣibindu yuktau tritanu phaṭ | 

tāraṃ padme yugaṃ paścāt mahāpadme samīrayet |

padmāvati dviṭhāntaika jaṭā saptārka varṇayuk | 

tritantaṃ hūṃ tāraṃ oṃ | tena vrīṁ vrīṁ hūṁ phaṭ oṃ || padme padme 
mahāpadme padmāvati svāhā dviṭha svāhā ||

tathā phetkāriṇītantre ||

kailāśaśikharāsīnaṃ candraṣaṇḍavirājitaṃ |
vakṣasthale samāsīnā pṛcchatisma nagātmajā ||

kathamīśāna sarvajña labhante te tapodhanāḥ |
kāṃ(?) vidyāṃ prāpya * * *? bhāratātīt pracakrire 
||

śrutvā nagātmajā vākyaṃ vihasya parameśvaraḥ |
prāha priyāṃ parityajya sādhvi sādhviti cākratanu(?) ||

śṛṇu śākti(?) mahāvidyā maṅkānandhana(?) 
dāhinīṃ |
yāmavāpya mahātmāno dharmakāmārtha muktiṣu ||

nāsādhyaṃ jāyate kiñcit kavayaḥ śuddhavuddha * * * *? 
sa *? si yāvā ca stambhinī prativādinaḥ ||

vidyāhānikarī caiva viduṣāṃ validānataḥ |

100a)

hāsyāya yāsadasyānāmaśuddhamapi jalpayet |
vādinaṃ vātharājānaṃ mantriṇā vaśamānayet ||

mantrasya jñānamātreṇa japāt sarvajñatāṃ nayet |
jihvāyāṃ nyasanādeva mukopi sukavirbhavet ||

avyāhatā matiḥ śāstre vijayo vākpaterapi | madhunā tridinādarvāk | garbhaka jihvā 
suvinyasedamuka mantrī || yadi paṇḍitosau nācenno mūrkhatāṃ yāti | 

mahācīna kramānnatvā tārāntri bhavatāriṇīṃ |
satsādhanamahaṃ vakṣye śṛṇusārvajña kāraṇaṃ ||

tat kramaḥ ||

pūjanaṃ mānasaṃ divyaṃ mānasaṃ kalpanādikaṃ |
sarva eva śubhaḥ kālo nāśubho vidyate kvacit ||

na viśeṣo divā rātrau na sandhyāyāṃ mahāniśi |
vastrāsanasthāna dehaṃ gehe sparśādhikāriṇaḥ ||

na śuddhiṃ carvayedatra nirvikalpaṃ manaścaret |
sarvadā pūjayed devīmasnātaḥ kṛta bhojanaḥ ||

mahāniśya sucaudeśe valimantreṇa dāpayet |
strī dveṣopi na kartavyaṃ viśeṣāt pūjanaṃ striyaḥ ||

japa sthāne mahāśaṃkhaṃ niveśyorddha carejjapaṃ |
striyaṃ gacchan spṛśan paśyan yatra kutrāpya cūḍake ||

bhakṣān tāmbūlamanyāṃśca bhakṣadravyān yathārucīn |
māṃsamatsya dadhi kṣaudra payaḥ śākāṭyamaikṣavaṃ ||

bhaktādyaśeṣa dravyāṇi hutvā bhakṣan carejjapaṃ | 
diśādi niyamo tātrasthidi niyamopi na ||

japena kāla niyamo nārghādiṣu valiṣvapi svecchā niyama uktotra mahāmantrasya 
sādhane ||

101a)

kalpayet sthiracitte na paṇḍitohaṃ mahākaviḥ |

hariharacaraṇāravindabhakto bhavati cirāya sarasvatī nivāsaḥ ||

manurvimṛṣya dātavyaḥ śreṣṭhasyāpi vilīnatāṃ |
putrasya kimutānyasya vigotrāya svapauruṣaṃ ||

tathā ||

sarvadā gopayedenaṃ manuṃ ca gurumeva ca |
tena vīryavatī vidyā nirvīryāsyāt prakāśataḥ ||

utpādaka brahmadāttrī garīyāna mantradaḥ pitā |
tasmān manyeta satataṃ piturapyadhikaṃ guruṃ ||

upadeśādaṅga mantrovali mantrastathaiva ca |
jñātavyo viduṣāṃ siddhirasya mantrasya lobhataḥ ||

upāṃśuḥ syācchataguṇaḥ sahasro mānaso japaḥ |
acirādā gatā siddhirmahāśaṃkhasya mālayā ||

kubjikatantre ||

yasmin mantreya ācārastaddharmastu hitā dṛśaḥ |
kṛtārthastena jāyeta svargaṃ vāmokṣameva vā ||

bhrāntiratra na kartavyo siddhihāniḥ prajāyate |
viśuddha cittotra bhajet siddhiḥ syādapavargajā ||

upacāra viśeṣeṇa rājapatnī vaśaṃ nayet |
rājānaṃ japamātreṇa valinā sakalaṃ jagat ||

tathā nīlasarasvatyāḥ prapañcoyaṃ pradarśitaḥ |
brahmaṇā pūrvamasmabhyaṃ mayātubhyaṃ nagātmaje ||

105b)

tasyopari tato dhyāyaṭāḥ tārādakṣa bhājanaṃ |
tasyopari tato dhyāye ddhūṁkāraṃ nīlasannibhaṃ ||

tasyopari tato dhyāyet kartṛkāṃ bījabhūṣitāṃ |
tasyopari mahādevī khaṭvānīla ghanaprabhāṃ ||

lambodarīṃ vyāghracarma samāvṛtanitamvinīṃ |
pinonnata payobhāraṃ raktavartula locanāṃ ||

lalarjihvāṃ mahābhīmāṃ daṃṣṭrākoṭi samujvalāṃ |
piṅgrograika(?) jaṭāṃ nīla nāgahāra samujvalāṃ ||

nīlotpalalasatmālā vaddhajūṭāṃ bhayaṅkarīṃ |
śvetāsthi pa * *? yukta kapālaṃ ca suśobhitāṃ ||

lalāṭa raktanāgena kṛtakarṇāvataṃsikāṃ |
ati śubhra mahānāga kṛtahāra mahojvalāṃ ||

dūrvādalaśyānāgakṛta yajñopavītinīṃ |
caturbhujāṃ raktamāṃsa khaḍgamaṇḍitamuṣṭina ||

jaṭājūṭena sūtreṇa śobhitātīkṣṇadhāraya |
khaḍgasya dakṣiṇasya ka *? śobhitaṃ ghoranādinīṃ ||

tadayastādvījavṛndāṃ kartṛkā laṃkṛtāṃ parāṃ |
vāmorddha raktamārgena digambara mato haraṃ ||

dadhatīnīlapadmañca tadaghna * *? pālakaṃ |
jagatāṃ jāḍyasaṃyuktaṃ dadhatī kundasannibhaṃ ||

dhūmrābha nāgasaṃdoha kṛtakeyūra satvarāṃ |
suvarṇavarṇanāgena kaṃkanojvala pānikāṃ ||

śubhravarṇa mahādeva śavadvadyāsanasthitāṃ |
niyastranabhiyā tadvat saṃkuśca prapadā * lāṃ ||

śavapādadvayārūḍha vāmapādāṃ mahonmukhīṃ |

106a)

kundāsanāgasaṃśobhi kaṭisutrāṃ trilocanāṃ |
īṣadraktena nāgena kṛtanūpura pallagāṃ ||

sadyaścchinnagaladrakta nṛmuṇḍairakta bhūṣaṇaiḥ |
anyonya keśagraṃthitaiḥ pādapadmāvalambitaiḥ ||

pañcāśadbhirmahāmāyāṃ śobhitāṃ bhuvaneśvarīṃ |
jvalaccitāṃ madhyasaṃsthāṃ dvīpi carmājvalāṃśukāṃ ||

akṣobhanāgasamvaddha jaṭājūṭāṃ varapradāṃ |
evaṃ bhūtāṃ mahādevīmātmānaṃ yāgavastu ca ||

vijñāpayen mahādeva paṇḍitohaṃ mahākavirityādi dhyāyet || tathā ||

amuktaḥ prajapenmantraṃ na doṣaḥ kṛta bhojane |
validāne ca pūjāyāṃ nātrakāryā vicāraṇā ||

niyamāniyamo nātra nālasyaṃ bhīrutā trayā |
svecchācārotra vihito mahācīna kramāditi ||

atha kāmyāni ||

puṣkariṇyā jalāgādhe yaṣṭikāṣṭhaṃ tamograhe |
dantenānīya tūrṇaṃ tu kīlakaṃ tena kārayet ||

te naiva madhūdhuvyutāktena padminī patra mātrake |
likhed vajrāraṇī madhye mūlamantraṃ tu siddhidaṃ ||

113a)

atha dhyānaṃ ||

śānti dvaya puṭāntasthaṃ kamaladvaya saṃsthitaṃ |
jyotistatva samaṃ dhyāyet kulākula niyojanāt ||

tathā 

śṛṃgāṭa dvaya madhyasthaṃ śakti dvaya puṭīkṛtaṃ |
sadā samarasaṃ dhyāyeddhyānantat kulayogināṃ || 

tathā ||

kiraṇasthānathāgnisthaṃ candrabhāskara madhyagaṃ |
mahāśūnya layaṃ kṛtvā pūrṇastiṣṭhati yogirāṭ || 

tathā ||

nirālambe pade śūnye yat teja upayojate |
tadgarbhaṃ sanyasen nityaṃ dhyānametad viyogināṃ ||

iti dhyānaṃ || atha pūjā || 

arghaṃ yadviṣayaiḥ puṣpaistatkṣaṇāt tanmayo bhavet |
nāsastanmayatā buddhiḥ sohaṃ bhāvena pūjayet ||

viṣaya puṣpāṇi yathā ||

anāyā cānahaṃkāramarāgamamadaṃ tathā |
amohakamadaṃtañca addheṣā bhakṣakau punaḥ ||

amātsaryamalobhañca dayātmānamvidurbudhāḥ |
ahiṃsā paramaṃ puṣpamindriyā nigrahaṃ tathā ||

dayā puṣpaṃ kṣamāpuṣpaṃ jñānapuṣpaṃñca pañcamaṃ |
ityaṣṭa saptabhiḥ puṣpaiḥ pūjayet paradevatāṃ ||

iti pūjanaṃ ||

mālāpañcāśikā proktā śūtraṃ śakti śivātmakaṃ |
granthitāṃ kuṇḍalī śakti kalānte merusaṃsthitiḥ ||

tena akārādi lakārāntaṃ lakārādi akārāntaṃ kṣaṃ merukaṃ kṛtvā japtvā 
paratejasi samarpayet ||

atha homaḥ || ātmānamaparicchinnaṃ vibhāvya ātmāntarānta 
paramātmajñānātma svarūpaṃ

114a)

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi yena vai dakṣiṇā vidhiṃ |

sadā |

ityevamādikaṃ coktamanyat kiṃ kathyatāmpunaḥ ||

na tasya durlabhaṃ kiñcid yaḥ smared ghoradakṣiṇāṃ ||

atha prātaḥkṛtyādi mātṛkāntaṃ karmasamāpya ṛṣyādi nyāsaṃ kuryāt ||

taduktaṃ kālikopaniṣatsu ||

115a)

ghorarūpāṃ mahāraudrīṃ śmaśānālayavāsinīṃ |

tato yantraṃ purato nidhāyā vāhayet || yantraṃ yathā ||

pādyādikaṃ datvā pañcadaśakoṇe vāmāvartena pūjayet ||

116a)

atha yantroddhāre'ṣṭa dalādau kiṃ pramāṇaṃ tatra māstīti kecit || tatra granthāntara 
darśanāt ||

117a)

* * * * * *? rvīta dhanadhānya sutānvitaḥ |
sarvavidyāvatāṃ śreṣṭho bhaven naivātra saṃśayaḥ ||

ṛtumatyā bhagaṃ paśyan prajapedayutaṃ naraḥ |
anārthakāpi tadvāṇī gadya padya mayī bhavet ||

chandovaddhāparaṃ tasya vāṇī vaktrāt prajāyate |
surateṣu ca japtavyaṃ mahāpātaka muktaye ||

dhanāgamāya ca tathā parayoṣit samāgame |
yadi yoṣinna saṃsarge tadā retaḥ prayatnataḥ ||

samutsārya tato japyaṃ sarvakāmārthasiddhaye |
śmaśānālayamāgatya muktakeśo digamvaraḥ ||

japedayutasaṃkhyantu sarvakāmārthasiddhaye |
tatraiva pretamāruhya yojapen mantravittamaḥ ||

ayutaṃ maithunī bhūtvā vibhoḥ satva parāyaṇaḥ |
sayāti paramāṃ siddhiṃ devirapi sudurlabhāṃ ||

ākarṣaṇavaśīkāro vandhanoccāṭanādayaḥ |
stambhanaṃ mohanaṃ caiva trāsanaṃ hrāsanaṃ tathā ||

vāgmitvañca dhanitvañca vahuputratva meva ca |
vahuvallabhatāṃ prāpya sarvaṃ priyatvameva ca ||

sa yāti khecaratvaṃ ca devatvamapi caiva hi |
na jarā na ca vai rogo na mṛtyurna ca nālayaṃ ||

na vā prītirmanuṣyatvena na ca vākkāya pātanaṃ |
athavā sa bhaven nityaṃ caturviṃśati siddhi yuk ||

svadeharudhirāktena vilvapatreṇa sahasraśaḥ |
śmaśāne'bhyarcya devīṃ tu vāgīśa samatāṃ vrajet ||

retā yuktena patreṇa vābhyarcyeha sahasraśaḥ |
śmaśānabhyarcya devīṃ tu sarvasiddhiṃ sa vindati ||

dhanavān valavān vāgmī sarvayoṣit priyo bhavet |

śaktiśūladhanurvāṇa khaḍgakheṭavarābhayān ||

dakṣādakṣa bhujairdevīṃ vidrāṇāṃ paribhūṣitāṃ |
dhyātvaivaṃ sādhakaḥ sādhyaṃ sādhayet manasepsitaṃ ||

pūrvādīśāna paryantaṃ dutyaḥ kumbhāṅgasaṃsthitāṃ |
tena pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ brāhmyādi śaktīḥ pūjayedityarthaḥ ||

tathā karṇikāṃ tataḥ kālī * * * mahāpahāṃ |
nāmāccareṇa saṃruddhe vahnau prajvalite pure ||

juhuyād vairiṇāṃ śuddho devī mantraṃ japan tathā |
samidhaḥ picumardasya vibhīvrateti kāṣṭikā ||

harīvirājikā kañcukaśca jāma pralambikāṃ |
gṛhadhūma śmaśānāsthi vibhītāṅgāra homataḥ ||

saptādvairiṇaṃ hanti kālikāmantra yogataḥ |
uccāṭamapagāhne ca sandhyāyāṃ māraṇantathā ||

dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi sthitāgrāmāderdakṣiṇāmukha ityādi ityādi ||

vicumardo nimbo vibhīvratatiramṛtā gṛha dhūmaḥ sona iti khyātaḥ | atrāpi 
puraścaraṇaṃ pūrvavat || aṣṭau sahasrāṇi vā viśeṣānabhidhānāditi saṃkṣepaḥ ||

119b)

śrīramāramaṇadevaśarmaṇā nirmite nigamasāranirṇaye | 
kālikāmanuvidhānapūjanadhyāna bhuvibhṛtakaḥ paṭaloyaṃ ||
